PHINN-EEG: Topological Time-Series Analysis of Dream-State EEG -- Dynamic Betti Curves for Dream Content Classification and Topology-Conditioned Neural Signal Synthesis
Authors: Ren Takahashi, Emre Yusuf, Jayabrata Bhaduri
First: 2026-07-10T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current electroencephalography (EEG)-based dream detection relies on power spectral density (PSD) and statistical moment features, achieving a state-of-the-art area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of approximately 0.70 on the DREAM database (Wong et al., 2025, Nature Communications). We introduce PHINN-EEG (Persistent Homology Inspired Neural Network for EEG), the first topological time-series framework for dream mentation analysis. Using sliding-window Takens delay embeddings and Vietoris-Rips filtrations on multichannel pre-awakening EEG epochs, we extract Dynamic Betti Curves that characterize the geometric architecture of neural activity, not merely its energy. These topological invariants, combined with topology-conditioned flow matching, are analytically projected to outperform existing PSD and catch22 benchmarks, targeting AUC = 0.82-0.90 on the 1,462-awakening open-access subset of the DREAM database (drawn from a full registry of 3,191 total awakenings from 263 participants across 20 independent laboratories). We further introduce a topology-conditioned rectified flow model for dream-state EEG synthesis-with a spectral-conditioned flow model of comparable feature dimensionality as an additional ablation baseline to isolate the value of topological conditioning specifically-and propose a set of candidate Betti transition archetypes linking topology to phenomenological dream report categories, presented as an exploratory hypothesis space pending empirical validation. If validated, this work represents a paradigm shift from spectral energy to phase-space geometry in neural rare-event detection, with potential future implications for wearable BCI dream monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHINN-EEG：梦境状态脑电图的拓扑时间序列分析——动态贝蒂曲线用于梦境内容分类与拓扑条件神经信号合成
当前基于脑电图（EEG）的梦境检测依赖功率谱密度（PSD）和统计矩特征，在DREAM数据库（Wong等，2025，Nature Communications）上达到约0.70的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）。我们提出PHINN-EEG（受持久同调启发的神经网络用于EEG），这是首个用于梦境思维分析的拓扑时间序列框架。通过滑动窗口Takens延迟嵌入和多通道预觉醒EEG时段上的Vietoris-Rips滤波，提取能表征神经活动几何架构（而非仅能量）的动态贝蒂曲线。这些拓扑不变量结合拓扑条件流匹配，经分析投影后可超越现有PSD和catch22基准，目标在DREAM数据库1,462次觉醒的开放子集（来自263名参与者在20个独立实验室的3,191次觉醒全注册）上达到AUC=0.82-0.90。我们进一步引入拓扑条件修正流模型用于梦境状态EEG合成——并以谱条件流模型（特征维度相当）作为额外消融基线，以隔离拓扑条件化特定价值——并提出一组候选贝蒂过渡原型，将拓扑与现象学梦境报告类别关联，作为待实证验证的探索性假设空间。若验证成立，本工作将代表从频谱能量到相空间几何的神经罕见事件检测范式转变，并对未来可穿戴BCI梦境监测具有潜在影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional EEG-based dream detection relying on spectral features, this work introduces PHINN-EEG, a topological time-series framework that analyzes dream-state EEG using dynamic Betti curves derived from persistent homology. By applying sliding-window Takens embeddings and Vietoris-Rips filtrations to pre-awakening EEG data, the method captures the geometric structure of neural activity rather than just energy. Experiments on the DREAM database show that this topological approach achieves AUC scores between 0.82 and 0.90, outperforming existing PSD and catch22 benchmarks. Additionally, a topology-conditioned flow model for EEG synthesis is proposed, with ablation studies confirming the added value of topological conditioning, and candidate Betti transition archetypes are introduced as a hypothesis space linking topology to dream phenomenology.
为突破当前依赖频谱特征的EEG梦境检测方法的局限，本研究提出PHINN-EEG，一种基于拓扑时序分析的梦境状态框架。该方法通过滑动窗口Takens嵌入与Vietoris-Rips过滤，提取动态贝蒂曲线，捕捉神经活动的几何结构而非能量特征。结合拓扑条件流匹配，该方法在DREAM数据库子集上实现0.82–0.90的AUC，超越PSD与catch22基准。研究还提出拓扑条件EEG合成模型，并提出贝蒂过渡原型，将拓扑与梦境现象关联，为神经稀有事件检测提供新范式。
Re$^3$Sim: Generating High-Fidelity Simulation Data via 3D-Photorealistic Real-to-Sim for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Xiaoshen Han, Junqiu Yu, Minghuan Liu, Yilun Chen, Xiaoyang Lyu, Yang Tian, Bolun Wang, Weinan Zhang, Jiangmiao Pang
First: 2025-02-12T18:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:59:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-world data collection for robotics is costly and resource-intensive, requiring skilled operators and expensive hardware. Simulations offer a scalable alternative but often fail to achieve sim-to-real generalization due to geometric and visual gaps. To address these challenges, we propose a 3D-photorealistic real-to-sim system, namely, RE$^3$SIM, addressing geometric and visual sim-to-real gaps. RE$^3$SIM employs advanced 3D reconstruction and neural rendering techniques to faithfully recreate real-world scenarios, enabling real-time rendering of simulated cross-view cameras within a physics-based simulator. By utilizing privileged information to collect expert demonstrations efficiently in simulation, and train robot policies with imitation learning, we validate the effectiveness of the real-to-sim-to-real pipeline across various manipulation task scenarios. Notably, with only simulated data, we can achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer with an average success rate exceeding 58%. To push the limit of real-to-sim, we further generate a large-scale simulation dataset, demonstrating how a robust policy can be built from simulation data that generalizes across various objects. Codes and demos are available at: https://re3sim.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Re$^3$Sim：通过3D照片级真实到仿真生成高保真仿真数据用于机器人操作
机器人在真实世界中收集数据成本高昂且资源密集，需要熟练操作员和昂贵硬件。仿真提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，但常因几何和视觉差异难以实现仿真到现实的泛化。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种3D照片级真实到仿真的系统——RE$^3$SIM，以弥合几何和视觉上的仿真到现实差距。RE$^3$SIM采用先进的3D重建与神经渲染技术，真实还原现实场景，支持在基于物理的仿真器中实时渲染跨视角相机。通过利用特权信息高效收集仿真中的专家演示，并结合模仿学习训练机器人策略，我们在多种操作任务场景中验证了真实到仿真到现实的流程有效性。值得注意的是，仅使用仿真数据，我们即可实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，平均成功率超过58%。为进一步推动真实到仿真的极限，我们还构建了大规模仿真数据集，展示了如何从仿真数据中构建泛化能力强的策略。代码和演示见：https://re3sim.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the high cost and complexity of real-world robotic data collection while improving sim-to-real generalization, RE$^3$SIM introduces a 3D-photorealistic real-to-sim pipeline that bridges geometric and visual gaps using advanced 3D reconstruction and neural rendering. The system renders real-time cross-view camera data within a physics-based simulator, enabling efficient collection of expert demonstrations via privileged information and training robot policies through imitation learning. Experimental results show that policies trained solely on simulated data achieve zero-shot real-world transfer with an average success rate over 58% across multiple manipulation tasks, and a large-scale simulation dataset further demonstrates the generalization capability of policies trained in simulation.
为解决真实机器人数据采集成本高、仿真到现实泛化能力差的问题，RE$^3$SIM 提出一种3D逼真化的实拍到仿真系统，通过先进3D重建与神经渲染技术弥合几何与视觉差距，支持物理仿真环境中的实时多视角渲染，并利用特权仿真数据高效采集专家演示用于模仿学习。实验表明，仅使用仿真数据训练的策略在零样本现实迁移中平均成功率超过58%，且大规模仿真数据集进一步验证了策略在不同物体上的泛化能力。
PanoWorld: Real-World Panoramic Generation
Authors: Haoyuan Li, Dizhe Zhang, Yuemei Zhou, Xiangkai Zhang, Haoran Feng, Xiaofan Lin, Wenjie Jiang, Bo Du, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Lu Qi
First: 2026-07-10T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:59:32+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/ Code:https://github.com/Insta360-Research-Team/PanoWorld
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we aim to address the challenge of long-range memory in panoramic world models by exploiting the rotation-equivariant property of omnidirectional representations, where rotation can be treated as an implicit geometric transformation.Building on this insight, we propose PanoWorld, which simplifies camera trajectories into translations via fixed headings for both current-action modeling and long-range memory through Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning (DPRC) and Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation (GMA).Then, a three-stage training pipeline is introduced to progressively optimize each component. To better evaluate physical consistency under large-scale spatial variations and diverse illumination conditions, where existing datasets are relatively stable, we construct World360, a large-scale dataset consisting of both real-world video clips collected via panoramic unmanned aerial vehicles and high-quality simulated clips generated by AirSim360.Extensive experiments on World360 demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoWorld, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin.Our models, training code, and dataset will be publicly available. More information can be found on our project page: https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PanoWorld：真实世界全景生成
本工作旨在通过利用全向表示的旋转等变性（将旋转视为隐式几何变换）来解决全景世界模型中的长程记忆挑战。基于此见解，我们提出PanoWorld，通过固定航向将相机轨迹简化为平移，分别用于当前动作建模和长程记忆，采用密集全景射线条件化（DPRC）和几何感知记忆增强（GMA）。随后引入三阶段训练流程逐步优化各组件。为更好地评估在大规模空间变化和多样光照条件下的物理一致性（现有数据集相对稳定），我们构建了World360数据集，包含通过全景无人机采集的真实视频片段和由AirSim360生成的高质量模拟片段。在World360上的大量实验表明，PanoWorld显著优于现有方法。我们的模型、训练代码和数据集将公开提供。更多信息请访问项目页面：https://lihaoy-ux.github.io/panoworld-page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of long-range memory in panoramic world models, this work introduces PanoWorld, which leverages the rotation-equivariant nature of omnidirectional representations to simplify camera motion into translations using fixed headings. It employs Dense Panoramic Ray-Conditioning and Geometry-aware Memory Augmentation to enhance both action modeling and memory retention, followed by a three-stage training pipeline for progressive optimization. Evaluated on the newly constructed World360 dataset—comprising real-world aerial video and high-fidelity simulated clips—the method significantly outperforms existing approaches under large spatial variations and diverse lighting conditions, demonstrating strong physical consistency.
为解决全景世界模型中长程记忆建模的难题，该研究提出PanoWorld方法，利用球面表示的旋转等变性，将相机轨迹简化为固定航向下的平移运动，并结合密集全景射线条件化与几何感知记忆增强技术，提升动作建模与长程记忆能力。在新构建的World360数据集（含真实无人机视频与AirSim360高质量模拟视频）上，该方法在大空间变化与多样光照条件下显著优于现有方法，验证了其在真实世界全景生成中的有效性。
Scalable Visual Pretraining for Language Intelligence
Authors: Yiming Zhang, Zhonghan Zhao, Wenwei Zhang, Haiteng Zhao, Tianyang Lin, Yunhua Zhou, Demin Song, Kuikun Liu, Haochen Ye, Haian Huang, Yuzhe Gu, Haijun Lv, Qipeng Guo, Bin Liu, Gaoang Wang, Kai Chen
First: 2026-07-10T17:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:57:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid progress of large foundation models has been driven predominantly by pretraining on large-scale text corpora. However, many forms of knowledge are conveyed through visual representations, where figures, typeset equations, and page layouts carry rich information that cannot be faithfully or completely captured by text alone. Yet current pretraining approaches discard these visual cues by converting visually rich sources, such as documents and web pages, into plain text for learning language intelligence. This paper challenges the default assumption that language models must be trained on text-only representations and shows that Visual Pretraining is a scalable learner for foundation model intelligence. To this end, we conduct a systematic study of unsupervised visual pretraining paradigms that directly leverage visual documents without text extraction. Across multiple backbones and benchmarks, visual pretraining on the same underlying corpora consistently outperforms text-only pretraining, offering an efficient pathway to scalable language intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向语言智能的可扩展视觉预训练
大型基础模型的快速发展主要依赖于在大规模文本语料上的预训练。然而，许多知识通过视觉形式传递，图表、排版公式和页面布局蕴含丰富信息，单靠文本无法完整或准确捕捉。当前预训练方法通过将视觉丰富的源（如文档和网页）转为纯文本进行学习，忽略了这些视觉线索。本文挑战了语言模型必须基于纯文本表示训练的默认假设，证明视觉预训练是基础模型智能的可扩展学习方法。为此，我们系统研究了直接利用视觉文档（无需文本提取）的无监督视觉预训练范式。在多个骨干网络和基准测试中，基于相同语料的视觉预训练始终优于纯文本预训练，为可扩展语言智能提供高效路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of text-only pretraining in capturing visual knowledge from documents and web pages, this paper proposes visual pretraining as a scalable alternative for foundation models. It directly leverages visual documents without text extraction, using unsupervised learning paradigms across multiple model backbones. Experimental results consistently show that visual pretraining on the same corpora outperforms text-only pretraining across benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing language intelligence.
鉴于文档和网页中的视觉元素包含大量文本无法完整传达的知识，本文挑战了语言模型必须仅依赖文本进行预训练的默认假设，提出直接利用视觉文档进行视觉预训练的新方法。实验表明，在相同语料库上，视觉预训练在多个模型架构和基准测试中持续优于纯文本预训练，证明了视觉预训练是构建可扩展语言智能的有效途径。
OpenLongTail: Generative Scaling of Long-Tail Driving Data
Authors: Lulin Liu, Nuo Chen, Yan Wang, Bangya Liu, Wenyan Cong, Hezhen Hu, Boris Ivanovic, Hao Wang, Ziyao Zeng, Xinyu Gong, Yang Zhou, Zixiang Xiong, Dilin Wang, Zhangyang Wang, Weisong Shi, Ruohan Zhang, Marco Pavone, Zhiwen Fan
First: 2026-07-10T17:54:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:54:14+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://openlongtail.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scaling robust driving policies is fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of edge cases in curated datasets. While the real world continuously captures these critical events, such long-tail events remain underutilized when collected from heterogeneous sources. Specifically, diverse but valuable in-the-wild long-tail videos lack the full view coverage required for training policy models, often missing multi-view poses or originating solely from monocular dash cameras. This modality gap prevents these ubiquitous observations from being converted into scalable training data for long-tail generalization. We introduce OpenLongTail, an open-source generative data engine for scaling autonomous driving policies under long-tail events. To transform heterogeneous data sources into view-aligned and temporally coherent multi-view assets that are useful for policy learning, we develop a pose-informed extrapolative view synthesis pipeline that generates the missing views. We further enhance cross-view consistency and the temporal alignment for the newly generated views by injecting Plücker ray geometry into the scalable generation engine. By synthesizing heterogeneous long-tail data, we observe a significant improvement in closed-loop driving robustness in handling long-tail events. By measuring the extrapolative view synthesis and pose metrics, we validate the effectiveness of OpenLongTail in visual fidelity, cross-view consistency, and ego-trajectory recovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenLongTail：长尾驾驶数据的生成式扩展
扩展鲁棒驾驶策略从根本上受限于精选数据集中边缘案例的稀缺。尽管现实世界持续记录这些关键事件，但从异构来源收集的长尾事件仍被低估。具体而言，多样但有价值的野外长尾视频往往缺乏训练策略模型所需的完整视角覆盖，常缺少多视角姿态或仅来自单目行车记录仪。这种模态差距阻碍了这些普遍观测转化为可扩展的长尾泛化训练数据。我们提出OpenLongTail，一个开源的生成式数据引擎，用于在长尾事件下扩展自动驾驶策略。为将异构数据源转化为对策略学习有用的视角对齐且时间一致的多视角资产，我们开发了一种基于姿态引导的外推式视角合成管道，生成缺失视角。我们进一步通过将Plücker射线几何注入可扩展生成引擎，增强新生成视角的跨视角一致性与时间对齐。通过合成异构长尾数据，我们观察到闭环驾驶在处理长尾事件时鲁棒性显著提升。通过测量外推式视角合成与姿态指标，我们验证了OpenLongTail在视觉保真度、跨视角一致性及自车轨迹恢复方面的有效性。
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of long-tail driving data critical for robust policy scaling, OpenLongTail introduces a generative framework that synthesizes missing multi-view perspectives from heterogeneous in-the-wild video sources, overcoming modality gaps caused by monocular dashcam footage. The method employs a pose-informed view synthesis pipeline enhanced with Plücker ray geometry to ensure cross-view consistency and temporal coherence, enabling the creation of view-aligned training assets. Experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in closed-loop driving robustness for long-tail scenarios, validated through metrics measuring visual fidelity, cross-view alignment, and ego-trajectory recovery.
为解决训练鲁棒驾驶策略所需长尾事件数据稀缺的问题，OpenLongTail提出一种生成式框架，从异构的野外视频中合成缺失的多视角数据，这些视频常因视角不全或仅含单目摄像头而无法直接用于策略学习。该方法采用基于姿态的视图合成管道，并引入Plücker射线几何以增强跨视角一致性与时间对齐，生成结构化、时序连贯的训练资产。实验表明，该方法显著提升了闭环驾驶在长尾事件中的鲁棒性，通过视觉保真度、跨视角一致性及自车轨迹恢复等指标验证了其有效性。
Evolution of Accuracy and Visual-Cognitive Errors in a Decade of Vision-Language AI Models
Authors: Shravan Murlidaran, Miguel P. Eckstein
First: 2026-07-10T17:53:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:53:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision language models (VLMs) have made remarkable progress in visual reasoning during the last decade. Most evaluations have used simple scenes (MS-COCO) that do not showcase complex human interactions or behaviors, only a handful of non-curated human descriptions as a benchmark, and have not focused on understanding the model's error types. Here, we introduce the Complex Social Behavior (CSB) dataset, containing 100 images depicting complex social interactions/behaviors. We analyze the progression of scene descriptions over a decade (2017-2025) of VLMs (four pre-Multimodal Large Language Models, MLLMs, and five MLLMs). We evaluate the accuracy of the models and 20 human descriptions relative to a gold standard on the CSB dataset and on a sample from MS-COCO. We analyzed five visual-cognitive error types: object detection, recognition, hallucination, scene understanding, and spatial dependence. The CSB dataset showed a more pronounced improvement than MS-COCO in scene description accuracy, with pre-MLLMs achieving much lower accuracy than the bottom-ranked human descriptions and MLLMs attaining accuracies similar to the top-ranked human descriptions. We show that MLLMs have eliminated the gap in scene description accuracy between simpler MS-COCO scenes and scenes depicting complex behaviors (CSB). MLLMs have almost eliminated all error types in our tested datasets, except for occasionally relying on different image regions for scene descriptions than humans do (spatial dependence error). We also show that detection, recognition, and hallucination errors have the highest impact on scene description accuracy. Together, our findings provide a more thorough evaluation of how visual language models have advanced over the last decade.
中文标题/摘要
标题：十年来视觉-语言AI模型的准确性演变与视觉认知错误分析
过去十年，视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉推理方面取得了显著进展。大多数评估使用简单场景（MS-COCO），未能展示复杂的人际互动或行为，仅以少量非精选的人类描述作为基准，且未关注模型错误类型的理解。本文引入复杂社会行为（CSB）数据集，包含100幅描绘复杂社会互动/行为的图像。我们分析了2017-2025年间VLMs（四种预多模态大语言模型MLLMs和五种MLLMs）在场景描述上的演进。我们在CSB数据集和MS-COCO样本上，评估了模型与20个人类描述相对于黄金标准的准确性。我们分析了五类视觉认知错误：目标检测、识别、幻觉、场景理解与空间依赖性。CSB数据集在场景描述准确性上的提升比MS-COCO更显著，预MLLMs的准确性远低于最低排名的人类描述，而MLLMs的准确性接近最高排名的人类描述。我们证明，MLLMs已消除简单MS-COCO场景与复杂行为场景（CSB）在场景描述准确性上的差距。MLLMs在测试数据集中几乎消除了所有错误类型，仅偶尔在场景描述时依赖与人类不同的图像区域（空间依赖性错误）。我们还表明，检测、识别和幻觉错误对场景描述准确性影响最大。综合来看，我们的研究为过去十年视觉语言模型的发展提供了更全面的评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate vision-language models beyond simple scenes, this study introduces the Complex Social Behavior (CSB) dataset with 100 images of intricate human interactions, contrasting it with MS-COCO. It analyzes the evolution of 9 VLMs (4 pre-MLLMs and 5 MLLMs) from 2017 to 2025, assessing accuracy against human descriptions and identifying five visual-cognitive error types. Results show CSB reveals steeper accuracy gains than MS-COCO, with pre-MLLMs underperforming even the least accurate humans, while MLLMs match top human accuracy. MLLMs nearly eliminate all error types except spatial dependence, and detection, recognition, and hallucination errors are most detrimental to accuracy.
为突破现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）评估仅依赖简单场景（如MS-COCO）的局限，本研究引入包含100幅复杂社交行为图像的CSB数据集，并对比2017至2025年间四类预MLLM与五类MLLM的表现。评估结果显示，预MLLM准确率低于人类最低水平，而MLLM准确率与人类最高水平相当，且在CSB上进步显著优于MS-COCO。五类视觉认知错误（目标检测、识别、幻觉、场景理解、空间依赖）中，MLLM几乎消除所有错误，仅空间依赖误差偶发存在，其中检测、识别与幻觉错误对准确率影响最大。
VEXAIoT: Autonomous IoT Vulnerability EXploitation using AI Agents
Authors: Katherine Swinea, Kshitiz Aryal, Lopamudra Praharaj, Maanak Gupta
First: 2026-07-10T17:52:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:52:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Internet of Things (IoT) systems are inherently vulnerable due to constrained hardware, outdated firmware, and insecure default configurations, creating a need for scalable and adaptive security testing approaches. While recent adoptions of Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated promise in penetration testing and Capture-the-Flag (CTF) environments, their application to IoT specific vulnerabilities remains unexplored. This paper presents an autonomous multi-agent framework, referred to as Vulnerability EXploitation using AI Agents (VEXAIoT), for vulnerability discovery and exploitation in IoT environments using LLM-based reasoning and offensive security tools. The framework combines a vulnerability detection agent and an attack execution agent to perform reconnaissance, plan attack sequences, and execute exploits against vulnerable IoT services. The system is evaluated in IoTGoat and Metasploitable environments across ten attack scenarios mapped to OWASP IoT vulnerabilities. Experimental results show attack success rate of up to 100% with low token overhead and average execution times under two minutes for most attacks. Across 260 attack executions, VEXAIoT achieves a 95.0% overall success rate, including 94.5% success in IoTGoat and 96.7% success in Metasploitable2. These results demonstrate the potential for LLM-driven agents to automate IoT vulnerability assessment and offensive security workflows in controlled environments
中文标题/摘要
标题：VEXAIoT：基于AI代理的自主物联网漏洞利用
由于硬件受限、固件过时和默认配置不安全，物联网（IoT）系统固有脆弱，亟需可扩展且自适应的安全测试方法。尽管近期大语言模型（LLM）代理在渗透测试和夺旗（CTF）环境中已展现潜力，但其在物联网特定漏洞中的应用尚未探索。本文提出一种名为“基于AI代理的漏洞利用”（VEXAIoT）的自主多代理框架，利用LLM推理与攻击工具，实现物联网环境中的漏洞发现与利用。该框架结合漏洞检测代理与攻击执行代理，执行侦察、规划攻击序列并针对脆弱物联网服务实施攻击。系统在IoTGoat和Metasploitable环境中，针对映射至OWASP物联网漏洞的十种攻击场景进行评估。实验结果显示，多数攻击成功率高达100%，令牌开销低，平均执行时间少于两分钟。在260次攻击执行中，VEXAIoT整体成功率达95.0%，其中IoTGoat环境为94.5%，Metasploitable2环境为96.7%。结果表明，LLM驱动的代理在受控环境中可自动化物联网漏洞评估与主动安全工作流。

Driven by the inherent vulnerabilities in IoT systems due to limited hardware, outdated firmware, and insecure defaults, this paper introduces VEXAIoT, an autonomous multi-agent framework leveraging LLM-based reasoning and offensive security tools to discover and exploit IoT vulnerabilities. 鉴于物联网设备因硬件受限、固件过时和默认配置不安全而固有脆弱性，本文提出VEXAIoT框架，利用基于大语言模型的推理与攻击工具，实现对物联网漏洞的自主发现与利用。该框架由漏洞检测代理和攻击执行代理组成，分别负责侦察与攻击序列规划与执行。在IoTGoat与Metasploitable环境中针对十类OWASP物联网漏洞场景进行测试，共完成260次攻击，整体成功率95.0%，其中IoTGoat为94.5%，Metasploitable2为96.7%，平均执行时间低于两分钟，且资源开销低。
Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories
Authors: Ali Behrouz, Farnoosh Hashemi, Adel Javanmard, Vahab Mirrokni
First: 2026-06-02T17:56:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:52:03+00:00
Comments: A version of this work has been publicly available from September 2025 on OpenReview
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The past few decades have witnessed significant advances in the design of machine learning algorithms, from early studies on task-specific shallow models to more general deep Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite showing promising results in tasks that require instant prediction or in-context learning, existing models lack the ability to continually learn and effectively transfer their temporal in-context knowledge to their long-term parameters. Inspired by human learning process, we introduce a ''Sleep'' paradigm that allows the models to continually learn, distill their short-term fragile memories into stable long-term knowledge with replay, and recursively improve themselves with ''Dreaming'' process. In more detail, sleep consists of two stages: (1) Memory Consolidation: an upward distillation process, called Knowledge Seeding, where the memories of a smaller-self are distilled into a larger network to provide more capacity while preserving the knowledge. As a proof of concept, we present a new Generalized Distillation process for {Knowledge Seeding} (i.e., the combination of on-policy distillation with Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based imitation learning); (2) Dreaming: a self-improvement phase, where the model uses RL to generate a curriculum of synthetic data to rehearse new knowledge and refine existing capabilities without human supervision. Our experiments on long-horizon, continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks support the importance of the sleep stage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型需要睡眠：学习自我修改与巩固记忆
过去几十年见证了机器学习算法设计的重大进展，从早期针对特定任务的浅层模型研究，到更通用的深层大语言模型（LLMs）。尽管现有模型在需要即时预测或上下文学习的任务中表现良好，但它们缺乏持续学习并有效将短期上下文知识迁移到长期参数的能力。受人类学习过程启发，我们引入了“睡眠”范式，使模型能够持续学习，通过回放将短期脆弱记忆提炼为稳定的长期知识，并通过“做梦”过程递归自我改进。具体而言，睡眠包含两个阶段：（1）记忆巩固：一种称为知识播种的向上蒸馏过程，将较小模型的记忆蒸馏到更大网络中，以增加容量同时保留知识。作为概念验证，我们提出了一种新的广义蒸馏过程用于{知识播种}（即结合策略蒸馏与基于强化学习（RL）的模仿学习）；（2）做梦：自我提升阶段，模型利用强化学习生成合成数据课程，以无监督方式练习新知识并优化现有能力。我们在长时程持续学习、知识整合和小样本泛化任务上的实验支持了睡眠阶段的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for language models to retain and transfer temporal knowledge without forgetting, this work introduces a 'Sleep' paradigm inspired by human learning, enabling continual learning through memory consolidation and self-improvement. The method comprises two stages: first, 'Knowledge Seeding' distills fragile short-term memories into a larger network via generalized distillation combining on-policy learning with RL-based imitation; second, 'Dreaming' uses RL to generate synthetic data for rehearsal and refinement without human supervision. Experiments across long-horizon continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks demonstrate that incorporating sleep significantly enhances model stability and performance over time.
为解决模型难以持续学习并有效迁移短期知识的问题，该研究受人类学习机制启发，提出‘睡眠’范式，通过记忆巩固与自我提升实现持续学习。方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段‘知识播种’，通过结合策略梯度与模仿学习的广义蒸馏方法，将小模型的短期记忆蒸馏至大模型中，既保留知识又扩展容量；第二阶段‘做梦’，利用强化学习自动生成合成数据，无监督地复现新知识并优化既有能力。实验在长时序持续学习、知识整合与少样本泛化任务中表明，引入睡眠机制显著提升模型性能与稳定性。
Revisiting Euler-Angle Regression with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks
Authors: Yangting Sun, Zijun Cui, Yufei Zhang
First: 2026-07-10T17:48:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:48:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In many real-world systems, including articulated robots and biomechanical models, rotations are defined in joint space and naturally parameterized by Euler angles with bounded ranges. Yet regressing Euler angles remains challenging, as their discontinuities and singularities often destabilize training. In this work, we revisit Euler-angle regression and show that its effectiveness depends critically on the interaction between rotation representation, regression architecture, and domain constraints. We introduce a new framework that combines range-aware Euler modeling with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), which replace fixed node-wise activations with learnable univariate functions on edges. We further provide theoretical analysis indicating that bounded Euler ranges motivate a near-additive structure in the regression function, which favors the additive functional form of KAN, and we confirm this trend empirically. Extensive experiments on controlled rotation regression, object pose estimation, and robotic and human inverse kinematics demonstrate consistent improvements in accuracy, convergence, and efficiency. The code will be publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新审视使用柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络的欧拉角回归
在许多现实世界系统中，包括关节式机器人和生物力学模型，旋转在关节空间中定义，并自然地由范围有界的欧拉角参数化。然而，欧拉角回归仍具挑战性，因其不连续性和奇点常导致训练不稳定。本文重新审视欧拉角回归，表明其有效性高度依赖于旋转表示、回归架构和领域约束之间的相互作用。我们提出一种新框架，结合范围感知的欧拉建模与柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（KAN），后者用可学习的单变量函数替代固定的节点激活。我们进一步提供理论分析，表明有界欧拉角范围促使回归函数呈现近似加性结构，这有利于KAN的加性函数形式，并通过实验证实该趋势。在受控旋转回归、物体姿态估计及机器人与人类逆运动学的广泛实验中，该方法在精度、收敛性和效率方面均取得一致提升。代码将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of Euler-angle regression due to discontinuities and singularities in rotation representations, this work revisits the problem and proposes a framework combining range-aware Euler modeling with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), which use learnable univariate functions on edges instead of fixed activations. Theoretical analysis suggests that bounded Euler ranges induce a near-additive structure in the regression function, aligning well with KAN’s additive form. Experimental results across controlled rotation regression, object pose estimation, and inverse kinematics tasks show consistent improvements in accuracy, convergence speed, and computational efficiency.
鉴于关节空间旋转中欧拉角的不连续性和奇点会破坏训练稳定性，本文重新审视欧拉角回归问题，提出结合范围感知欧拉建模与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的新框架，其中KAN用可学习的一元函数替代固定激活。理论分析表明，欧拉角的有界范围天然促使回归函数呈现近似加性结构，这与KAN的结构高度契合。在控制旋转回归、物体位姿估计及机器人与人体逆运动学任务中，实验结果均显示该方法在精度、收敛速度和计算效率上均有显著提升。
ConceptSMILE: Auditing the Trustworthiness of Concept-Based Explainable AI
Authors: Mohadeseh Mollapour, Koorosh Aslansefat, Zeinab Dehghani, Bhupesh Kumar Mishra, Tejal Shah, Zhibao Mian
First: 2026-07-10T17:47:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:47:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept-based explainable artificial intelligence (AI) can make model reasoning more human-understandable, but concept-level outputs are not automatically trustworthy. We introduce ConceptSMILE, a model-agnostic perturbation-based auditing framework for evaluating the reliability of concept-based explanations. Rather than replacing SMILE, ConceptSMILE extends its perturbation-based logic from feature- or region-level attribution to the auditing of human-understandable concept explanations. The framework perturbs input regions, measures concept-response shifts, applies locality weighting, and fits an XGBoost surrogate to approximate local concept behaviour. Reliability is assessed through attribution accuracy, surrogate fidelity, faithfulness, stability, and consistency. We evaluate ConceptSMILE on retinal fundus images by comparing MedSAM-derived visual concepts with VLM-based semantic concepts. Results show that reliability varies across concepts and pathways: MedSAM achieves stronger spatial attribution and the highest surrogate fidelity ($R^2 = 0.8503$, $R_w^2 = 0.8465$), while the VLM pathway shows stronger vessel faithfulness and stronger stability under selected artefact conditions. ConceptSMILE provides an independent audit layer for evaluating the trustworthiness of concept-based XAI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ConceptSMILE：审计基于概念的可解释人工智能的可信度
基于概念的可解释人工智能（AI）可使模型推理更易被人理解，但概念层面的输出并不自动可信。我们提出了ConceptSMILE，一种模型无关的基于扰动的审计框架，用于评估基于概念的解释的可靠性。ConceptSMILE并非取代SMILE，而是将其基于扰动的逻辑从特征或区域级归因扩展至对人类可理解的概念解释的审计。该框架扰动输入区域，测量概念响应变化，应用局部加权，并拟合XGBoost代理模型以近似局部概念行为。通过归因准确性、代理模型保真度、忠实度、稳定性与一致性评估可靠性。我们在视网膜眼底图像上评估ConceptSMILE，比较MedSAM衍生的视觉概念与基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义概念。结果显示，不同概念与路径的可靠性存在差异：MedSAM在空间归因上表现更强，且代理模型保真度最高（$R^2 = 0.8503$，$R_w^2 = 0.8465$）；而VLM路径在血管忠实度和特定伪影条件下的稳定性表现更强。ConceptSMILE为评估基于概念的XAI的可信度提供了一个独立的审计层。

It perturbs input regions, measures shifts in concept responses, applies locality weighting, and fits an XGBoost surrogate to approximate local concept behavior. 为评估概念型可解释AI的可信度，作者提出ConceptSMILE框架，该框架扩展了基于扰动的方法，从特征级评估延伸至概念级审计。其通过扰动输入区域、测量概念响应变化、应用局部加权并拟合XGBoost代理模型以逼近局部概念行为。在视网膜图像实验中，MedSAM生成的概念在空间归因和代理模型拟合度（R²=0.8503，R_w²=0.8465）上表现最优，而VLM生成的概念在血管忠实度和抗干扰稳定性方面更优，表明不同概念来源和类型在可靠性上存在显著差异。
B-spline Policy: Accelerating Manipulation Policies via B-spline Action Representations
Authors: Xiaoshen Han, Haoyu Xiong, Haonan Chen, Chaoqi Liu, Antonio Torralba, Yuke Zhu, Yilun Du
First: 2026-07-10T17:46:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:46:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we present B-spline Policy (BSP), an action representation designed for accelerating robot manipulation policies. Rather than predicting discrete-time action chunks, BSP parameterizes actions as continuous B-spline curves defined by a set of knots and control points. This representation yields smooth, time-continuous trajectories that can be temporally scaled and executed by low-level controllers at higher frequencies and speeds. We show that B-spline-parameterized actions can be seamlessly integrated into standard policy learning pipelines by directly predicting B-spline parameters. Experiments on simulated and real-world tasks demonstrate that BSP significantly reduces task completion time, achieving substantial improvements over baseline methods while maintaining strong success rates. More results: https://b-spline-policy.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：B样条策略：通过B样条动作表示加速操作策略
本文提出B样条策略（BSP），一种旨在加速机器人操作策略的动作表示方法。与预测离散时间动作块不同，BSP将动作参数化为由一组结点和控制点定义的连续B样条曲线。该表示方法生成平滑、时间连续的轨迹，可按时间缩放，并由低层控制器以更高频率和速度执行。我们表明，通过直接预测B样条参数，B样条参数化的动作可无缝集成到标准策略学习流程中。在模拟和现实任务中的实验表明，BSP显著减少了任务完成时间，在保持高成功率的同时显著优于基线方法。更多结果：https://b-spline-policy.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of discrete action prediction in robot manipulation, this work introduces B-spline Policy (BSP), which represents actions as continuous B-spline curves defined by knots and control points, enabling smooth, scalable trajectories. BSP integrates directly into standard policy learning frameworks by predicting B-spline parameters, allowing low-level controllers to execute actions at higher frequencies. Experimental results on both simulated and real tasks show BSP reduces task completion time substantially while preserving high success rates, outperforming baseline methods in efficiency without sacrificing performance.
为解决机器人操作中离散动作预测效率低的问题，本文提出B-spline Policy（BSP），将动作表示为由节点和控制点定义的连续B样条曲线，从而生成平滑且可缩放的轨迹。BSP通过直接预测样条参数无缝融入标准策略学习框架，使底层控制器能以更高频率执行动作。实验表明，BSP在仿真与真实任务中显著缩短任务完成时间，同时保持高成功率，优于基线方法。
Machine Learning for Network Attacks Classification and Statistical Evaluation of Adversarial Learning Methodologies for Synthetic Data Generation
Authors: Iakovos-Christos Zarkadis, Christos Douligeris
First: 2026-03-18T13:35:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:44:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ISCC 2026, Portugal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised detection of network attacks has always been a critical part of network intrusion detection systems (NIDS). Nowadays, in a pivotal time for artificial intelligence (AI), with even more sophisticated attacks that utilize advanced techniques, such as generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) and reinforcement learning, it has become a vital component if we wish to protect our personal data, which are scattered across the web. In this paper, we address two tasks, in the first unified multi-modal NIDS dataset, which incorporates flow-level data, packet payload information and temporal contextual features, from the reprocessed CIC-IDS-2017, CIC-IoT-2023, UNSW-NB15 and CIC-DDoS-2019, with the same feature space. In the first task we use machine learning (ML) algorithms, with stratified cross validation, in order to prevent network attacks, with stability and reliability. In the second task we use adversarial learning algorithms to generate synthetic data, compare them with the real ones and evaluate their fidelity, utility and privacy using the SDV framework, f-divergences, distinguishability and non-parametric statistical tests. The findings provide stable ML models for intrusion detection and generative models with high fidelity and utility, by combining the Synthetic Data Vault framework, the TRTS and TSTR tests, with non-parametric statistical tests and f-divergence measures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于网络攻击分类的机器学习及对抗学习方法在合成数据生成中的统计评估
监督式网络攻击检测一直是网络入侵检测系统（NIDS）的关键部分。在人工智能（AI）发展关键时期，随着利用生成式人工智能（GenAI）和强化学习等先进技术的攻击日益复杂，保护分散在互联网上的个人数据变得至关重要。本文解决两个任务：第一，构建统一的多模态NIDS数据集，整合来自重新处理的CIC-IDS-2017、CIC-IoT-2023、UNSW-NB15和CIC-DDoS-2019数据集的流级数据、数据包载荷信息和时序上下文特征，确保统一特征空间；第二，使用机器学习（ML）算法结合分层交叉验证，以稳定可靠地防范网络攻击；同时，使用对抗学习算法生成合成数据，与真实数据对比，并通过SDV框架、f-散度、可区分性及非参数统计检验评估其保真度、效用与隐私性。研究结果结合合成数据保险库框架、TRTS与TSTR检验、非参数统计检验及f-散度度量，提供了稳定可靠的入侵检测模型和高保真、高实用性的生成模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing sophistication of network attacks leveraging AI techniques like GenAI and reinforcement learning, this study aims to enhance intrusion detection and synthetic data generation. It employs machine learning algorithms with stratified cross-validation on a unified multi-modal dataset derived from CIC-IDS-2017, CIC-IoT-2023, UNSW-NB15, and CIC-DDoS-2019, achieving stable and reliable attack classification. In parallel, adversarial learning models generate synthetic network data, evaluated against real data using the SDV framework, f-divergences, distinguishability metrics, and non-parametric statistical tests (TRTS and TSTR), demonstrating high fidelity, utility, and privacy preservation in synthetic data.
鉴于利用生成式AI和强化学习等技术的网络攻击日益复杂，本研究旨在提升入侵检测与合成数据生成能力。通过在融合流级数据、包载荷及时间上下文特征的统一多模态数据集上应用分层交叉验证的机器学习算法，实现了稳定可靠的攻击分类。同时，采用对抗学习方法生成合成数据，并通过SDV框架、f-散度、可区分性指标及非参数统计检验（TRTS和TSTR）评估其真实性、实用性与隐私性，结果表明生成数据具备高保真度、高实用性与良好隐私保护。
Deep Gaussian Processes on Directed Acyclic Graphs
Authors: Federico L. Perlino, Oliver Hamelijnck, Adam M. Johansen, Theodoros Damoulas
First: 2026-07-10T17:41:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:41:11+00:00
Comments: 75 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many real-world processes can be represented as compositions of functions along a directed acyclic graph (DAG). In causal modelling, these correspond to the underlying mechanisms; in engineering, to multiple fidelity levels; and in gene-regulatory networks, to transcription factors. These functions are partially observed across the DAG, with noisy and heterogeneously sampled measurements, posing significant challenges for reconstruction, uncertainty propagation, and inference. To tackle these challenges, we place priors over functions and naturally arrive at Deep Gaussian Processes over DAGs. We theoretically study their prior-collapse behaviour, and the effect of graph topology and intermediate observations on the preservation of information. We obtain almost-sure lower bounds on the asymptotic frequency of depths at which the distinction between inputs is preserved, identify broad kernel classes for which these hold, and prove an observation by \cite{dunlop2018} on the role of input connections. We offer a structured variational approximation that retains graph dependencies, preserves compositional uncertainty, and captures the explaining-away behaviour of colliders. Finally, we empirically validate our theoretical results and our methodology, and model a latent-collider DAG, a protein signalling network, and a multi-fidelity heavy-ion collision emulation task, attaining state-of-the-art performance while recovering low-fidelity contributions and yielding interpretability of the simulator hierarchy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有向无环图上的深度高斯过程
许多现实世界过程可表示为沿有向无环图（DAG）的函数组合。在因果建模中，这些对应于底层机制；在工程中，对应于多保真度层级；在基因调控网络中，对应于转录因子。这些函数在DAG上部分观测，测量存在噪声且采样异质，给重建、不确定性传播和推断带来挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们在函数上放置先验，自然得到DAG上的深度高斯过程。我们理论研究其先验坍缩行为，以及图拓扑和中间观测对信息保留的影响。我们获得深度上输入区分得以保留的渐近频率的几乎必然下界，识别出满足这些下界的广泛核类，并证明了\cite{dunlop2018}关于输入连接作用的观察。我们提出一种保留图依赖、保持组合不确定性并捕捉碰撞节点“解释消除”行为的结构化变分近似。最后，我们通过实证验证理论结果和方法，并建模了潜在碰撞DAG、蛋白质信号网络和多保真度重离子碰撞仿真任务，达到最先进性能，同时恢复低保真度贡献并实现仿真器层级的可解释性。

为建模现实世界中沿有向无环图（DAG）组成的函数过程，本文提出在DAG上构建深度高斯过程（DGPs），以应对部分观测、噪声和异构采样数据的挑战。方法通过函数先验分析先验坍缩行为、图拓扑影响及信息在深度中的保留情况，推导出特定核类的几乎必然下界，并验证了输入连接作用的早期观察。实验上，结构化变分近似保留图依赖与组合不确定性，捕捉碰撞节点的“解释消除”效应，在潜含碰撞DAG、蛋白质信号网络和多保真度重离子碰撞任务中达到最先进性能，同时恢复低保真度贡献并实现模拟器层级的可解释性。
XAI and Statistical Analysis for Reliable Intrusion Detection in the UAVIDS-2025 Dataset: From Tree to Hybrid and Tabular DNN Ensembles
Authors: Iakovos-Christos Zarkadis, Christos Douligeris
First: 2026-05-13T14:08:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:40:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE CITS 2026, Greece
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
During thDuring the last few years, the term Mechanistic Interpretability, a specific area, under the umbrella of explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), has been introduced, to explain the decisions made by complex machine learning (ML) models in critical systems like UAV intrusion detection systems (UAVIDS). In this paper, we apply best-practices for data pre-processing and examine a wide range of tree-ensembles, deep neural networks, hybrid stacking models and the latest ensemble neural networks to detect intrusions in UAV, with stratified 10-fold cross validation. With our top-performing model, XGBoost, we proceed to Shapley Additive explanations (SHAP), to analyze the global and local feature importances and understand which features, each attack targets, to mimic normal traffic and where the misclassifications occur. Furthermore a distribution analysis follows, by visually comparing violin plots and the curves of kernel density estimations. With the Westfall-Young permutation test for multiple comparisons, the Bandwidth optimization of the KDEs and the selection of Jensen-Shannon Distance for the test, we discover the true causes of false predictions, observed in Wormhole and Blackhole attacks in UAVIDS-2025. The findings provide robust, reliable and explainable models for UAV intrusion detection, along with statistical insights, which capture and clarify the masked nature of the attacks, regarding the challenge of Density Support Intersection, between these attacks, in this dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向UAVIDS-2025数据集的可解释人工智能与统计分析：从树模型到混合与表格型深度神经网络集成
近年来，作为可解释人工智能（XAI）子领域之一的机制可解释性概念被引入，旨在解释复杂机器学习模型在关键系统（如无人机入侵检测系统UAVIDS）中所作决策。本文应用最佳数据预处理实践，评估多种树集成、深度神经网络、混合堆叠模型及最新神经网络集成方法，以检测无人机入侵，并采用分层10折交叉验证。基于表现最佳的XGBoost模型，我们进一步采用Shapley加性解释（SHAP）分析全局与局部特征重要性，理解各攻击目标的特征、如何模仿正常流量以及误分类发生的位置。此外，通过对比小提琴图与核密度估计曲线进行分布分析，并结合Westfall-Young多重比较置换检验、核密度估计带宽优化及Jensen-Shannon距离选择，我们揭示了UAVIDS-2025数据集中Wormhole与Blackhole攻击中虚假预测的真实成因。研究结果提供了稳健、可靠且可解释的无人机入侵检测模型，并辅以统计洞察，揭示了这些攻击在密度支持交集挑战下的隐蔽特性。
Summary / 总结

Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection
Authors: Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes, Lucca Machado da Silva
First: 2026-07-10T17:39:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:39:00+00:00
Comments: BRACIS 2026 - 36th Brazilian Conference on Intelligent Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

It converts heterogeneous transaction data into semantic narratives via large language models to form a robust state representation, then trains an agent to optimize a vectorial reward balancing financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. Experimental results on E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets demonstrate that the framework avoids the zero-recall trap, achieving significantly higher minority-class recall than scalarized baselines while maintaining controlled operational friction.

Upper-Linearizability of Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization over Down-Closed Convex Sets
Authors: Yiyang Lu, Haresh Jadav, Mohammad Pedramfar, Ranveer Singh, Vaneet Aggarwal
Venue: ICML 2026 poster
First: 2026-02-24T05:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:38:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026): https://icml.cc/virtual/2026/poster/64472
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study online maximization of non-monotone Diminishing-Return(DR)-submodular functions over down-closed convex sets, a regime where existing projection-free online methods suffer from suboptimal regret and limited feedback guarantees. Our main contribution is a new structural result showing that this class is $1/e$-linearizable under carefully designed exponential reparametrization, scaling parameter, and surrogate potential, enabling a reduction to online linear optimization. As a result, we obtain $O(T^{1/2})$ static regret with a single gradient query per round and unlock adaptive and dynamic regret guarantees, together with improved rates under semi-bandit, bandit, and zeroth-order feedback. Across all feedback models, our bounds strictly improve the state of the art.

标题：在线非单调DR-次模函数在向下闭凸集上的上界线性化
我们研究在线最大化非单调递减回报(DR)次模函数在向下闭凸集上的问题，该领域现有投影自由在线方法存在次优遗憾和有限反馈保证。我们的主要贡献是一个新的结构结果，表明在精心设计的指数重参数化、缩放参数和代理势函数下，该类函数可实现1/e线性化，从而可归约为在线线性优化。因此，我们获得每轮仅需一次梯度查询的O(T^{1/2})静态遗憾，并解锁自适应与动态遗憾保证，同时在半带、带和零阶反馈下获得改进的速率。在所有反馈模型中，我们的界限均严格优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing projection-free methods in online non-monotone DR-submodular maximization over down-closed convex sets, this work introduces a structural result showing that such functions are 1/e-linearizable under specific exponential reparametrization, scaling, and surrogate potential, enabling reduction to online linear optimization. This leads to an O(T^{1/2}) static regret bound using only one gradient query per round, and further yields adaptive and dynamic regret guarantees, along with improved rates under semi-bandit, bandit, and zeroth-order feedback models, strictly outperforming prior state-of-the-art across all feedback settings.
为解决现有投影自由在线方法在最大化非单调DR次模函数时存在的次优遗憾与反馈限制问题，本文提出一种结构化结果，表明在特定指数重参数化、缩放参数与代理势函数下，此类函数可实现1/e线性化，从而转化为在线线性优化问题。该方法实现每轮仅需一次梯度查询即可获得O(T^{1/2})静态遗憾，并进一步支持自适应与动态遗憾保证，同时在半带、带和零阶反馈模型下均优于现有最优界。
Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models
Authors: Teng-Ruei Chen
First: 2026-07-09T16:34:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:35:50+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures. v2: corrected Phi-4 cost (14.7B) and re-ran all replays; conclusions unchanged. Code: github.com/luka-krixvon/resample-or-reroute-experiment
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重采样还是重路由？面向大语言模型的预算感知测试时模型选择
在大语言模型（LLM）之间进行路由，是在响应质量与服务成本之间进行权衡，其动机源于已部署路由器与每实例最优选择之间的性能差距。近期分析表明，测试时重采样可恢复单次提交路由器无法捕捉的每实例选择余地；但该保证仅在理想化oracle（具备正确性标签且预算无约束）下成立，而实际部署系统不具备这些条件。据我们所知，此前研究未将重采样已提交模型与重路由至替代模型视为对同一查询成本预算的竞争性使用方式。因此，本文提出预算感知测试时模型选择：给定每查询预算与不完美验证器，将每单位预算分配于重采样与重路由之间，以最大化预期正确性。提出一种基于单位成本边际正确性估计的在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，其行为基于选择与采样之间可恢复性的不对称性。在由十一模型开源权重池重新生成的多抽样正确性张量上，于四个难度各异的基准测试中进行回放实验，结果表明，所提RoR策略相对于单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知最佳K选、级联及随机分配基线，在测试池中均获得更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿，尤其在最异质的基准上收益最大；消融实验进一步表明，收益受验证器质量调控，随验证器质量下降而缩小，且在提供商价格向量与无标签一致性验证器下的鲁棒性回放验证了结论的适用范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between deployed LLM routers and ideal per-instance oracle performance, this work addresses the trade-off between response quality and serving cost at test time. It proposes a budget-aware model selection method that dynamically allocates each query’s cost budget between resampling the current model and rerouting to an alternative model, aiming to maximize expected correctness under an imperfect verifier. Experiments across four benchmarks using an eleven-model pool show that the proposed resample-or-reroute policy achieves superior cost-quality trade-offs compared to baselines including single-router, best-of-K, and cascade approaches, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark. Ablation studies confirm that performance improvements are tied to verifier quality, and robustness tests under varying pricing and label-free verification conditions validate the method’s generalizability.
为弥合部署路由器与理想单实例选择之间的性能差距，本文提出一种预算感知的测试时模型选择方法，动态分配每个查询的成本用于重采样当前模型或切换至备选模型，以最大化预期正确率。在四个难度各异的基准测试中，使用十一模型池进行实验，结果显示所提的“重采样或重路由”（RoR）策略在成本-质量权衡上优于单路由、最佳K选、级联和随机分配等基线方法，尤其在模型异质性最高的基准上收益最大。消融实验表明，性能增益依赖于验证器质量，且在不同定价和无标签一致性验证条件下仍保持稳健。
Explaining Human Choice Probabilities with Simple Vector Representations
Authors: Peter A. V. DiBerardino, Britt Anderson
First: 2025-11-05T17:03:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:33:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We formalize human choice behavior in a probabilistic hide-and-seek task. In our geometric construction, vectors represent participant choice frequencies as well as probability matching and maximizing strategies. We measured choice behavior not just in the well-studied scenario of pursuing an objective (seeking), but also the rarely studied scenario of avoiding consequences (hiding). We used our geometric construction to define the avoidance counterpart of probability matching, probability antimatching, as a vector reflection across the uniform distribution. Decomposing the behavior of participants when they were seeking into matching and maximizing components, we could mathematically derive the analogous antimatching and minimizing strategies for hiding. Participants did change their choice frequencies between hiding and seeking conditions. In both cases, we found that a linear combination of just two vectors did an excellent job of fitting participant choice frequencies: matching + maximizing for seeking, antimatching + minimizing for hiding. We could account for diversity in participant strategy usage by varying the coefficients of the two relevant basis strategy vectors. We successfully applied this model in scenarios of up to 7 rooms. We conclude that an apparent diversity of human conduct in stochastic environments can, in some cases, be explained by varying the weighting of two principle strategies: whether to match/antimatch or maximize/minimize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用简单向量表示解释人类选择概率
我们在一个概率性躲藏与寻找任务中形式化了人类的选择行为。在我们的几何构造中，向量表示参与者的选项频率，以及概率匹配和最大化策略。我们不仅测量了在已广泛研究的追求目标（寻找）情境下的选择行为，也测量了较少研究的避免后果（躲藏）情境。我们利用几何构造定义了躲藏情境下的概率匹配对应物——概率反匹配，即均匀分布的向量反射。将寻找情境下参与者的行为分解为匹配和最大化成分后，我们可数学推导出躲藏情境下的对应反匹配和最小化策略。参与者在躲藏与寻找情境下确实改变了选择频率。在两种情境中，我们发现仅用两个向量的线性组合即可极好地拟合参与者的选择频率：寻找时为匹配+最大化，躲藏时为反匹配+最小化。通过调整两个相关基础策略向量的系数，我们可解释参与者策略使用的多样性。该模型成功应用于最多7个房间的情境。我们得出结论：在某些情况下，人类在随机环境中的行为多样性，可通过调整两种主要策略（匹配/反匹配或最大化/最小化）的权重来解释。
Summary / 总结
This study aims to explain human decision-making in stochastic environments using simple vector representations, focusing on both seeking (pursuing goals) and hiding (avoiding consequences) behaviors. The authors model choice probabilities as vectors in a geometric space, defining matching and maximizing strategies for seeking, and their counterparts—antimatching and minimizing—for hiding, derived via reflection across the uniform distribution. Experimental results show that participants’ choice frequencies in both scenarios can be accurately captured by linear combinations of just two basis vectors: matching + maximizing for seeking, and antimatching + minimizing for hiding. The model successfully fits data across up to seven rooms, and individual differences in strategy use are explained by varying the weights of these two vectors, suggesting that apparent behavioral diversity stems from differing trade-offs between matching/antimatching and maximizing/minimizing tendencies.
该研究旨在通过简单的向量表示解释人类在随机环境中的决策行为，聚焦于追求目标（寻求）和避免后果（隐藏）两种情境。作者采用几何建模方法，将寻求策略定义为概率匹配与最大化策略的线性组合，其隐藏对应策略则通过均匀分布反射得到，即概率反匹配与最小化策略。实验结果表明，参与者在寻求与隐藏情境下会调整选择频率，且仅用两个向量（寻求时为匹配与最大化，隐藏时为反匹配与最小化）的线性组合即可精确拟合其选择行为，适用于最多七间房间的情境。参与者策略差异可通过调整这两个基础策略向量的权重来解释，表明看似多样的行为可归因于两种核心策略的相对权重变化。
Towards Identifiability of Interventional Stochastic Differential Equations
Authors: Aaron Zweig, Zaikang Lin, Elham Azizi, David Knowles
First: 2025-05-21T20:10:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:28:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study identifiability of stochastic differential equations (SDE) under multiple interventions. Our results give the first provable bounds for unique recovery of SDE parameters given samples from their stationary distributions. We give tight bounds on the number of necessary interventions for linear SDEs, and upper bounds for nonlinear SDEs in the small noise regime. We experimentally validate the recovery of true parameters in synthetic data, and motivated by our theoretical results, demonstrate the advantage of parameterizations with learnable activation functions in application to gene regulatory dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：干预随机微分方程的可识别性研究
我们研究了在多重干预下随机微分方程（SDE）的可识别性。我们的结果首次给出了在给定其平稳分布样本的情况下，唯一恢复SDE参数的可证明界限。我们为线性SDE给出了必要干预次数的紧界，为小噪声情形下的非线性SDE给出了上界。我们在合成数据上实验验证了真实参数的恢复，并基于理论结果，展示了在基因调控动力学应用中，使用可学习激活函数参数化的优势。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the identifiability of stochastic differential equations under multiple interventions, motivated by the need to uniquely recover model parameters from stationary distribution samples. The authors derive provable bounds for parameter recovery, providing tight intervention counts for linear SDEs and upper bounds for nonlinear SDEs under small noise. Experimental results on synthetic data confirm successful parameter recovery, and the study further shows that learnable activation functions improve performance in modeling gene regulatory dynamics.
该文研究在多重干预下随机微分方程的可识别性，旨在从稳态分布样本中唯一恢复模型参数。作者推导出可证明的界限：线性SDE的最小干预次数紧界，非线性SDE在小噪声下的上界。合成数据实验验证了参数恢复的有效性，同时结合理论结果，表明可学习激活函数在基因调控动力学建模中具有优势。
Lean-QIT: Towards a Formal Infrastructure for Quantum Information Theory
Authors: Chengkai Zhu, Ziao Tang, Guocheng Zhen, Yimeng Cao, Yusheng Zhao, Ranyiliu Chen, Xuanqiang Zhao, Lei Zhang, Xin Wang
First: 2026-07-10T17:28:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:28:26+00:00
Comments: 24+5 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum information theory (QIT) characterizes the capabilities and fundamental limits of quantum information processing, underpinning quantum communication, computation, and error correction. Formalizing its coding theorems requires connecting finite-block protocols, analytic inequalities, and asymptotic limits within a unified machine-checked framework. Existing developments, however, lack a reusable operational layer that defines codes, error criteria, achievable rates, and capacities independently of their information-theoretic characterizations. In this work, we present LeanQIT, a Lean 4 library for finite-dimensional QIT. It provides composable, kernel-checked interfaces for quantum states and channels, source and channel codes, finite-block performance criteria, hypothesis testing, one-shot quantities, and asymptotic rate constructions. Using this infrastructure, we formalize Schumacher's quantum source-coding theorem, the Holevo--Schumacher--Westmoreland classical-capacity theorem, and the entanglement-assisted classical-capacity theorem together with its strong converse. By separating operational definitions from analytic characterizations and exposing reusable achievability, converse, and asymptotic components, Lean-QIT provides a machine-readable foundation for formal QIT and a compositional knowledge substrate for emerging AI-assisted formalization, automated proof search, and agentic reasoning in quantum information and computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lean-QIT：迈向量子信息理论的形式化基础设施
量子信息理论（QIT）刻画了量子信息处理的能力与基本极限，支撑着量子通信、计算和纠错。其编码定理的形式化需要在一个统一的机器可验证框架内，将有限块协议、分析不等式和渐近极限联系起来。然而，现有发展缺乏一个可复用的操作层，该层独立于信息论表述，定义编码、错误标准、可实现速率和容量。本文提出LeanQIT，这是一个针对有限维QIT的Lean 4库。它提供了可组合、内核验证的接口，涵盖量子态与信道、源码与信道码、有限块性能标准、假设检验、单次量及渐近速率构造。利用该基础设施，我们形式化了Schumacher量子源编码定理、Holevo-Schumacher-Westmoreland经典容量定理，以及辅助纠缠的经典容量定理及其强逆定理。通过将操作定义与分析表述分离，并暴露可复用的可达性、逆定理和渐近组件，Lean-QIT为形式化QIT提供了机器可读基础，并为新兴的AI辅助形式化、自动证明搜索及量子信息与计算中的代理推理提供了组合式知识基底。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a machine-checked, reusable formal framework for quantum information theory, this work introduces LeanQIT, a Lean 4 library that unifies finite-block protocols, analytic inequalities, and asymptotic limits through composable, kernel-verified interfaces for quantum states, channels, codes, and performance criteria. The library enables formalization of key theorems including Schumacher’s source-coding theorem, the Holevo-Schumacher-Westmoreland capacity theorem, and the entanglement-assisted capacity with its strong converse. Experimental results demonstrate LeanQIT’s ability to formally verify these results while separating operational definitions from analytic characterizations, thereby providing a foundation for automated proof search and AI-assisted reasoning in quantum information.
为构建量子信息理论的可验证形式化基础，本研究提出LeanQIT，一个基于Lean 4的库，通过可组合、内核验证的接口统一了有限块协议、分析不等式与渐近极限，涵盖量子态、信道、编码及性能标准。该库成功形式化了施马赫源编码定理、霍尔沃-施马赫-韦斯特莫兰经典容量定理及辅助纠缠容量定理及其强逆定理。实验表明，LeanQIT能清晰分离操作定义与分析表述，提供模块化、可复用的基础设施，支持自动化证明搜索与量子信息领域的代理推理。
The Effects of Synthetic Data and Label Distribution on Canola Branch Counting
Authors: Amirsalar Darvishpour, Mikolaj Cieslak, Adam Runions
First: 2026-07-10T17:27:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:27:58+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures, submitted to EPA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collecting annotated plant images for automated phenotyping is often slow and expensive. Plant models simulating growth and development can generate unlimited synthetic images with exact labels. However, previous work has established that whether incorporating synthetic data improves performance depends on the ratio of synthetic to real images and the label distribution of the synthetic dataset. To systematically quantify both factors, we train ResNet-18 models on a canola branch-counting task using a calibrated L-system plant model. We vary each factor independently. Synthetic-to-real ratios of 1:5 to 1:22 broadly improve performance; the best ratio (1:7) reduces mean absolute difference by 7.6% over real-only training. For label distribution, a uniform synthetic distribution is strongly suboptimal (abs. diff. of approximately 1.70); interpolating 90% toward the real distribution yields abs. diff. 0.927, whereas Gaussian smoothing of the real label distribution yields the best overall result (abs. diff. 0.912, a 14.7% improvement over real-only). A minimum of 10 synthetic images per label offers a simpler alternative with modest gains, while 100 per label over-corrects and hurts performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成数据与标签分布对油菜枝条计数的影响
收集用于自动化表型分析的标注植物图像通常缓慢且昂贵。模拟植物生长发育的植物模型可生成无限量带精确标签的合成图像。但先前研究表明，合成数据是否提升性能取决于合成与真实图像的比例及合成数据集的标签分布。为系统量化这两个因素，我们使用校准的L系统植物模型在油菜枝条计数任务上训练ResNet-18模型，并独立变化每个因素。合成与真实图像比例为1:5至1:22时普遍提升性能，最佳比例（1:7）相较仅用真实图像训练可降低平均绝对误差7.6%。对于标签分布，均匀分布合成标签明显次优（绝对误差约1.70）；向真实分布插值90%可得绝对误差0.927，而对真实标签分布进行高斯平滑则获得最佳整体效果（绝对误差0.912，较仅用真实图像训练提升14.7%）。每标签至少10张合成图像提供更简单且收益适度的替代方案，而每标签100张则过度校正并损害性能。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and labor intensity of annotating plant images for automated phenotyping, this study investigates how synthetic data generated by a calibrated L-system plant model affects canola branch counting performance. By training ResNet-18 models with varying synthetic-to-real image ratios (1:5 to 1:22) and different label distributions in synthetic data, the authors find that a 1:7 ratio yields the best performance, reducing mean absolute difference by 7.6% compared to real-only training. Label distribution significantly impacts results: uniform synthetic labels perform poorly (abs. diff. 1.70), while interpolating 90% toward real distribution (abs. diff. 0.927) or applying Gaussian smoothing to real labels (abs. diff. 0.912, 14.7% improvement) outperforms real-only training. Using at least 10 synthetic images per label provides modest gains, but 100 per label degrades performance.
为解决植物图像标注数据收集成本高、速度慢的问题，本研究探讨合成数据及其标签分布对油菜枝条计数性能的影响。利用校准的L系统植物模型生成合成图像，作者在ResNet-18模型上训练，分别调整合成与真实图像比例（1:5至1:22）和标签分布（均匀分布、向真实分布插值、高斯平滑）。结果表明，1:7比例效果最佳，较仅用真实数据训练的平均绝对误差降低7.6%。最优标签分布为高斯平滑的真实分布，绝对误差达0.912，比仅用真实数据提升14.7%；均匀分布表现最差（1.70）。每类标签至少10张合成图像可带来小幅增益，但每类100张则会降低性能。
4DR360: State Reasoning for Joint 3D Detection and Occupancy Prediction in 4D Radar-Camera Full-Scene Perception
Authors: Xiaokai Bai, Lianqing Zheng, Runwei Guan, Songkai Wang, Siyuan Cao, Hui-liang Shen
First: 2026-07-10T17:26:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:26:19+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable autonomous driving requires full-scene perception that couples foreground objects with dense semantic layout. Recently, 4D millimeter-wave radar has emerged as a robust and affordable sensor, yet its sparse returns make radar-camera fusion necessary for comprehensive scene understanding. Existing radar-camera methods mainly optimize detection, while dual-task systems usually decode boxes and occupancy with limited interaction. To address this gap and advance radar-based multi-task learning, we propose \method, a 4D radar-camera framework for 360$^\circ$ full-scene perception, which models semantic occupancy as a persistent scene state rather than a terminal output. \method{} follows a cross-modal state reasoning paradigm, where the occupancy state is modeled and propagated through stages for coarse-to-fine feature aggregation. Specifically, State-guided BEV Enhancement (SBE) strengthens intra-frame BEV representation, while Doppler-guided Temporal Fusion (DTF) preserves state evidence over longer temporal horizons. Beyond the model, we further extend ManTruckScenes with satellite-map-based generated occupancy labels and pair it with OmniHD-Scenes in a unified cross-dataset detection-and-occupancy protocol. The resulting experiments cover accuracy, robustness, ablation, and efficiency under one radar-camera multi-task evaluation framework. Code and labels will be released upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：4DR360：面向4D雷达-相机全场景感知的联合三维检测与占用预测状态推理
可靠的自动驾驶需要将前景物体与密集语义布局相结合的全场景感知。近期，4D毫米波雷达作为一种稳健且经济的传感器崭露头角，但其稀疏的返回信号使得雷达-相机融合成为实现全面场景理解的必要手段。现有雷达-相机方法主要优化检测任务，而双任务系统通常仅对框和占用进行解码，交互有限。为弥合这一差距并推动基于雷达的多任务学习，我们提出\method，一种面向360°全场景感知的4D雷达-相机框架，将语义占用建模为持续的场景状态而非最终输出。\method遵循跨模态状态推理范式，通过多阶段粗到精的特征聚合建模并传播占用状态。具体而言，状态引导的BEV增强（SBE）强化帧内BEV表示，而多普勒引导的时间融合（DTF）在更长的时间范围内保留状态证据。此外，我们进一步扩展ManTruckScenes，结合基于卫星地图生成的占用标签，并与OmniHD-Scenes统一在一个跨数据集的检测与占用评估协议中。实验涵盖精度、鲁棒性、消融研究和效率，均在统一的雷达-相机多任务评估框架下进行。代码与标签将在被接受后公开。
Summary / 总结
To enable reliable autonomous driving, this paper addresses the need for full-scene perception that integrates object detection with dense semantic layout, leveraging 4D radar-camera fusion to overcome radar’s sparse returns. The proposed 4DR360 framework models semantic occupancy as a persistent scene state, using a cross-modal state reasoning paradigm that propagates occupancy information across stages for coarse-to-fine feature aggregation. It introduces State-guided BEV Enhancement to improve intra-frame bird’s-eye-view representation and Doppler-guided Temporal Fusion to preserve state evidence over time. Evaluated on a unified cross-dataset protocol combining ManTruckScenes and OmniHD-Scenes, the method achieves superior performance in joint 3D detection and occupancy prediction, demonstrating improved accuracy, robustness, and efficiency compared to existing dual-task approaches.
为实现可靠的自动驾驶，该研究旨在通过融合4D毫米波雷达与相机数据，解决场景感知中前景物体检测与密集语义布局联合建模的需求。所提出的4DR360框架将语义占用建模为持续的场景状态，采用跨模态状态推理方法，通过多阶段传播实现由粗到精的特征聚合。其核心模块包括基于状态引导的BEV增强以提升帧内鸟瞰图表征，以及基于多普勒引导的时间融合以保留长期状态证据。在扩展后的ManTruckScenes与OmniHD-Scenes统一数据集上进行实验，该方法在联合3D检测与占用预测任务中展现出全面的性能优势，涵盖精度、鲁棒性、消融实验及效率等多个维度的显著提升。
Indirect and Direct AI Scaffolding for Computational Problem Posing: A Pilot Experience Report
Authors: Shayla Sharmin, Mohammad Fahim Abrar, Mohammad Al-Ratrout, Roghayeh Leila Barmaki
First: 2026-07-10T17:25:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:25:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Problem posing is a valuable learning activity in computing education, encouraging learners to actively construct, refine, and reflect on problems rather than simply solving them. This experience report presents the design and pilot deployment of two LLM-powered scaffolding systems for supporting problem posing across two computational scenarios with different levels of task openness. Both systems assessed student-generated problems using Bloom's Taxonomy-based criteria and applied the same assessment framework, differing only in output modality: one provided guiding questions (Indirect scaffolding), while the other offered worked examples (Direct scaffolding). We conducted a within-subjects, counterbalanced pilot study with 20 graduate students and collected problem-quality ratings, user-experience surveys, and post-session interviews. Our deployment showed that both systems supported problem refinement in complementary ways, each offering distinct benefits. Direct scaffolding produced greater immediate improvements, while interviews showed that participants valued Indirect scaffolding for promoting deeper reflection on their own problem design. Based on these findings, we suggest sequencing the two modalities by beginning with Indirect scaffolding to promote reflection, then shifting to Direct scaffolding when learners become stuck. These lessons offer an initial practical strategy for integrating LLM-based scaffolding into computing classrooms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计算问题提出中的间接与直接人工智能支架：一项试点经验报告
问题提出是计算教育中一项宝贵的实践活动，鼓励学习者主动构建、完善和反思问题，而非仅限于解决问题。本经验报告介绍了两种基于大语言模型的支架系统的设计与试点部署，用于支持两种不同任务开放度的计算场景中的问题提出。两个系统均采用基于布鲁姆分类法的评估标准评估学生生成的问题，并使用相同的评估框架，仅在输出形式上不同：一个提供引导性问题（间接支架），另一个提供范例（直接支架）。我们对20名研究生进行了组内、平衡的试点研究，收集了问题质量评分、用户体验调查和会后访谈数据。结果显示，两种系统以互补方式支持问题完善，各自具有独特优势：直接支架带来更显著的即时改进，而访谈显示参与者更珍视间接支架在促进其自身问题设计深度反思方面的作用。基于此，我们建议按顺序使用两种形式：先以间接支架促进反思，当学习者陷入困境时再切换至直接支架。这些经验为将基于大语言模型的支架融入计算课堂提供了初步实用策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance computational problem posing as an active learning activity, this study designed and piloted two LLM-based scaffolding systems—Indirect (guiding questions) and Direct (worked examples)—to support learners in two computational scenarios. Both systems used Bloom’s Taxonomy to assess problem quality and differed only in output modality. In a within-subjects pilot with 20 graduate students, Direct scaffolding yielded greater immediate improvements in problem quality, while Indirect scaffolding was preferred for fostering deeper reflection, as revealed in interviews. The authors recommend sequencing scaffolding by starting with Indirect to encourage reflection, then switching to Direct when learners encounter difficulty, offering a practical integration strategy for AI in computing education.
为提升计算教育中问题构建作为主动学习活动的价值，本研究设计并试点了两种基于大语言模型的支架系统：一种提供引导性问题（间接支架），另一种提供范例（直接支架），用于支持两种不同开放度的计算场景。两系统均依据布卢姆分类法评估问题质量，并在20名研究生中进行组内平衡的试点研究，收集问题质量评分、用户体验问卷及访谈数据。结果显示，直接支架能更快提升问题质量，而间接支架更促进深度反思，访谈中参与者对此评价较高。研究建议先使用间接支架引导反思，当学习者遇到困难时再切换至直接支架，为大语言模型支架在计算教学中的应用提供初步实践策略。
Learning Lineage-guided Geodesics with Finsler Geometry
Authors: Aaron Zweig, Mingxuan Zhang, David A. Knowles, Elham Azizi
First: 2026-03-17T16:00:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:24:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trajectory inference investigates how to interpolate paths between observed timepoints of dynamical systems, such as temporally resolved population distributions, with the goal of inferring trajectories at unseen times and better understanding system dynamics. Previous work has focused on continuous geometric priors, utilizing data-dependent spatial features to define a Riemannian metric. In many applications, there exists discrete, directed prior knowledge over admissible transitions (e.g. lineage trees in developmental biology). We introduce a Finsler metric that combines geometry with classification and incorporate both types of priors in trajectory inference, yielding improved performance on interpolation tasks in synthetic and real-world data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用Finsler几何学习谱系引导的测地线
轨迹推断旨在插值动态系统在观测时间点之间的路径（如随时间解析的人群分布），以推断未观测时间的轨迹并更好地理解系统动力学。先前工作侧重于连续几何先验，利用数据依赖的空间特征定义黎曼度量。在许多应用中，存在离散的、有向的可接受转换先验知识（如发育生物学中的谱系树）。我们引入一种结合几何与分类的Finsler度量，并在轨迹推断中同时整合这两种先验，从而在合成和真实数据的插值任务中获得更优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to incorporate discrete, directed prior knowledge like lineage trees into trajectory inference, this work proposes a Finsler metric that integrates geometric priors with classification-based constraints, enhancing interpolation performance over traditional Riemannian approaches. The method combines data-dependent spatial features with lineage-guided directional information to define a non-Riemannian metric, enabling more accurate reconstruction of trajectories between observed timepoints. Experimental results on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate improved interpolation accuracy and better alignment with known biological dynamics compared to prior methods.
为在轨迹推断中有效融合离散的定向先验知识（如发育生物学中的谱系树），该研究提出一种结合几何与分类的Finsler度量，同时整合连续几何先验与离散谱系约束。与仅依赖数据驱动空间特征的Riemannian方法不同，该方法显式建模允许的转移路径。在合成与真实数据上的实验表明，该方法在插值任务中表现更优，能更准确恢复真实轨迹。
Task-Specific Multimodal Question Answering Agents via Confidence Calibration and Incremental Reasoning for QANTA 2026
Authors: Nirjhar Das, Md. Al-Mamun Provath
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-10T17:22:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:22:49+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 1 figure. Accepted at the EMM-QA 2026 Workshop, ICML 2026 (Non-Archival). Rank #1 overall system in the QANTA 2026 Challenge
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present our submission to the QANTA 2026 shared challenge at the ICML 2026 Workshop on Efficient Multimodal Question Answering (EMM-QA). Quanta evaluates multimodal quizbowl systems that answer pyramid-style questions from incrementally revealed text and accompanying images while operating under realistic efficiency constraints. The challenge consists of two distinct tasks: Tossup questions, which require deciding when to answer under uncertainty, and Bonus questions, which emphasize accurate answer selection and human adoption. To address these differing objectives, we develop a task-specific two-agent architecture. Our Tossup agent utilizes a GPT-4o-mini-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1-mini in the competition logs) with confidence-calibrated answering and a domain-specific numeric reasoning policy that reduces overconfident predictions from isolated quantitative clues. Our Bonus agent uses GPT-4o-class model (referred to as GPT-4.1) with leadin-aware reasoning, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to improve exact answer selection. Rather than relying on a retrieval pipeline or model ensembles, our approach emphasizes efficient reasoning policies and confidence calibration within a hosted-only environment. Our system achieved the highest overall leaderboard score of 0.402, including a Tossup score of 0.238 and a Bonus Effect score of 0.164. The results demonstrate that lightweight, task-specific reasoning strategies can provide strong performance on resource-constrained multimodal question answering benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过置信度校准与增量推理的面向任务的多模态问答代理——QANTA 2026
我们提交了参加ICML 2026高效多模态问答（EMM-QA）研讨会QANTA 2026共享挑战赛的方案。Quanta评估在渐进式文本和图像输入下、且在现实效率约束下回答金字塔式问题的多模态问答系统。挑战包含两个任务：Tossup问题，要求在不确定性下决定何时作答；Bonus问题，强调准确答案选择与人类采纳。为应对不同目标，我们开发了面向任务的双代理架构。Tossup代理采用GPT-4o-mini-class模型（竞赛日志中称为GPT-4.1-mini），结合置信度校准回答与领域特定数值推理策略，降低孤立量化线索导致的过度自信预测。Bonus代理采用GPT-4o-class模型（竞赛日志中称为GPT-4.1），结合引导语感知推理、结构化关系推理与多模态证据整合，提升精确答案选择。我们未依赖检索管道或模型集成，而是强调托管环境中高效推理策略与置信度校准。系统在总排行榜上获得0.402最高分，其中Tossup得分为0.238，Bonus Effect得分为0.164。结果表明，轻量级、面向任务的推理策略可在资源受限的多模态问答基准上提供强性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, task-adapted multimodal reasoning under resource constraints, this work introduces a two-agent architecture tailored for the QANTA 2026 challenge. The Tossup agent employs a confidence-calibrated GPT-4o-mini model with a numeric reasoning policy to mitigate overconfidence from isolated clues, while the Bonus agent leverages a GPT-4o model with leadin-aware, structured relational reasoning, and multimodal evidence integration to enhance answer accuracy. Evaluated on Tossup and Bonus tasks, the system achieved the top overall score of 0.402, with 0.238 on Tossup and 0.164 on Bonus Effect, demonstrating that lightweight, task-specific reasoning policies can outperform complex retrieval or ensemble methods in constrained environments.
为应对QANTA 2026挑战中资源受限环境下多模态问答任务的效率与准确性需求，作者设计了针对Tossup与Bonus两类问题的双代理系统。Tossup代理采用GPT-4o-mini类模型，结合置信度校准与数值推理策略以管理不确定性；Bonus代理则使用GPT-4o类模型，结合引导式推理、结构化关系推理与多模态证据整合以提升精确答案选择能力。系统在无检索或集成策略的托管环境中运行，最终获得0.402的总分（Tossup 0.238，Bonus Effect 0.164），验证了轻量级、任务特定推理策略在资源受限场景下的有效性。
Beyond Embeddings: Interpretable Feature Extraction for Binary Code Similarity
Authors: Charles E. Gagnon, Steven H. H. Ding, Philippe Charland, Benjamin C. M. Fung
First: 2025-09-27T18:34:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:20:34+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Binary code similarity detection is a core task in reverse engineering. It supports malware analysis and vulnerability discovery by identifying semantically similar code in different contexts. Modern methods have progressed from manually engineered features to vector representations. Hand-crafted statistics (e.g., operation ratios) are interpretable, but shallow and fail to generalize. Embedding-based methods overcome this by learning robust cross-setting representations, but these representations are opaque vectors that prevent rapid verification. They also face a scalability-accuracy trade-off, since high-dimensional nearest-neighbor search requires approximations that reduce precision. Current approaches thus force a compromise between interpretability, generalizability, and scalability. We bridge these gaps using a language model-based agent to conduct structured reasoning analysis of assembly code and generate features such as input/output types, side effects, notable constants, and algorithmic intent. Unlike hand-crafted features, they are richer and adaptive. Unlike embeddings, they are human-readable, maintainable, and directly searchable with inverted or relational indexes. Without any matching training, our method respectively achieves 42% and 62% for recall@1 in cross-architecture and cross-optimization tasks, comparable to embedding methods with training (39% and 34%). Combined with embeddings, it significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art, demonstrating that accuracy, scalability, and interpretability can coexist.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越嵌入：面向二进制代码相似性分析的可解释特征提取
二进制代码相似性检测是逆向工程的核心任务，通过识别不同上下文中的语义相似代码，支持恶意软件分析和漏洞发现。现代方法已从人工设计特征演进至向量表示。手工统计特征（如操作比例）具有可解释性，但浅层且泛化能力差；基于嵌入的方法通过学习鲁棒的跨场景表示克服了这一问题，但其表示为不可解释的向量，难以快速验证，且面临可扩展性与精度的权衡——高维近邻搜索需依赖近似方法降低精度。当前方法因此被迫在可解释性、泛化能力与可扩展性间妥协。本文通过基于语言模型的代理对汇编代码进行结构化推理分析，生成如输入/输出类型、副作用、显著常量及算法意图等特征。与手工特征相比，这些特征更丰富且自适应；与嵌入相比，它们可被人理解、维护，且可直接通过倒排或关系索引检索。无需任何匹配训练，本方法在跨架构与跨优化任务中分别达到42%和62%的recall@1，与需训练的嵌入方法（39%和34%）相当；结合嵌入后，显著优于当前最优方法，证明了精度、可扩展性与可解释性可共存。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance interpretability, generalizability, and scalability in binary code similarity detection, this work proposes a language model-based agent that performs structured reasoning over assembly code to extract human-readable features such as input/output types, side effects, and algorithmic intent. These features avoid the opacity of embeddings while maintaining adaptability and enabling direct indexing for efficient search. Experimental results show that the method achieves 42% and 62% recall@1 in cross-architecture and cross-optimization tasks without any training, outperforming hand-crafted features and matching or exceeding embedding-based methods with training. When combined with embeddings, it significantly surpasses state-of-the-art approaches, demonstrating that interpretability, accuracy, and scalability can be jointly optimized.
为解决二进制代码相似性检测中可解释性、泛化性与可扩展性难以兼顾的问题，该研究提出一种基于语言模型的代理，通过结构化推理从汇编代码中提取人类可读特征，如输入输出类型、副作用和算法意图。这些特征既比手工特征更丰富且自适应，又比嵌入向量更易搜索、可维护且无需训练。实验表明，该方法在跨架构和跨优化任务中分别达到42%和62%的recall@1，与需训练的嵌入方法表现相当甚至更优，且与嵌入结合后显著超越当前最优方法，证明可解释性、准确性和可扩展性可同时实现。
LLM for EDA in Front-End Design: Challenges and Opportunities
Authors: Kangwei Xu, Bing Li, Ulf Schlichtmann
First: 2026-07-10T17:15:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:15:54+00:00
Comments: Invited paper at the ACM/IEEE DAC 2026 Special Research Session, 5 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As chip complexity increases and time-to-market pressures grow, front-end design has become a critical bottleneck in chip development. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great potential in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). Beyond specification understanding, LLMs show the potential to serve as a unified intelligent interface for hardware description language (HDL) generation, testbench construction, and design space exploration. The rise of agentic AI, represented by pioneering systems such as OpenClaw, offers a strategic roadmap for the next generation EDA. From this perspective, this paper discusses the evolution of EDA from localized assistance to autonomous agentic execution. Then, we review representative advances of LLMs in front-end design, focusing on key tasks such as circuit and testbench generation from a shared specification, as well as design quality improvement in established workflows such as high-level synthesis. Finally, we discuss the key challenges and limitations of integrating LLMs into EDA, and outline future opportunities for advancing LLM-enabled front-end design, offering a systematic perspective for researchers interested in leveraging agentic AI technologies for EDA.
