Driven by escalating chip complexity and market pressure, this paper explores how Large Language Models (LLMs) can transform front-end EDA from manual, localized tasks into autonomous agentic workflows. It reviews recent advances where LLMs generate HDL code and testbenches from shared specifications and enhance high-level synthesis, demonstrating improved design quality and efficiency. The study identifies key challenges such as reliability, interpretability, and integration with existing tools, while outlining future opportunities for agentic AI to enable fully automated design exploration and optimization.
随着芯片复杂度上升和上市压力增大，本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）如何将前端EDA从人工、局部化任务转变为自主代理式工作流。文章回顾了LLM在从自然语言规格生成HDL代码和测试平台、提升高层次综合设计质量方面的最新进展，展示了效率与质量的提升。研究指出了可靠性、可解释性及与现有EDA工具集成等关键挑战，并提出通过如OpenClaw等代理式AI框架推动端到端设计自动化，为未来研究提供系统性方向。
AS-Bridge: A Bidirectional Generative Framework Bridging Next-Generation Astronomical Surveys
Authors: Dichang Zhang, Yixuan Shao, Simon Birrer, Dimitris Samaras
First: 2026-03-12T13:41:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:13:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted at KDD26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The upcoming decade of observational cosmology will be shaped by large sky surveys, such as the ground-based LSST at the Vera C. Rubin Observatory and the space-based Euclid mission. While they promise an unprecedented view of the Universe across depth, resolution, and wavelength, their differences in observational modality, sky coverage, point-spread function, and scanning cadence make joint analysis beneficial, but also challenging. To facilitate joint analysis, we introduce A(stronomical)S(urvey)-Bridge, a bidirectional generative model that translates between ground- and space-based observations. AS-Bridge learns a diffusion model that employs a stochastic Brownian Bridge process between the LSST and Euclid observations. The two surveys have overlapping sky regions, where we can explicitly model the conditional probabilistic distribution between them. We show that this formulation enables new scientific capabilities beyond single-survey analysis, including faithful probabilistic predictions of missing survey observations and inter-survey detection of rare events. These results establish the feasibility of inter-survey generative modeling. AS-Bridge is therefore well-positioned to serve as a complementary component of future LSST-Euclid joint data pipelines, enhancing the scientific return once data from both surveys become available. Data and code are available at https://github.com/ZHANG7DC/AS-Bridge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AS-Bridge：连接下一代天文巡天的双向生成框架
未来十年的观测宇宙学将由大型天空巡天项目主导，如地面的Vera C. Rubin天文台LSST和空间的Euclid任务。尽管它们在深度、分辨率和波长上提供了前所未有的宇宙视图，但其观测模式、天空覆盖范围、点扩散函数和扫描周期的差异使得联合分析既有益处也具挑战性。为促进联合分析，我们提出了A(stronomical)S(urvey)-Bridge，一种可在地面与空间观测之间双向转换的生成模型。AS-Bridge学习一种扩散模型，利用LSST与Euclid观测之间的随机布朗桥过程。两项目在天空区域上存在重叠，我们可显式建模它们之间的条件概率分布。我们证明，该框架能实现单巡天分析之外的新科学能力，包括对缺失巡天观测的忠实概率预测和跨巡天罕见事件的探测。这些结果确立了跨巡天生成建模的可行性。因此，AS-Bridge有望成为未来LSST-Euclid联合数据处理流程的互补组件，在两项目数据可用后提升科学产出。数据与代码见https://github.com/ZHANG7DC/AS-Bridge。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to jointly analyze data from upcoming large-scale astronomical surveys like LSST and Euclid, which differ in observational characteristics, AS-Bridge introduces a bidirectional generative framework using a diffusion model based on a stochastic Brownian Bridge process. This model learns the conditional probability distribution between ground- and space-based observations in overlapping sky regions, enabling faithful probabilistic reconstruction of missing data and detection of rare events across surveys. Experimental results demonstrate that AS-Bridge successfully generates realistic observations from one survey conditioned on the other, validating its utility for enhancing scientific discovery in future joint data pipelines.
为应对即将开展的大型天文巡天项目（如LSST与Euclid）在观测模式、覆盖范围和数据特性上的差异，AS-Bridge提出一种双向生成框架，通过基于布朗桥过程的扩散模型实现地面与空间观测数据间的转换。利用二者重叠的天区，模型学习了二者间的条件概率分布，从而能准确预测缺失观测并跨巡天发现罕见事件。实验表明，该方法在单巡天分析之外提供了新的科学能力，验证了跨巡天生成建模的可行性，有望成为未来联合数据处理流程中的重要补充工具。
Toward Real-Time Sentence-Level Sign Language Translation
Authors: Thanh-Hoang Nguyen Doan
First: 2026-07-10T17:11:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T17:11:03+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most sign language understanding systems operate at the level of isolated signs, limiting their usefulness in natural communication. We study sentence-level sign language translation (SLT) with the primary goal of real-time deployment rather than proposing a new translation architecture. We fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 translation stack on a uniformly sampled 9,872-example subset of How2Sign, selected because of compute and storage constraints, using QLoRA while keeping SHuBERT frozen. The model obtains a validation BLEU of 16.7 and, on the test split, BLEU 15.9 and BLEURT 44.7. The main contribution is a hardware-aware streaming system: a Raspberry Pi 4B reference client provides camera capture, local text display, and speech output, while compute-intensive perception and translation run on a CPU/GPU backend. The capture protocol remains client-agnostic, so the same backend can serve a browser, phone, or laptop. Chunked ingestion, bounded queues, parallelized perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine reduce mean post-finalization response latency from 1.873 to 1.354 seconds (27.71%) and P95 latency from 2.919 to 2.130 seconds (27.03%) over the complete 9,872-example working subset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向实时句子级手语翻译的研究
大多数手语理解系统仅在孤立手势层面运行，限制了其在自然交流中的实用性。本研究聚焦于句子级手语翻译（SLT），主要目标是实现实时部署，而非提出新的翻译架构。我们在How2Sign数据集中选取了9,872个样本的均匀子集（受限于计算与存储资源），使用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5翻译栈，同时保持SHuBERT模型冻结。模型在验证集上获得BLEU分数16.7，在测试集上获得BLEU 15.9和BLEURT 44.7。主要贡献是一个硬件感知的流式系统：树莓派4B参考客户端负责摄像头捕获、本地文本显示和语音输出，而计算密集型的感知与翻译任务在CPU/GPU后端运行。捕获协议保持客户端无关性，因此同一后端可服务浏览器、手机或笔记本电脑。通过分块输入、有界队列、并行感知、时间重排序及句边界状态机，将平均最终响应延迟从1.873秒降至1.354秒（降低27.71%），P95延迟从2.919秒降至2.130秒（降低27.03%），覆盖完整9,872样本工作子集。

Motivated by the need for real-time, sentence-level sign language translation in natural communication, the authors fine-tune a SHuBERT-ByT5 stack on a subset of How2Sign using QLoRA while freezing SHuBERT, achieving BLEU scores of 16.7 on validation and 15.9 on test with BLEURT 44.7. They introduce a hardware-aware streaming system where a Raspberry Pi 4B handles camera input and output while intensive processing runs on a backend, enabling client-agnostic deployment. Through chunked ingestion, parallel perception, temporal reordering, and a sentence-boundary state machine, they reduce mean response latency by 27.71% and P95 latency by 27.03% across their 9,872-example dataset.
为实现自然交流中的实时句子级手语翻译，作者在How2Sign子集上使用QLoRA微调SHuBERT-ByT5模型（冻结SHuBERT），在验证集和测试集上分别获得BLEU 16.7和15.9，BLEURT 44.7。核心贡献是一个硬件感知的流式系统：Raspberry Pi 4B负责摄像头采集、本地显示和语音输出，而计算密集型感知与翻译任务由后端CPU/GPU处理，支持浏览器、手机或笔记本等客户端。通过分块输入、有界队列、并行感知、时间重排序和句子边界状态机，将平均响应延迟降低27.71%，P95延迟降低27.03%，覆盖全部9,872条样本。
RELISH: LLM REgression with a Latent Iterative State Head
Authors: Yiheng Su, Matthew Lease
First: 2026-04-01T17:50:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:59:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Third Conference on Language Modeling (COLM 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present RELISH (REgression with a Latent Iterative State Head), a novel, lightweight architecture designed for text regression with large language models. Rather than decoding numeric targets as text or aggregating multiple generated outputs, RELISH predicts scalar values directly from frozen LLM representations by iteratively refining a learned latent state through cross-attention over token-level representations, and then mapping the final state to a point estimate with a linear regressor. Across six datasets, four LLM backbones, and two LLM training regimes, RELISH consistently outperforms prior baselines from all three major LLM regression families, including autoregressive decoding, regression-aware inference, and existing predictive head methods. Despite these gains, RELISH remains highly parameter-efficient, requiring only $\sim$3.4-3.7M trainable parameters across frozen LLM backbones (only 0.01-0.04$\%$ additional overhead), far less than LoRA-based alternatives that grow with model size (0.26-0.42$\%$). Our code is available at https://github.com/SamSoup/RELISH.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RELISH：带隐式迭代状态头的LLM回归
我们提出了RELISH（带隐式迭代状态头的回归），一种专为大语言模型文本回归设计的轻量级新架构。与将数值目标解码为文本或聚合多个生成输出不同，RELISH通过跨注意力机制迭代优化从冻结LLM表示中学习到的隐状态，并最终通过线性回归器将最终状态映射为点估计，直接预测标量值。在六个数据集、四种LLM骨干模型和两种LLM训练范式下，RELISH在三大主流LLM回归方法（自回归解码、回归感知推理、现有预测头方法）的所有基线中均表现更优。尽管性能提升显著，RELISH仍保持高度参数效率，仅需约340万至370万可训练参数（仅占冻结LLM骨干的0.01%-0.04%额外开销），远低于随模型规模增长的LoRA类替代方案（0.26%-0.42%）。代码已开源：https://github.com/SamSoup/RELISH。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing LLM regression methods, RELISH introduces a lightweight architecture that directly predicts scalar values from frozen LLM representations using an iterative latent state refinement mechanism powered by cross-attention over token-level features, followed by a linear regressor. Evaluated across six datasets, four LLM backbones, and two training regimes, RELISH consistently outperforms baselines from autoregressive decoding, regression-aware inference, and predictive head approaches. Despite its superior performance, it remains highly parameter-efficient, requiring only 3.4–3.7M trainable parameters (0.01–0.04% overhead) compared to LoRA-based methods that scale with model size (0.26–0.42% overhead).
为解决现有大语言模型回归方法效率低的问题，RELISH 提出一种轻量架构，通过跨注意力机制迭代优化从冻结 LLM 表示中学习的潜在状态，再经线性回归器直接输出标量值。在六个数据集、四种 LLM 基座模型及两种训练范式下，RELISH 一致优于自回归解码、回归感知推理及现有预测头方法的基线模型。尽管性能更优，其参数效率极高，仅需约 340 万至 370 万可训练参数（额外开销 0.01–0.04%），远低于随模型规模增长的 LoRA 方法（额外开销 0.26–0.42%）。
Agora: Enhancing LLM Agent Reasoning Via Auction-Based Task Allocation
Authors: Kaiji Zhou, Ales Leonardis, Yue Feng
First: 2026-07-10T16:54:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:54:52+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 12 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents requires effective orchestration of diverse expert models and tools. However, existing frameworks typically call APIs based on coarse-grained matching between tasks and the functions of expert models or tools, while overlooking critical factors such as performance variability and cost efficiency among functionally similar alternatives. To address this, we propose Agora, a framework that introduces an incentive-compatible auction mechanism for dynamically allocating tasks to expert models and tools. By treating reasoning steps as tradeable items, Agora enables agents to bid based on their rectified competence-ensuring that critical logic is routed to the most capable solver rather than the most overconfident one. Evaluations across five benchmarks show that Agora improves over matched single-model, routing, and cascade baselines under comparable candidate pools, while exposing a controllable cost-quality trade-off through a single auction parameter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agora：通过基于拍卖的任务分配增强大语言模型代理推理能力
增强大语言模型（LLM）代理的推理能力，需要有效协调多样化的专家模型和工具。然而，现有框架通常基于任务与专家模型或工具功能之间的粗粒度匹配来调用API，而忽视了功能相似替代方案之间的性能波动和成本效率等关键因素。为解决此问题，我们提出Agora框架，引入一种激励相容的拍卖机制，动态分配任务给专家模型和工具。通过将推理步骤视为可交易物品，Agora使代理能够根据其修正后的能力进行竞标，确保关键逻辑被分配给最擅长的求解器，而非最自信的求解器。在五个基准测试中的评估表明，Agora在可比候选池下优于匹配单模型、路由和级联基线，同时通过单一拍卖参数实现可控的成本-质量权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve LLM agent reasoning through better orchestration of diverse expert models and tools, Agora introduces an auction-based task allocation mechanism that dynamically assigns reasoning steps to the most competent agents while accounting for performance variability and cost efficiency. The method treats reasoning steps as tradeable items and allows agents to bid based on their rectified competence, ensuring critical logic is routed to capable solvers rather than overconfident ones. Experimental results across five benchmarks demonstrate that Agora outperforms baseline methods including single-model, routing, and cascade approaches under comparable candidate pools, while offering a controllable cost-quality trade-off via a single auction parameter.
为提升大语言模型代理的推理能力，Agora提出一种基于拍卖机制的任务分配框架，将推理步骤视为可交易物品，根据模型的实际能力而非自信心进行动态分配，确保关键逻辑交由最胜任的求解器处理。实验在五个基准上表明，该框架在同等候选池下优于单模型、路由和级联基线方法，并可通过单一拍卖参数实现可控的成本与质量权衡。
Tokenizer Transplantation: Mitigating Autoregressive Collapse in Edge-Efficient Bengali ASR
Authors: Sanjid Hasan, Md. Abdur Rahman
Venue: ICML 2026 poster
First: 2026-07-10T16:54:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:54:38+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 2 figures. Accepted as a poster at the MusIML Workshop, ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Lightweight speech recognition models are critical for edge deployment, yet highly optimized architectures like Moonshine often fail on morphologically rich, non-Latin languages such as Bengali. This study identifies the root cause of this failure as the model's English-centric byte-level tokenizer, which fragments Bengali words into high-fertility byte chains and triggers catastrophic autoregressive collapse during inference. To resolve this, a novel vocabulary transplantation pipeline is proposed to replace the decoder vocabulary with the native-script BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resize the corresponding token embedding matrix. Experimental results demonstrate a reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30. By decreasing autoregressive sequence length by 85.8%, decoding instability is entirely mitigated. When evaluated on the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the modified architecture achieves a competitive 21.54% Word Error Rate (WER) and a Real-Time Factor (RTF) of 0.0053. Ultimately, this research provides a scalable, reproducible blueprint for cross-script adaptation of compact ASR models without the need for resource-intensive pre-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分词器移植：缓解孟加拉语边缘高效语音识别中的自回归崩溃
轻量级语音识别模型对边缘部署至关重要，但高度优化的架构（如Moonshine）在形态丰富的非拉丁语种（如孟加拉语）上常失效。本研究将失效根源归结为模型的英语中心字节级分词器，该分词器将孟加拉语单词切分为高生育率字节链，导致推理时发生灾难性自回归崩溃。为此，提出一种新颖的词汇移植流程，将解码器词汇表替换为原生文字脚本BanglaBERT WordPiece词汇表，并相应调整词嵌入矩阵。实验结果表明，词生育率从9.16降至1.30。通过将自回归序列长度减少85.8%，解码不稳定性被完全消除。在882小时Lipi-Ghor数据集上的评估显示，改进架构达到21.54%的词错误率（WER）和0.0053的实时因子（RTF）。本研究最终提供了一种可扩展、可复现的紧凑ASR模型跨文字脚本适配方案，无需资源密集型预训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of lightweight ASR models like Moonshine on morphologically rich non-Latin languages such as Bengali, this study identifies an English-centric byte-level tokenizer as the root cause of autoregressive collapse during inference. The proposed method replaces the decoder vocabulary with a native BanglaBERT WordPiece vocabulary and resizes the token embedding matrix, effectively transplanting the tokenizer. Experiments show a significant reduction in token fertility from 9.16 to 1.30, cutting autoregressive sequence length by 85.8% and eliminating decoding instability. On the 882-hour Lipi-Ghor dataset, the adapted model achieves a 21.54% WER and RTF of 0.0053, demonstrating a scalable and reproducible approach for cross-script adaptation without costly pre-training.
该研究针对轻量级ASR模型（如Moonshine）在形态丰富的非拉丁语种（如孟加拉语）上失效的问题，指出其根本原因是英文中心的字节级分词器将孟加拉语词汇切分为高生育率字节链，导致解码时出现自回归崩溃。研究提出一种词汇移植方案，将本地孟加拉语WordPiece词汇替换解码器词汇表并调整嵌入矩阵，使词汇生育率从9.16降至1.30，自回归序列长度减少85.8%，彻底消除解码不稳定。在Lipi-Ghor数据集上，改进模型实现21.54%的词错误率和0.0053的实时因子，提供了一种无需重训练即可跨文字系统适配的可扩展方法。
Lost in Backpropagation: The LM Head is a Gradient Bottleneck
Authors: Nathan Godey, Yoav Artzi
First: 2026-03-10T18:27:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:51:04+00:00
Comments: To be presented at COLM'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The last layer of neural language models (LMs) projects output features of dimension $D$ to logits in dimension $V$, the size of the vocabulary, where usually $D \ll V$. This mismatch is known to raise risks of limited expressivity in neural LMs, creating a so-called softmax bottleneck. We show the softmax bottleneck is not only an expressivity bottleneck but also an optimization bottleneck. Backpropagating $V$-dimensional gradients through a rank-$D$ linear layer induces unavoidable compression, which alters the training feedback provided to the vast majority of the parameters. We present a theoretical analysis of this phenomenon and measure empirically that 95-99% of the gradient norm is suppressed by the output layer, resulting in vastly suboptimal update directions. We conduct controlled pretraining experiments showing that the gradient bottleneck makes trivial patterns unlearnable, and drastically affects the training dynamics of LLMs. We argue that this inherent flaw contributes to training inefficiencies at scale independently of the model architecture, and raises the need for new LM head designs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迷失在反向传播中：语言模型头部是梯度瓶颈
神经语言模型（LMs）的最后一层将维度为 $D$ 的输出特征投影到维度为 $V$（词汇表大小）的对数几率中，通常 $D \ll V$。这种不匹配已知会引发神经LM表达能力受限的风险，形成所谓的softmax瓶颈。我们证明，softmax瓶颈不仅是表达能力瓶颈，也是优化瓶颈。通过秩为 $D$ 的线性层反向传播 $V$ 维梯度会不可避免地造成压缩，从而改变提供给绝大多数参数的训练反馈。我们对该现象进行了理论分析，并实证测量发现，输出层会抑制95-99%的梯度范数，导致更新方向严重次优。我们通过受控的预训练实验表明，梯度瓶颈使简单模式无法学习，并显著影响大语言模型的训练动态。我们认为，这一固有缺陷独立于模型架构，导致大规模训练效率低下，亟需新的LM头部设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that the final layer of language models projects high-dimensional features to a vastly larger vocabulary space, creating a gradient compression issue, the paper investigates how this softmax bottleneck hinders both expressivity and optimization. Through theoretical analysis and empirical experiments, it demonstrates that backpropagating gradients through a low-rank linear layer suppresses 95–99% of the gradient norm, leading to suboptimal parameter updates. Controlled pretraining experiments reveal that this gradient bottleneck renders trivial patterns unlearnable and severely distorts training dynamics in large language models, suggesting that this architectural flaw independently limits training efficiency regardless of model design.
该研究发现语言模型最后一层将高维梯度压缩至稀疏词汇空间，造成优化瓶颈。作者分析表明，通过低秩线性层反向传播梯度会大幅抑制梯度范数，95-99%的梯度信号被丢失，导致参数更新方向严重偏离最优。控制预训练实验显示，该梯度瓶颈使模型无法学习简单模式，严重扭曲训练动态，且该问题与模型架构无关，从根本上限制了大规模训练效率。
HiPO: Hierarchical Preference Optimization for Adaptive Reasoning in LLMs
Authors: Darsh Kachroo, Arjun Prasaath Anbazhagan, Adriana Caraeni, Brennan Lagasse, Kevin Zhu
First: 2026-04-22T03:08:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:49:35+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables. Includes ablation study across Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on 5 math reasoning benchmarks (GSM8K, MATH500, Minerva, AIME24, Gaokao2023). GPT-4.1 used for structured evaluation of reasoning quality
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is an effective framework for aligning large language models with human preferences, but it struggles with complex reasoning tasks. DPO optimizes for the likelihood of generating preferred over dispreferred responses in their entirety and lacks the granularity to provide feedback on subsections of many-step solutions typical of reasoning tasks. Existing methods excel at either stable preference learning (e.g., DPO variants like KTO and RSO) or structured reasoning (e.g., ReMA's multi-agent RL framework, Tree of Thoughts), but fail to merge these complementary strengths. We propose HiPO (Hierarchical Preference Optimization), an extension of DPO that separates responses into reasoning segments (query clarification and context, reasoning steps, and answer) and computes loss as a weighted sum of the DPO loss for each segment. Our approach enables segment-specific training while maintaining DPO's computational efficiency and training stability. We demonstrate that for multiple 7B LLMs fine-tuned using HiPO and DPO on the Math Stack Exchange preference dataset, the models trained with HiPO outperform the others on a variety of common math benchmarks and achieve greater organization, logical flow, and consistency as measured by GPT-4.1.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiPO：用于大语言模型自适应推理的分层偏好优化
直接偏好优化（DPO）是使大语言模型与人类偏好对齐的有效框架，但在复杂推理任务中表现不佳。DPO优化的是生成偏好响应与非偏好响应的整体似然，缺乏对多步骤推理任务中各子步骤提供反馈的粒度。现有方法擅长稳定偏好学习（如KTO和RSO等DPO变体）或结构化推理（如ReMA的多智能体强化学习框架、思维树），但未能融合这些互补优势。我们提出HiPO（分层偏好优化），作为DPO的扩展，将响应划分为推理段落（查询澄清与上下文、推理步骤、答案），并以各段落的DPO损失加权和作为总损失。该方法支持分段训练，同时保持DPO的计算效率和训练稳定性。我们在Math Stack Exchange偏好数据集上对多个7B规模LLM进行HiPO与DPO微调，结果表明HiPO训练的模型在多种常见数学基准测试中表现更优，且在GPT-4.1评估下展现出更佳的结构化、逻辑连贯性和一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) in handling multi-step reasoning tasks, the authors propose HiPO, a hierarchical extension that splits model responses into reasoning segments—query clarification, reasoning steps, and answer—and optimizes each segment with weighted DPO losses. This allows for granular feedback while preserving DPO’s efficiency and stability. Experiments on 7B LLMs fine-tuned on Math Stack Exchange preferences show HiPO consistently outperforms DPO and other baselines across five math benchmarks, with models exhibiting improved logical flow, consistency, and structure as evaluated by GPT-4.1.
为解决DPO在复杂推理任务中的粒度不足问题，作者提出HiPO方法，将模型输出按查询澄清、推理步骤和答案三个层级拆分，对各层级分别计算加权DPO损失，实现精细化训练而不牺牲DPO的稳定性和效率。在Math Stack Exchange偏好数据集上对多个7B模型进行微调后，HiPO在五个数学推理基准上均优于DPO及其他基线方法，且经GPT-4.1评估，其推理过程更具逻辑性、结构性和一致性。
SpikeATac: A Multimodal Tactile Finger with Taxelized Dynamic Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Eric T. Chang, Peter Ballentine, Zhanpeng He, Do-Gon Kim, Kai Jiang, Hua-Hsuan Liang, Joaquin Palacios, William Wang, Pedro Piacenza, Ioannis Kymissis, Matei Ciocarlie
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-10-30T23:33:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:45:48+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures, ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we introduce SpikeATac, a multimodal tactile finger combining a taxelized and highly sensitive dynamic response (PVDF) with a static transduction method (capacitive) for multimodal touch sensing. Named for its `spiky' response, SpikeATac's 16-taxel PVDF film sampled at 4 kHz provides fast, sensitive dynamic signals to the very onset and breaking of contact. We characterize the sensitivity of the different modalities, and show that SpikeATac provides the ability to stop quickly and delicately when grasping fragile, deformable objects. Beyond parallel grasping, we show that SpikeATac can be used in a learning-based framework to achieve new capabilities on a dexterous multifingered robot hand. We use reinforcement learning from human feedback to fine-tune the behavior of a policy to modulate force. Our hardware platform and learning pipeline together enable a difficult dexterous and contact-rich task that has not previously been achieved: in-hand manipulation of fragile objects. Videos are available at https://roamlab.github.io/spikeatac/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpikeATac：一种用于灵巧操作的多模态触觉手指，具备网格化动态传感
本文介绍了SpikeATac，一种结合网格化高灵敏动态响应（PVDF）与静态转换方法（电容式）的多模态触觉手指。因其‘尖刺’式响应而得名，SpikeATac的16网格PVDF薄膜以4 kHz采样率提供快速、灵敏的动态信号，用于接触起始与脱离的瞬间。我们表征了不同模态的灵敏度，并表明SpikeATac在抓取易碎、可变形物体时能够快速而精细地停止。除平行抓取外，我们还展示了SpikeATac可在基于学习的框架中实现灵巧多指机器人手的新能力。我们通过人类反馈的强化学习微调策略，以调节力的大小。该硬件平台与学习流程共同实现了此前未曾达成的复杂灵巧且接触密集的任务：在手中操作易碎物体。视频见https://roamlab.github.io/spikeatac/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity tactile sensing in dexterous manipulation, SpikeATac integrates a taxelized PVDF film for fast dynamic sensing with a capacitive sensor for static feedback, enabling multimodal touch perception. The 16-taxel PVDF array, sampled at 4 kHz, captures rapid contact onset and break events with high sensitivity, while the capacitive sensor provides stable pressure readings. Experimental results demonstrate SpikeATac’s ability to delicately stop during grasping of fragile, deformable objects and, when combined with reinforcement learning from human feedback, enables in-hand manipulation—a previously unachieved task on a dexterous robot hand.
为实现灵巧操作所需的高保真触觉反馈，本研究提出SpikeATac，一种融合税格化PVDF动态传感与电容式静态传感的多模态触觉手指。16税格PVDF阵列以4kHz采样率实现接触起始与脱离的快速精准检测，电容传感器则捕捉持续压力。实验表明，该装置可精细控制抓取脆弱可变形物体时的停止动作，并结合人类反馈强化学习框架调节力控策略，实现此前机器人手无法完成的物体在手中操作任务。
PAC-ACT: Post-training Actor-Critic for Action Chunking Transformers
Authors: Yujie Pang, Zudong Li
First: 2026-07-10T16:42:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:42:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Precision industrial contact manipulation requires reliable robot policies under pose perturbations and contact-force constraints. Vision-language-action models offer broad generalization but often introduce high inference latency and GPU-memory cost, while vision-action chunking policies are more suitable for real-time industrial control. However, these policies are usually trained by behavior cloning and suffer from distribution shift in contact-rich tasks. This paper proposes PAC-ACT, a reinforcement-learning post-training framework for pretrained Action Chunking Transformer policies. PAC-ACT reformulates policy optimization at the chunk level, constructs an ACT-transferred actor-critic architecture, and introduces a hybrid behavior-prior constraint to preserve the pretrained action distribution during online fine-tuning. Experiments on industrial precision-contact benchmarks show that PAC-ACT improves task success, contact stability, and force safety while retaining low latency and low GPU-memory usage. On the Contour task, PAC-ACT significantly reduces peak contact force and decreases the proportion of force readings above 60 N by 46 times. Sparse-reward ablations further show that the proposed behavior-prior constraint enables effective exploration under randomized initial poses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PAC-ACT：用于动作分块Transformer的后训练演员-评论家方法
高精度工业接触操作需要在姿态扰动和接触力约束下可靠的机器人策略。视觉-语言-动作模型具有广泛泛化能力，但常伴随高推理延迟和GPU内存开销；而视觉-动作分块策略更适合实时工业控制。然而，这些策略通常通过行为克隆训练，在接触密集任务中易受分布偏移影响。本文提出PAC-ACT，一种针对预训练动作分块Transformer策略的强化学习后训练框架。PAC-ACT在分块层面重构策略优化，构建ACT迁移的演员-评论家架构，并引入混合行为先验约束，在在线微调中保持预训练动作分布。在工业高精度接触基准测试中，PAC-ACT提升任务成功率、接触稳定性与力安全性，同时保持低延迟和低GPU内存占用。在轮廓任务中，PAC-ACT显著降低峰值接触力，并使超过60N的力读数比例降低46倍。稀疏奖励消融实验进一步表明，所提行为先验约束可在随机初始姿态下实现有效探索。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable robot control in precision industrial contact tasks under pose perturbations and force constraints, PAC-ACT introduces a reinforcement learning post-training framework for pretrained Action Chunking Transformer policies. It reformulates policy optimization at the action chunk level, builds an actor-critic architecture adapted from the ACT model, and incorporates a hybrid behavior-prior constraint to maintain the pretrained action distribution during online fine-tuning. Experimental results on industrial benchmarks demonstrate that PAC-ACT enhances task success rate, contact stability, and force safety while preserving low latency and memory usage; on the Contour task, it reduces peak contact force and cuts the proportion of force readings exceeding 60 N by 46 times, with ablation studies confirming the behavior-prior constraint enables effective exploration under randomized initial poses.
为提升机器人在工业精密接触任务中面对姿态扰动和力约束时的可靠性，本文提出PAC-ACT框架，对预训练的动作分块Transformer进行强化学习后训练。该方法在动作分块层面重构策略优化，构建了从预训练ACT迁移而来的actor-critic架构，并引入混合行为先验约束以在在线微调中保持预训练动作分布。实验表明，该方法在工业基准上显著提升任务成功率、接触稳定性与力安全性，同时保持低延迟和低显存；在Contour任务中，峰值接触力降低，超过60N的力读数减少46倍，消融实验验证行为先验约束在随机初始姿态下可有效促进探索。
A Fourier analytique approach to Gaussian mixture learning
Authors: Somnath Chakraborty, Hariharan Narayanan
Venue: Ind.Appl.Math.Springer (2025) 149-181
First: 2020-04-13T08:06:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:40:11+00:00
Comments: Almost the same as the published version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Suppose that we are given independent, identically distributed random samples $x_1,\cdots,x_n$ from a mixture at most $k$ many $d$-dimensional spherical Gaussian distributions $μ_1,\cdots,μ_{k_0}$ of identical and known variance $σ^2$ in each coordinate, such that the minimum $\ell^2$ distance between two distinct centers $y_l$ and $y_j$ is greater than $2Δσ\min\{\sqrt{d},\sqrt k\}$, where $Δ>C_0$, and $C_0$ is a sufficiently large universal constant. We develop a randomized algorithm that learns the centers $y_l$'s of the Gaussian components to within an $\ell^2$ distance of $k^{-\tilde C_0}$ -- in presence of arbitrarily large number of components and in arbitrary dimension, when the weights are known to be uniform. Furthermore, if the number of components is $k= Ω(2^d)$, then for arbitrary universal constant $c>0$, even for unknown weights, the algorithm learns the centers to within an $\ell^2$ distance of $d^{-\tilde C_0}$ and the weights up to an accuracy of $cw_{min}$, with probability greater than $1 - \exp(-k/c)$, provided that the weights lie in $[c/k,1/ck]$, and the minimum separation is just $2c\sqrt d$. The number of samples and the computational time is bounded above by $\mathrm{poly}(k, d)$ in either case. Such a bound on the sample and computational complexity was previously unknown in the regime of non-constant dimension, and in particular, when $d$ is not $O(1)$. When $d = O(1)$, this complexity bound follows from work of Regev and Vijayaraghavan, where it has also been shown that the sample complexity of learning a random mixture of Gaussians in a ball of radius $o(\sqrt{d})$ in $d$ dimensions, when $d$ is $Θ( \log k)$, is at least super-polynomial in $k, d$, showing that our result is tight in this case.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种基于傅里叶分析的高斯混合模型学习方法
假设我们获得独立同分布的随机样本 $x_1,\cdots,x_n$，这些样本来自至多 $k$ 个 $d$ 维球形高斯分布 $μ_1,\cdots,μ_{k_0}$ 的混合，每个坐标具有相同的已知方差 $σ^2$，且任意两个不同中心 $y_l$ 和 $y_j$ 之间的最小 $\ell^2$ 距离大于 $2Δσ\min\{\sqrt{d},\sqrt k\}$，其中 $Δ>C_0$，$C_0$ 为足够大的通用常数。我们开发了一种随机算法，能够在已知权重为均匀分布的情况下，将高斯分量中心 $y_l$ 的学习误差控制在 $\ell^2$ 距离 $k^{-\tilde C_0}$ 以内——即使在分量数量任意大、维度任意高的情况下。进一步地，若分量数量 $k=Ω(2^d)$，则对任意通用常数 $c>0$，即使权重未知，算法也能以概率大于 $1 - \exp(-k/c)$，将中心学习误差控制在 $d^{-\tilde C_0}$ 以内，权重学习精度达到 $cw_{min}$，前提是权重位于 $[c/k,1/ck]$ 区间内，且最小分离距离仅为 $2c\sqrt d$。两种情况下，样本数量和计算时间均被上界限制为 $\mathrm{poly}(k, d)$。此前在非恒定维度（特别是 $d$ 不是 $O(1)$）的情形下，此类样本与计算复杂度的上界尚属未知。当 $d = O(1)$ 时，该复杂度界可由 Regev 和 Vijayaraghavan 的工作推导得出，其中亦证明：在 $d$ 维空间中，若 $d=Θ(\log k)$，学习半径为 $o(\sqrt{d})$ 的球内随机高斯混合模型的样本复杂度至少为 $k, d$ 的超多项式级，表明本结果在此情形下是紧的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning Gaussian mixture models in high dimensions with unknown or unknown weights, this paper proposes a randomized algorithm based on Fourier analysis to recover the centers and weights of spherical Gaussians with identical known variance. The method leverages the Fourier transform to exploit structure in the data, achieving $\ell^2$-accurate center recovery within $k^{-\tilde C_0}$ for known uniform weights, even when the number of components $k$ and dimension $d$ are arbitrary and large. When $k = Ω(2^d)$, the algorithm also estimates weights up to $cw_{min}$ accuracy under mild separation and weight constraints, with sample and computational complexity bounded by $\mathrm{poly}(k, d)$. This represents the first such complexity bound for non-constant dimensions, and is tight when $d = Θ(\log k)$, as shown by prior work on lower bounds for random mixtures.
为解决高维空间下球形高斯混合模型中心与权重学习问题，本文提出一种基于傅里叶分析的随机算法。该方法利用傅里叶变换结构，当权重均匀且中心间分离条件满足时，可在 $k^{-\tilde C_0}$ 范数误差内恢复中心；当分量数为 $\Omega(2^d)$ 时，即使权重未知，也能在较弱分离条件下以 $cw_{min}$ 精度估计权重，样本与计算复杂度均为 $\mathrm{poly}(k,d)$。该结果首次在非常数维度下实现，且当 $d = \Theta(\log k)$ 时，复杂度下界已由前人工作证明，故本方法在该情形下最优。
CoDiMAD: Diffusion-Based Privileged Distillation for Communication-Free Multi-Robot Coordination
Authors: Jiyue Tao, Shunheng Xin, Tongsheng Shen, Dexin Zhao, Feitian Zhang
First: 2026-07-10T16:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:36:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decentralized multi-robot coordination under partial observability remains challenging, especially in communication-free settings where agents must act solely from local sensor observations. Privileged policy distillation provides a promising approach by transferring knowledge from a globally informed oracle to sensor-constrained students. However, in multi-agent systems, the same local observation may correspond to multiple global configurations requiring qualitatively different cooperative actions, making the conditional action distribution inherently multi-modal. Standard deterministic distillation collapses these modes to their mean, often yielding invalid or hesitant actions. To address this issue, we propose CoDiMAD, a three-stage framework that trains a privileged oracle with MAPPO, constructs an offline dataset of local-observation-oracle-action pairs, and distills the oracle into decentralized students parameterized as conditional denoising diffusion probabilistic models. By approximating the conditional oracle-action distribution through the diffusion reverse process, CoDiMAD samples decisive actions from coherent coordination modes rather than averaging across them. Theoretical analysis characterizes the mode-averaging failure of deterministic distillation and the distributional recovery property of diffusion-based distillation. Experiments on three cooperative tasks show that CoDiMAD consistently outperforms direct local MARL and deterministic distillation baselines. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoDiMAD：基于扩散的特权蒸馏用于无通信多机器人协调
在部分可观测条件下实现去中心化多机器人协调仍具挑战性，尤其是在无通信环境中，代理必须仅依据局部传感器观测进行行动。特权策略蒸馏通过将全局信息丰富的 oracle 的知识迁移到受限于传感器的学生代理，提供了一种有前景的方法。然而，在多智能体系统中，相同的局部观测可能对应多个全局配置，需要定性不同的协作动作，使得条件动作分布本质上是多模态的。标准确定性蒸馏将这些模式坍缩至均值，常导致无效或犹豫不决的动作。为解决此问题，我们提出 CoDiMAD，一个三阶段框架：首先用 MAPPO 训练特权 oracle，构建包含局部观测- oracle-动作对的离线数据集，然后将 oracle 蒸馏至参数化为条件去噪扩散概率模型的去中心化学生代理。通过扩散逆过程近似条件 oracle-动作分布，CoDiMAD 从一致的协调模式中采样决断性动作，而非在模式间平均。理论分析刻画了确定性蒸馏的模式平均失败性及基于扩散的蒸馏的分布恢复特性。在三个协作任务上的实验表明，CoDiMAD 一致优于直接局部 MARL 和确定性蒸馏基线。论文被接受后，源代码将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of coordinating multiple robots without communication under partial observability, this paper introduces CoDiMAD, a diffusion-based privileged distillation framework that addresses the multi-modal nature of cooperative actions. It trains a globally informed oracle using MAPPO, constructs an offline dataset of local-observation-oracle-action pairs, and distills the oracle into decentralized students modeled as conditional denoising diffusion probabilistic models. By sampling actions via the diffusion reverse process, CoDiMAD avoids collapsing multi-modal distributions into a single mean, instead generating decisive actions aligned with coherent coordination modes. Experimental results across three cooperative tasks demonstrate consistent performance superiority over direct local MARL and deterministic distillation baselines.
为解决在通信受限环境下多机器人部分可观测时的协调难题，本文提出CoDiMAD框架，通过扩散模型实现从全局信息源到局部感知代理的知识蒸馏。该方法先用MAPPO训练全局信息源，收集局部观测-源动作数据集，再将源蒸馏至由条件去噪扩散概率模型参数化的分布式学生代理。通过扩散逆过程采样动作，CoDiMAD能保留多模态协调模式而非平均化，避免无效或犹豫行为。在三个协作任务实验中，CoDiMAD持续优于直接本地多智能体强化学习及确定性蒸馏基线方法。
TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework (ARC): Risk-Tiering Internally Created Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Hannah M. Liu, Rhea Saxena, Shiv Asthana
First: 2026-07-10T16:36:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:36:44+00:00
Comments: This is a working paper on our risk classification tool, with iterations currently underway
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The proliferation of agentic AI systems across enterprise and public-sector contexts has outpaced the capacity of general-purpose AI risk frameworks to classify and govern them. In this paper, we introduce the TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework, a structured, repeatable instrument that can be applied to seven types of agentic AI systems and is grounded in foundational pre-existing AI governance frameworks. At the core of the framework is a twelve-dimension scoring rubric that robustly quantifies the risk. This rubric is combined with other components, such as the GPA + IAT classification model and the five-level autonomy framework derived from existing literature. These inputs produce a three-tier governance output with mapped control recommendations. A specialised Coding Assistant extension is also included to account for nuances specific to this type of agentic AI system. We then use an illustrative example to show our framework in practice. ARC is intended for AI governance practitioners, risk officers, developers, and regulators, and it will regularly undergo iteration as we continue to expand it and make it more robust. The community can access the interactive framework here: https://arc.responsible.ai/
中文标题/摘要

Driven by the need to govern rapidly proliferating agentic AI systems beyond the scope of general-purpose risk frameworks, this paper introduces the TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework (ARC), a structured tool designed for seven types of agentic AI. A Coding Assistant extension addresses system-specific nuances. KnitID: Machine-Knitted RFID Antennas for Battery-Free Authentication, Localization and Interaction
Authors: Weiye Xu, Yue Xu, Devin Murphy, Sen Zhang, Te-yen Wu, Yiyue Luo
First: 2026-07-10T16:34:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:34:26+00:00
Comments: 2-pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Battery-free RFID systems offer a scalable and maintenance-free approach to interaction. We present KnitID, a machine-knitted textile RFID antenna design that enables on-body authentication, localization, and interaction. Unlike prior antenna designs, KnitID achieves a compact antenna form factor (60mm by 8mm) by integrating magnet wire into the unique loop-over-loop structure of machine knitting. This structure reduces the size of conventional loop antennas by around 90\%, while also providing 30\% longer sensing ranges than standard dipole designs with similar size on the human body. The compact form factor creates new opportunities to embed multiple RFID tags across the human body, enriching backscatter signals and supporting a broader range of battery-free on-body interactions. To demonstrate this capability, we build an interactive sleeve to support wearer authentication, spatial localization, and interaction detection. Through technical evaluations, we show the feasibility of KnitID to provide diverse and battery-free interactions on knitted user interfaces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KnitID：用于无电池身份验证、定位与交互的机织RFID天线
无电池RFID系统提供了一种可扩展且无需维护的交互方式。我们提出了KnitID，一种机织纺织RFID天线设计，支持体表身份验证、定位与交互。与以往天线设计不同，KnitID通过将磁线集成到机织特有的“环套环”结构中，实现了紧凑的天线外形（60mm×8mm），使传统环形天线尺寸缩小约90%，同时在人体上比同类尺寸的标准偶极天线传感距离长30%。紧凑的外形创造了在人体多处嵌入多个RFID标签的新机会，增强回波信号并支持更广泛的无电池体表交互。为展示该能力，我们构建了一款互动袖套，支持穿戴者身份验证、空间定位与交互检测。通过技术评估，我们证明了KnitID可在针织用户界面中提供多样化的无电池交互。

This design also delivers 30% longer sensing ranges than standard dipole antennas of similar size on the human body. Experimental results demonstrate that KnitID enables embedding multiple tags across the body, enhancing backscatter signals and supporting authentication, localization, and interaction detection via an interactive sleeve prototype.

Promptable Concept Segmentation from Above: Evaluating SAM 3's Zero-Shot and One-Shot Capabilities in Remote Sensing
Authors: Mohammad Dabaja, Turgay Celik
First: 2026-07-10T16:33:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:33:05+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The deployment of large-scale foundation models, such as the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3), promises a transition toward open-vocabulary, training-free computer vision. However, their capacity to generalize out-of-distribution to the complex, top-down geometric structures of Earth Observation imagery remains largely unquantified. Driven by SAM 3's performance disparities in highly specialized domains, we present a comprehensive, multi-task empirical evaluation across remote sensing scene classification, object detection, and instance segmentation under strict zero-shot and one-shot constraints. To achieve this, we introduce a structural adaptation of SAM 3 by repurposing its decoupled binary presence head into a standalone zero-shot classifier. Furthermore, by systematically isolating textual and visual prompt modalities across five configurations, we explicitly diagnose the alignment mechanics within the model's multimodal decoder. Our findings reveal severe cross-modal interference: while visual prompts successfully align the decoder to complex remote sensing geometry, textual prompts inject misaligned, ground-level semantic bias, actively degrading coordinate regression. To benchmark these capabilities without resource-intensive training, we formulate a novel training-free proxy evaluation protocol for Generalized Zero-Shot tasks (scene classification and instance segmentation). Ultimately, our results demonstrate that SAM 3 avoids the overfitting commonly seen in legacy domain-adapted models, achieving high Harmonic Mean scores in segmentation tasks. However, it remains fundamentally constrained by sub-pixel resolution limits and overhead semantic blind spots, charting a definitive mandate for parameter-efficient geospatial fine-tuning of its multimodal decoder.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从上往下提示概念分割：评估SAM 3在遥感中的零样本与单样本能力
大规模基础模型（如Segment Anything Model 3，SAM 3）的部署预示着向开放词汇、无需训练的计算机视觉转型。然而，其在遥感图像复杂、自上而下的几何结构上的分布外泛化能力尚未被充分量化。鉴于SAM 3在高度专业化领域中的性能差异，我们对其在遥感场景分类、目标检测和实例分割任务中进行了全面的多任务实证评估，严格限定于零样本和单样本条件。为此，我们对SAM 3进行了结构化适配，将其解耦的二元存在头重新设计为独立的零样本分类器。此外，通过在五种配置中系统性隔离文本与视觉提示模态，我们明确诊断了模型多模态解码器内部的对齐机制。研究发现存在严重的跨模态干扰：视觉提示能有效对齐解码器以适应复杂的遥感几何结构，而文本提示则注入了与地面语义不符的偏差，主动降低坐标回归性能。为避免资源密集型训练，我们设计了一种全新的无训练代理评估协议，用于评估广义零样本任务（场景分类与实例分割）。最终结果表明，SAM 3避免了传统领域自适应模型常见的过拟合问题，在分割任务中取得了高调和均值得分。然而，其仍受亚像素分辨率限制和高层语义盲区的根本制约，明确指出了其多模态解码器参数高效地理空间微调的必要性。

Motivated by the need to evaluate SAM 3’s generalization to remote sensing imagery without training, this study assesses its zero-shot and one-shot performance across scene classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. The authors adapt SAM 3 by converting its binary presence head into a standalone zero-shot classifier and isolate textual and visual prompts across five configurations to diagnose multimodal alignment. A novel training-free proxy evaluation protocol is introduced to benchmark these capabilities, revealing that SAM 3 avoids overfitting and achieves high Harmonic Mean scores in segmentation, yet remains limited by sub-pixel resolution and semantic blind spots, necessitating parameter-efficient fine-tuning.
为评估SAM 3在遥感图像中零样本和一样本场景下的泛化能力，本研究在场景分类、目标检测和实例分割任务中进行系统性实验。方法通过将SAM 3的二元存在头改造为独立分类器，并在五种配置中隔离文本与视觉提示以诊断多模态对齐机制。结果表明，视觉提示能有效对齐遥感几何结构，而文本提示则引入地面语义偏差，损害坐标回归。提出的无训练代理评估协议显示，SAM 3避免了传统领域适配模型的过拟合，分割任务中达到高调和均值，但仍受限于亚像素分辨率和语义盲区，需进行参数高效的地理空间解码器微调。
Entropy-Constrained Machine Learning with Residual Data Augmentation for Modeling Chemical Kinetics
Authors: Okezzi Ukorigho, Opeoluwa Owoyele
First: 2026-07-10T16:31:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:31:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a physics-constrained machine learning framework for accelerating the direct numerical simulation (DNS) of turbulent reacting flows. The model replaces the direct evaluation of detailed chemical source terms with a surrogate that predicts reaction rates from a reduced thermochemical state. To improve physical consistency, the second law of thermodynamics is incorporated as a training constraint by enforcing non-negative entropy generation, which restricts the evolution of the thermochemical state to physically admissible directions and improves stability during time integration. The approach is demonstrated on DNS of a two-dimensional planar lean premixed methane-air flame interacting with a turbulent flow field. The model reproduces detailed-chemistry results with high fidelity while achieving more than an order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost. Furthermore, a residual-based synthetic data augmentation strategy enables parametric exploration by constructing new training data from the original dataset, allowing accurate simulation at new inlet conditions without additional detailed-chemistry CFD runs. These results demonstrate that thermodynamically constrained machine learning can provide reliable and computationally efficient surrogates for detailed chemistry in high-fidelity combustion simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于残差数据增强与熵约束的机器学习方法用于化学动力学建模
我们提出了一种受物理约束的机器学习框架，旨在加速湍流燃烧流动的直接数值模拟（DNS）。该模型用一个代理模型替代直接计算详细的化学源项，该代理模型根据简化热化学状态预测反应速率。为提高物理一致性，通过强制熵生成非负，将热力学第二定律作为训练约束，从而限制热化学状态的演化方向为物理上可接受的方向，并提升时间积分过程中的稳定性。该方法在二维平面甲烷-空气稀薄预混火焰与湍流场相互作用的DNS中得到验证，模型在保持高保真度的同时，计算成本降低超过一个数量级。此外，基于残差的合成数据增强策略可构建新训练数据，实现参数探索，无需额外的详细化学CFD计算即可准确模拟新入口条件下的流动。这些结果表明，受热力学约束的机器学习可为高保真燃烧模拟中的详细化学提供可靠且计算高效的代理模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of detailed chemical kinetics in turbulent combustion simulations, this study introduces a physics-constrained machine learning framework that replaces direct chemical source term evaluation with a surrogate model predicting reaction rates from a reduced thermochemical state. The method enforces the second law of thermodynamics by constraining entropy generation to be non-negative during training, ensuring physically admissible state evolution and numerical stability. Tested on a two-dimensional lean premixed methane-air flame in turbulent flow, the model achieves high-fidelity reproduction of detailed chemistry while reducing computational cost by over an order of magnitude. Additionally, a residual-based data augmentation technique generates synthetic training data to enable accurate predictions under new inlet conditions without re-running detailed chemistry simulations.
为降低湍流燃烧模拟中详细化学动力学的计算成本，该研究提出一种物理约束的机器学习框架，用简化热化学状态预测反应速率的代理模型替代直接计算化学源项。方法在训练中强制熵生成非负，以确保热化学状态演化符合热力学第二定律，提升数值稳定性。在二维贫燃甲烷-空气火焰与湍流相互作用的直接数值模拟中，模型高保真复现了详细化学结果，计算成本降低一个数量级以上。此外，基于残差的合成数据增强策略可生成新训练数据，使模型在无需重新运行详细化学计算的情况下，准确预测新入口条件下的流动行为。
Wan-Dancer: A Hierarchical Framework for Minute-scale Coherent Music-to-Dance Generation
Authors: Mingyang Huang, Peng Zhang, Li Hu, Guangyuan Wang, Bang Zhang
First: 2026-07-10T16:30:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:30:29+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 13 figures, project: https://github.com/Wan-Video/Wan-Dancer
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generating long-duration, high-definition, and rhythmically synchronized dance videos directly from music remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the temporal constraints of current diffusion models, which typically fail beyond 20 seconds. Existing approaches, whether they rely on intermediate 3D skeletons or on end-to-end video synthesis, suffer from temporal drift, identity inconsistency, and repetitive motion patterns when extended to longer horizons. To address these limitations, we propose a novel hierarchical framework for minute-scale coherent music-to-dance generation. Our method decouples the process into global keyframe planning and local temporal refinement, leveraging full-track musical context to ensure long-range coherence. Key innovations include dynamic frame rate adaptation via time-mapped RoPE embeddings for precise alignment, an optical-flow-based loss function to enhance motion continuity, and motion-speed control to preserve high-fidelity details during rapid movements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework surpasses the conventional duration barrier, generating stable, 720p/30fps videos exceeding one minute with superior temporal stability. Furthermore, the model exhibits robust versatility across five distinct dance genres, conditioned on both audio and textual prompts, establishing a new state-of-the-art in coherent, long-form dance video synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wan-Dancer：面向分钟级连贯音乐到舞蹈生成的层次化框架
直接从音乐生成长时长、高清晰度且节奏同步的舞蹈视频仍是一个重大挑战，主要受限于当前扩散模型的时间约束，通常无法超过20秒。现有方法，无论是依赖中间3D骨架还是端到端视频合成，当扩展至更长时长时均存在时间漂移、身份不一致和重复运动模式等问题。为解决这些局限，我们提出了一种面向分钟级连贯音乐到舞蹈生成的新型层次化框架。该方法将过程解耦为全局关键帧规划与局部时序细化，利用完整音乐轨道上下文确保长程连贯性。关键创新包括通过时间映射RoPE嵌入实现动态帧率自适应以精确对齐，基于光流的损失函数增强运动连续性，以及运动速度控制以在快速运动中保持高保真细节。大量实验表明，我们的框架突破了传统时长限制，可生成稳定、720p/30fps、超过一分钟的视频，且具有更优的时间稳定性。此外，该模型在五种不同舞蹈风格上展现出强大泛化能力，支持音频与文本提示条件生成，建立了连贯长时舞蹈视频合成的新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of generating long-duration, high-definition, rhythmically synchronized dance videos from music due to temporal limitations in diffusion models, this paper introduces Wan-Dancer, a hierarchical framework that separates global keyframe planning from local temporal refinement to ensure coherence over minutes. It employs time-mapped RoPE embeddings for dynamic frame rate adaptation, an optical-flow-based loss for motion continuity, and motion-speed control to preserve detail during rapid movements. Experiments show the model generates stable 720p/30fps videos exceeding one minute, outperforming prior methods in temporal stability and achieving state-of-the-art results across five dance genres when conditioned on audio and text.
为解决扩散模型在音乐驱动舞蹈视频生成中因时间限制难以支持超过20秒的挑战，本文提出Wan-Dancer框架，通过全局关键帧规划与局部时序细化分离，结合时间映射RoPE嵌入、光流损失和运动速度控制，确保长时序一致性与高细节保真。实验表明该方法可稳定生成超一分钟的720p/30fps视频，显著优于现有方法，在五种舞蹈风格中均实现音频与文本条件下的最优表现。
Knowledge Graphs and Explainable AI as Complementary Resources for Urban Mining
Authors: Jan Gronewald, Andreas Emrich, Nijat Mehdiyev
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-07-10T16:27:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:27:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the AISE Workshop @ IJCAI-ECAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-demolition assessment, the regulated audit process at the heart of urban mining, is an information process in which AI support must serve qualified auditors who remain accountable for the decisions taken. The relevant unit of value is not prediction accuracy alone, but the defensibility of the supported decisions: their legibility, plausibility, sourcing, and contestability. Explainable AI techniques and domain knowledge graphs each address parts of this requirement, and existing taxonomies have catalogued their integration. The literature is descriptively rich but structurally under-specified: what remains less developed is a structural account of why specific integrations produce artefacts neither resource can provide alone. This paper offers a complementarity-theoretic interpretation grounded in the IS resource-based tradition. We propose four consolidated KG-XAI integration modes (Lifting, Constraining, Typing, and Revising), each defined as a typed operation over XAI artefacts and knowledge-graph substrate structures. Each mode unlocks a distinct property of defensibility and contributes to the kind of regulatory artefact pre-demolition assessment demands. A fire-door example from the urban-mining process illustrates the modes using the W3C Linked Building Data stack and valuation extensions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：知识图谱与可解释人工智能作为城市采矿的互补资源
拆前评估是城市采矿的核心监管审计流程，属于一个信息处理过程，其中AI支持必须服务于合格的审计师，审计师需对所作决策负责。相关价值单位并非预测准确性本身，而是支持决策的可辩护性：其可理解性、合理性、来源可追溯性及可争议性。可解释人工智能技术和领域知识图谱各自解决该需求的部分内容，现有分类体系已对它们的整合进行了编目。文献描述丰富但结构上未充分界定：尚未充分发展的部分是对特定整合为何能产生单一资源无法提供的成果的结构性解释。本文基于信息系统资源基础理论，提出一种互补性理论解释。我们提出四种整合模式（提升、约束、类型化、修订），每种模式均定义为在XAI成果与知识图谱基础结构上的类型化操作。每种模式解锁一种独特的可辩护性属性，并贡献于拆前评估所需的监管成果类型。以城市采矿流程中的防火门为例，使用W3C链接建筑数据栈及估值扩展说明这些模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for defensible, auditable AI support in pre-demolition urban mining assessments, this paper proposes a complementarity-theoretic framework that integrates knowledge graphs (KGs) and explainable AI (XAI) to enhance decision legibility and accountability. It introduces four integration modes—Lifting, Constraining, Typing, and Revising—each defined as a typed operation on XAI artefacts and KG structures, designed to unlock specific defensibility properties required by regulatory processes. Experimental analysis using a fire-door valuation case study within the W3C Linked Building Data stack demonstrates how each mode contributes uniquely to producing audit-ready, contestable outputs that neither KGs nor XAI alone can achieve.
为提升城市采矿预拆除审计中AI支持的可问责性和决策可辩护性，本文提出一种互补性理论框架，整合知识图谱与可解释AI以增强决策的可理解性与可争议性。文章定义了四种集成模式——提升、约束、类型化与修订，每种模式作为对XAI产物与知识图谱结构的类型化操作，分别解锁不同的可辩护性属性。通过W3C Linked Building Data栈中的防火门估值案例实证分析，表明每种模式均能独特贡献于监管合规与可审计性，证明单一资源无法满足预拆除评估的全面需求。
Conceptual Networks for Cross-Linguistic Idiomatic Expressions:A Feature-Based Graph Approach
Authors: Kiran Pala, Punam Silu, Lixun Yu
First: 2026-07-10T16:25:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:25:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present an interpretable network-based framework for representing idiomatic and figurative meaning across eight typologically diverse languages, totaling 160 conventional expressions, the large majority of which are idiomatic. Each expression is annotated with binary conceptual features (containment, concealment, emotional, social, etc.) derived from cognitive-linguistic theory, and pairwise Jaccard similarities define a weighted graph. Community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than by language, producing a structure consistent with cognitive-linguistic predictions. The conceptual network captures unique semantic information not present in distributional embeddings, can be scaled via automatic annotation with LLMs, improves downstream idiom detection, and remains robust when enriched with corpus frequencies. Cross-lingual transfer experiments show that conceptual proximity alone can identify acceptable translation equivalents across five language families, with substantial gains over embedding-based baselines. Ablation studies demonstrate that all three feature dimensions -- schemas, roles, and valence -- contribute non-redundantly to both the network's organizational properties and its performance on idiom detection, and that specific graph-derived signals (community membership, neighbor similarity) are particularly informative. The framework offers an interpretable, cross-linguistically stable representation of idiomatic meaning, combining theoretical grounding with practical utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨语言习语表达的概念网络：基于特征的图方法
我们提出了一种可解释的基于网络的框架，用于在八种句法类型各异的语言中表示习语和比喻意义，共涵盖160个常规表达，其中绝大多数为习语。每个表达标注了二元概念特征（包含、隐藏、情感、社会等），这些特征源自认知语言学理论，且成对Jaccard相似性定义加权图。社区检测表明，习语按概念模式聚类而非按语言聚类，其结构符合认知语言学预测。该概念网络捕捉了分布式嵌入中不存在的独特语义信息，可通过LLM自动标注扩展，提升下游习语检测性能，并在加入语料频率后仍保持鲁棒性。跨语言迁移实验表明，仅凭概念接近性即可在五种语言家族中识别可接受的翻译等价物，显著优于基于嵌入的基线方法。消融研究表明，三个特征维度——模式、角色和效价——对网络结构属性和习语检测性能均有非冗余贡献，且特定图衍生信号（社区成员身份、邻居相似性）尤为信息丰富。该框架提供了一种可解释、跨语言稳定的习语意义表示，兼具理论基础与实用价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model idiomatic meaning across diverse languages, the study proposes a feature-based graph framework that encodes idioms using binary conceptual features derived from cognitive-linguistic theory. Each of the 160 expressions is mapped into a weighted graph based on pairwise Jaccard similarities, and community detection reveals that idioms cluster by conceptual schema rather than language, aligning with cognitive theory. The framework outperforms distributional embeddings in idiom detection, scales via LLM-assisted annotation, and shows strong cross-lingual transfer, identifying translation equivalents based on conceptual proximity alone. Ablation studies confirm that schema, role, and valence features each contribute uniquely, and graph-derived signals like community membership are particularly predictive.
为在多种语言中建模习语意义并实现理论与实践结合，该研究构建了一个基于概念特征的图结构，每个习语被赋予来自认知语言学理论的二元概念特征。通过Jaccard相似度构建加权边，社区检测显示习语按概念框架而非语言聚类，符合认知语言学预测。该概念网络在习语检测中优于分布式嵌入，可借助大语言模型自动标注扩展，且在跨语言迁移中表现优异，仅凭概念相似性即可识别翻译等价物。消融实验表明，框架中的三种特征维度（框架、角色、情感）均不可替代，且图结构衍生信号（如社区归属、邻居相似度）尤为有效。
Large-Scale Portfolio Optimization Problem Under Cardinality Constraint With Enhanced Multi-Objective Evolutionary Algorithms
Authors: Danial Ramezani, Mostafa Abouei Ardakan
First: 2026-07-10T16:12:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:12:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision-making is posing an increasingly formidable challenge to investors because of the growing number of alternatives available in financial markets. A hot area of research over the past few decades has been portfolio optimization that seeks to determine how much an investor should invest in which asset. Introducing real-world conditions to the optimization model turns the problem into an NP-hard one for whose solution exact methods become inefficient; hence, researchers have turned to evolutionary algorithms to approximate solutions. In this paper, strengthening strategies are presented for multi-objective evolutionary algorithms that can provide a faster convergence rate and extensive search ability in the portfolio optimization problem under the cardinality constraint. To implement those features, a unique solution representation, a novel operator, and new repair mechanisms are introduced for solving the aforementioned problem in which lower and upper limits are set on the number of assets in the portfolio. For this purpose, new mating strategies along with the aforesaid package are implemented in well-known multi-objective evolutionary algorithms to solve the problem. The customized algorithms are subsequently tested against traditional ones using well-known market indices as benchmarks. Results indicate that the proposed strategy not only provides better approximations but also converges faster as well at no loss of performance with an increasing number of assets in the market.

标题：基于增强多目标进化算法的带基数约束的大规模投资组合优化问题
由于金融市场中可供选择的投资品种日益增多，投资者面临的决策挑战愈发严峻。过去几十年来，投资组合优化一直是热门研究领域，旨在确定投资者应将多少资金投资于何种资产。将现实世界条件引入优化模型，使该问题转化为NP难问题，精确求解方法效率低下，因此研究人员转向进化算法来近似求解。本文提出针对带基数约束的投资组合优化问题的多目标进化算法强化策略，以实现更快的收敛速度和更广泛的搜索能力。为实现这些特性，本文引入了独特的解表示方法、新颖的操作符和新的修复机制，以解决设定投资组合中资产数量上下限的问题。为此，将上述方法与已知的多目标进化算法结合，实施新的配对策略以解决该问题。随后，将定制算法与传统算法在知名市场指数基准上进行测试。结果表明，所提策略不仅提供更优的近似解，且在市场资产数量增加时收敛速度更快，同时不损失性能。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the complexity of modern financial markets and the need for efficient portfolio selection under asset limits, this study enhances multi-objective evolutionary algorithms with novel solution representations, operators, and repair mechanisms to tackle the NP-hard portfolio optimization problem under cardinality constraints. The proposed methods are integrated into established evolutionary frameworks and tested against traditional algorithms using real market indices. Experimental results demonstrate that the enhanced algorithms converge faster and yield superior approximations of Pareto-optimal portfolios without sacrificing performance, even as the number of available assets increases.
为应对金融市场资产选择复杂化及卡迪纳利约束下的投资组合优化难题，本文提出增强型多目标进化算法，通过创新解码方式、算子与修复机制提升收敛速度与搜索能力。在主流市场指数基准测试中，该方法相较传统算法表现更优，收敛更快，且在资产规模扩大时仍保持性能稳定。
TCLA: Training-Free Class-wise Logit Adaptation for Medical Vision-Language Models
Authors: Tianyou Jiang, Ziyu Zhou
First: 2026-07-10T16:06:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:06:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot performance, yet their effectiveness still declines on out-of-distribution (OOD) data due to domain shifts and class bias inherited from large-scale pretraining. Existing few-shot adaptation methods typically introduce additional trainable components, which can be unstable in extremely low-data regimes (e.g., 1-shot), and lack robustness on different medical data. We present TCLA, a purely training-free few-shot adaptation method for Medical VLMs, which is fast and model-agnostic. TCLA corrects inference logits based on a small set of support samples, boosting pretrained VLMs performance by improving inter-class deconfusion and reducing domain shift. Extensive experiments on nine datasets across multiple medical imaging modalities including X-ray, Ultrasound, MRI, CT, Histopathology, demonstrate that TCLA consistently improves OOD performance of Medical VLMs and, in most of cases, outperforms existing training-based adaptation methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TCLA：面向医学视觉-语言模型的无训练类别对数适应方法
医学视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在零样本场景下表现强劲，但在分布外（OOD）数据上效果仍因领域偏移和大规模预训练带来的类别偏差而下降。现有少样本适应方法通常引入额外可训练组件，在极端低数据量场景（如1-shot）中不稳定，且对不同医学数据缺乏鲁棒性。本文提出TCLA，一种纯无训练的少样本适应方法，适用于医学VLMs，具有快速、模型无关的特点。TCLA基于少量支持样本修正推理对数，通过提升类别间区分度并减少领域偏移，显著增强预训练VLMs性能。在涵盖X光、超声、MRI、CT、组织病理学等多模态医学影像的九个数据集上的大量实验表明，TCLA持续提升医学VLMs在OOD数据上的表现，并在多数情况下优于现有基于训练的适应方法。
