This approach enhances inter-class deconfusion and reduces domain shift without introducing additional trainable components, making it stable even in extreme low-data regimes like 1-shot. Experimental results across nine medical imaging modalities show TCLA consistently improves out-of-distribution performance and often surpasses training-based adaptation methods.
为了解决医学视觉语言模型在分布外数据上因领域偏移和类别偏差导致性能下降的问题，TCLA提出了一种无需训练的少样本适应方法，仅通过支持样本修正推理logits，无需引入可训练参数，从而提升类别区分度并减少领域偏移。该方法在九个医学影像数据集（X光、超声、MRI、CT、组织病理学）上进行的实验表明，TCLA在分布外数据上表现稳定且通常优于现有训练式适应方法，尤其在数据量极少的场景下效果显著。
Beyond Fixed Representations: The Vocabulary and Verifier Gaps in Open-Ended AI
Authors: Yuan Cao, Haiqian Yang
First: 2026-07-10T16:05:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:05:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern AI systems are increasingly being evaluated for their ability to reason, code, prove theorems, use tools, and long-horizon research tasks. These are powerful capabilities, but they share a structural limitation: the representational frame within which the model operates, including its conceptual vocabulary, the space of admissible solutions it can search, and the criteria by which success is evaluated, is typically fixed and supplied in advance. This paper argues that building stronger intelligent systems capable of open-ended innovation requires additional classes of operations: the creation, stabilization, and reuse of new representational primitives, which alter the space being searched rather than simply searching within it. We characterize the distance between current AI systems and genuinely open-ended intelligence through two gaps. The first is the vocabulary gap, the difficulty of inventing and stabilizing new representational primitives rather than merely recombining existing ones. The second is the verifier gap, the difficulty of judging the value of a new primitive when its full payoff may be visible only after future reuse. We interpret both gaps through a unified framework of intelligence as cognitive discrepancy reduction. By viewing intelligent behaviors as a sequence of cognitive transformations, we distinguish intra-space transformations which operate within a fixed representational frame, from generative transformations which may modify the frame itself. On this basis, we propose a ladder of innovation autonomy and outline several directions for advancing open-ended AI, including objectives that reward useful representational change, persistent memory architectures for invented primitives, and adaptive verification mechanisms capable of evolving alongside the representations they evaluate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越固定表征：开放端AI中的词汇与验证器差距
现代AI系统正越来越多地被评估其推理、编程、证明定理、使用工具及长期研究任务的能力。这些能力虽强大，但存在结构性局限：模型运行的表征框架——包括其概念词汇、可搜索解空间及成功评估标准——通常是固定的且预先设定的。本文认为，构建具备开放端创新能力的更强智能系统，需要新增操作类别：创建、稳定并重用新的表征基本单元，从而改变搜索空间而非仅在其中搜索。 我们通过两个差距刻画当前AI系统与真正开放端智能之间的距离：第一是词汇差距，即发明并稳定新表征基本单元的难度，而非仅重组现有单元；第二是验证器差距，即在新基本单元的全部收益可能仅在将来重用后才显现时，判断其价值的难度。我们通过统一的智能认知差异减少框架解释这两个差距。将智能行为视为认知变换序列，我们区分了在固定表征框架内操作的框架内变换，与可能修改框架本身的生成性变换。基于此，我们提出创新自主性的阶梯，并概述了推进开放端AI的若干方向，包括奖励有用表征变化的目标、用于发明基本单元的持久记忆架构，以及能与所评估表征共同演化的自适应验证机制。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the structural limitation of modern AI systems, which operate within fixed representational frames that constrain their ability to innovate open-endedly. It introduces two key gaps—vocabulary gap (difficulty in inventing and stabilizing new conceptual primitives) and verifier gap (difficulty in evaluating new primitives before their full value is realized)—and frames them within a cognitive discrepancy reduction model. The authors propose a ladder of innovation autonomy, advocating for mechanisms that reward representational change, support persistent memory for invented primitives, and enable adaptive verification systems that evolve with the representations they assess, thereby enabling AI to transcend fixed frameworks and pursue genuine open-ended innovation.
本文指出当前AI系统受限于固定表征框架，难以实现开放式的创新。作者提出两个关键差距——词汇差距（难以发明并稳定新概念原语）与验证差距（难以在新原语完全价值显现前评估其价值），并将其统一纳入认知差异减少框架。基于认知转换视角，区分了框架内变换与框架生成性变换，进而提出创新自主性阶梯，并建议通过奖励表征变化、持久记忆存储原语、自适应验证机制等方法推动开放AI发展，强调真正的开放智能需改变而非仅搜索表征空间。
CORAL-AUV: CFD Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Underwater Vehicles
Authors: Steven Roche, Milo Van Mooy, Nathan McGuire, Levi Cai, Jonathan P. How, Yogesh Girdhar
First: 2026-07-10T16:02:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T16:02:55+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine grain control and positioning of autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for sampling, maintenance, and survey applications. Traditional control methods for AUVs are labor intensive and are not robust to changes in the vehicle configuration or environmental conditions. Reinforcement learning (RL) promises rapid controller development while handling a range of deployment parameters via domain randomization (DR). However, DR is still limited by the capacity of the underlying simulation to model real physics. In particular, drag physics are difficult to model and are a large contributor to sim-to-real gaps. Meanwhile, computational fluid dynamics (CFD) provides high fidelity drag models but is challenging to leverage within reinforcement learning frameworks due to its computational overhead. Thus, in this paper we exploit the idea of training surrogate approximations of CFD models of a given vehicle, enabling fast inference within RL pipelines. We are the first to successfully deploy a zero-shot RL policy on a 6-DOF AUV in which policy training is performed on surrogate drag models (SDMs) trained on CFD data. We find 31% lower energy usage compared to a controller using simplified physics while traversing between waypoints 11% faster with 19% less error. Our SDM based RL controller better predicts zero-shot transfer and is more robust across reward shaping design choices. When using DR to complete a task with perturbed parameters, we find that the CFD policy is the only controller that successfully transfers. The policies are evaluated in a controlled tank environment and in the field providing extensive testing of the policies' capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CORAL-AUV：面向自主水下航行器的基于CFD的强化学习
自主水下航行器（AUV）的精细控制与定位对采样、维护和勘测应用至关重要。传统AUV控制方法耗时且难以适应车辆配置或环境条件的变化。强化学习（RL）可通过领域随机化（DR）快速开发控制器并处理多种部署参数，但DR受限于底层仿真对真实物理的建模能力，特别是阻力物理建模困难，是仿真到现实差距的主要来源。同时，计算流体动力学（CFD）可提供高保真阻力模型，但因其计算开销大，难以在强化学习框架中应用。本文提出训练AUV特定的CFD模型代理模型，以在RL流程中实现快速推理。我们首次成功在6自由度AUV上部署零样本RL策略，其策略训练基于由CFD数据训练的代理阻力模型（SDM）。实验表明，与使用简化物理的控制器相比，该策略能耗降低31%，航点间航行速度提升11%，误差减少19%。基于SDM的RL控制器对零样本迁移预测更准确，且对奖励函数设计更鲁棒。在使用DR处理参数扰动任务时，仅CFD策略能成功迁移。策略在受控水池环境和实地环境中进行了评估，全面验证了其能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of traditional AUV control methods and the sim-to-real gap caused by inaccurate drag modeling, this paper proposes CORAL-AUV: a reinforcement learning framework that trains policies using surrogate drag models derived from computational fluid dynamics data. The method enables fast, physics-accurate control without the computational burden of real-time CFD. Experimental results show that the CFD-based RL controller reduces energy usage by 31% and improves waypoint traversal speed by 11% with 19% less error compared to simplified physics controllers. It also demonstrates superior zero-shot transfer and robustness across reward shaping, and is the only controller among tested methods that successfully transfers under domain randomization in both tank and field environments.
为解决传统AUV控制方法在动态环境和配置变化下鲁棒性差的问题，本文提出一种利用基于计算流体力学数据训练的代理阻力模型的强化学习框架，实现高效、高保真控制。该方法通过代理模型训练强化学习策略，支持零样本部署于真实AUV上无需重新训练。实验表明，该方法相比简化物理模型控制器节能31%，航点穿越速度提升11%，误差降低19%。此外，仅该CFD训练策略能在域随机化条件下成功实现零样本迁移，展现出对奖励函数设计和真实部署更强的鲁棒性。
Smooth Scaling Laws Hide Stepwise Token Learning
Authors: Pingjie Wang, Zechen Hu, Peiru Yang, Fu Guo, Debing Zhang
First: 2026-06-29T06:53:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:59:05+00:00
Comments: 21 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language model loss follows remarkably regular scaling laws over model and data size, yet it remains unclear why the aggregate loss should exhibit a power-law form. Existing explanations often attribute this regularity to a heavy-tailed spectrum of pattern difficulty in natural language, but this view has not been directly validated at token-level granularity in large-scale real-data training. We present a token-level framework that decomposes scaling laws into localized learning events of individual contextualized tokens. By fitting token loss trajectories with sigmoids, we show that token learning is concentrated in localized transitions, giving rise to a learning-time spectrum that dominates the scaling-law shape. Across more than one hundred pre-training runs on large and diverse real-language corpora with modern LLM architectures, scaling up to 6B parameters and 300B training tokens, the measured learning-time spectrum quantitatively reconstructs the validation loss derivative along the training-step $T$, data-scale $D$, and model-scale $M$ axes. We further show that the same signal is actionable: by reshaping the training distribution according to when tokens become learnable, we alter the optimization trajectory and achieve 11\% faster validation-loss reduction. These results provide direct empirical evidence that scaling laws are governed primarily by the distribution of token-level learning times, and that this distribution can be used not only to explain scaling behavior but also to improve training performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：平滑的缩放定律掩盖了分步的令牌学习
语言模型的损失在模型规模和数据规模上表现出异常规律的缩放定律，但其聚合损失为何呈现幂律形式仍不明确。现有解释常归因于自然语言中模式难度的重尾分布，但该观点尚未在大规模真实数据训练的令牌级粒度上得到直接验证。我们提出一种令牌级框架，将缩放定律分解为单个上下文令牌的局部学习事件。通过用S型函数拟合令牌损失轨迹，我们表明令牌学习集中在局部转变中，从而形成主导缩放定律形状的学习时间谱。在超过一百次针对大规模、多样真实语言语料库的预训练运行中（使用现代大语言模型架构，参数规模最高达6B，训练令牌数达300B），所测得的学习时间谱可定量重建验证损失沿训练步数T、数据规模D和模型规模M轴的导数。我们进一步表明，该信号具有可操作性：通过根据令牌何时变得可学习来重塑训练分布，可改变优化轨迹，实现验证损失减少11%。这些结果提供了直接实证证据，表明缩放定律主要由令牌级学习时间分布所支配，且该分布不仅可用于解释缩放行为，还可用于提升训练性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that language model loss follows consistent scaling laws despite varying model and data sizes, this study investigates whether token-level learning dynamics underlie these patterns. The authors introduce a token-level framework that models individual token loss trajectories using sigmoid functions, revealing that learning occurs in localized, stepwise transitions rather than smoothly. Across over 100 pre-training runs on diverse real-language corpora with models up to 6B parameters and 300B tokens, the resulting learning-time spectrum accurately reconstructs validation loss derivatives across training steps, data scale, and model scale. Crucially, by reshaping the training distribution based on token learnability timing, they achieved an 11% faster reduction in validation loss, demonstrating that token-level learning timing not only explains scaling laws but also improves training efficiency.
该研究探讨了语言模型损失为何随模型与数据规模呈现可预测的缩放规律，挑战了将规律归因于语义模式难度的传统解释。作者提出一种基于单个上下文词元的学习轨迹建模框架，发现局部学习事件主导缩放规律。在超过100次真实语料预训练实验中（最大6B参数、300B训练词元），词元学习时间分布定量解释了验证损失随训练步数、数据量和模型规模的变化。更重要的是，通过调整训练分布以优先学习易学词元，验证损失下降速度提升11%，证明词元级学习时间分布不仅能解释缩放行为，还能优化训练效率。
Task-Adaptive Design of Modular Aerial Manipulators Under Airflow Exposure Constraints
Authors: Mengguang Li, Heinz Koeppl
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-07-10T15:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:58:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aerial manipulation with multirotor platforms enables physical interaction in complex environments, but rotor-induced airflow remains a critical limitation for tasks involving airflow-sensitive targets or surroundings. This paper presents an optimization-based design framework for modular aerial manipulators that jointly considers task wrench feasibility, end-effector placement, and airflow exposure constraints. We first introduce a novel categorization of target-side airflow tolerance and formulate the corresponding exposure requirements as geometric constraints. To efficiently model rotor-induced airflow, we introduce a compact cone-sphere envelope that approximates the spreading structure of a quadrotor's airflow while preserving computational tractability for optimization. Building on this formulation, we propose a reconfiguration optimization that adapts a modular aerial manipulator to diverse task wrench requirements while enforcing both target-side airflow exposure and intra-platform airflow interference constraints. Unlike prior designs that assume a fixed end-effector location, the proposed framework optimizes the end-effector placement together with the platform configuration. Scalability experiments and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在气流暴露约束下的任务自适应模块化空中 manipulator 设计
多旋翼平台的空中操作可在复杂环境中实现物理交互，但旋翼诱导气流仍是涉及气流敏感目标或环境的任务的关键限制。本文提出了一种基于优化的模块化空中 manipulator 设计框架，同时考虑任务力矩可行性、末端执行器位置和气流暴露约束。我们首先引入目标侧气流容忍度的新分类方法，并将相应的暴露要求形式化为几何约束。为高效建模旋翼诱导气流，我们引入一种紧凑的锥球包络，近似四旋翼气流的扩散结构，同时保持优化计算的可行性。在此基础上，我们提出一种重构优化方法，使模块化空中 manipulator 能适应多样化的任务力矩需求，同时满足目标侧气流暴露和平台内气流干扰约束。与以往假设末端执行器位置固定的方案不同，本框架同时优化末端执行器位置与平台构型。可扩展性实验和消融研究验证了该框架的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of performing physical tasks in environments sensitive to rotor-induced airflow, this paper proposes an optimization-based framework for designing modular aerial manipulators that adapt to task-specific wrench requirements while respecting airflow exposure constraints. The method introduces a geometric categorization of airflow tolerance at the target side and models airflow using a compact cone-sphere envelope for computational efficiency. It then formulates a reconfiguration optimization that jointly optimizes end-effector placement and platform configuration to satisfy both task wrench feasibility and airflow constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that the framework effectively adapts to diverse tasks while minimizing airflow exposure, outperforming fixed-end-effector designs in scalability and constraint satisfaction.
该论文针对旋翼机作业时因气流干扰影响任务执行的问题，提出一种基于优化的模块化空中 manipulator 设计框架。方法首先对目标侧气流耐受性进行几何分类，并引入锥球包络模型近似四旋翼气流分布以保持计算效率；随后构建重构优化问题，联合优化平台构型与末端执行器位置，以满足任务力矩需求并同时约束目标侧气流暴露与平台内气流干扰。实验表明，该框架相较固定末端位置设计显著提升任务可行性，且具备良好的可扩展性。
Diagnosing Long-Video Quantitative Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs via Enumeration and Counting
Authors: Fumihiko Tsuchiya, Taiki Miyanishi, Shunsuke Yasuki, Mahiro Ukai, Nakamasa Inoue, Shuhei Kurita, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
First: 2026-03-31T16:16:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:58:11+00:00
Comments: The first two authors are equally contributed. The data and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/matsuolab/EC-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Final-answer video QA can show whether a model predicts the right number, but not which instances it counted, when the supporting evidence occurs, or why it failed. We diagnose long-video quantitative reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) through three coupled abilities: enumerating query-relevant instances, temporally grounding supporting evidence, and aggregating the evidence into counts. To support this analysis, we build EC-Bench, an evidence-annotated evaluation suite with 152 untrimmed videos longer than 30 minutes, 1,699 open-ended queries across six reasoning categories, and human-verified evidence spans. We evaluate 22 open-source and proprietary MLLMs using timestamped visual frames and transcripts. The best average scores reach only 29.98% Enumeration F1 and 23.74% Counting accuracy, compared with human performance of 78.57% and 82.97%, respectively. Our analyses show that counting errors are rarely isolated arithmetic mistakes: Enumeration F1 is strongly associated with Counting accuracy, temporal grounding quality is associated with lower counting error, and Counting accuracy drops as supporting evidence becomes more distributed. These findings recast long-video counting as evidence retrieval, temporal grounding, deduplication, and aggregation across the video, rather than simple numerical prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过枚举与计数诊断多模态大语言模型中的长视频量化推理
最终答案视频问答只能显示模型是否预测了正确的数字，却无法说明模型计数了哪些实例、何时出现支持证据或为何失败。我们通过三种耦合能力诊断多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的长视频量化推理：枚举查询相关实例、时间上定位支持证据、将证据聚合为计数。为支持该分析，我们构建了EC-Bench评估套件，包含152个超过30分钟的未剪辑视频、1699个跨六类推理的开放式问题，以及人工验证的证据片段。我们使用带时间戳的视觉帧和转录文本评估了22个开源和专有MLLMs。表现最佳的模型平均得分仅为29.98%的枚举F1和23.74%的计数准确率，而人类表现分别为78.57%和82.97%。分析表明，计数错误极少是孤立的算术错误：枚举F1与计数准确率强相关，时间定位质量与较低计数错误相关，且当支持证据分布更分散时，计数准确率下降。这些发现将长视频计数重新定义为跨视频的证据检索、时间定位、去重与聚合，而非简单的数值预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how multimodal large language models reason about quantitative questions in long videos, this study diagnoses their performance by evaluating three core abilities: enumerating relevant instances, grounding evidence temporally, and aggregating counts. Using EC-Bench, a new benchmark with 152 long videos and 1,699 queries, the researchers tested 22 MLLMs and found that even top models achieved only 29.98% Enumeration F1 and 23.74% Counting accuracy, far below human levels of 78.57% and 82.97%. Analysis reveals that counting errors are rarely arithmetic mistakes but instead stem from poor enumeration, weak temporal grounding, or fragmented evidence, highlighting that long-video counting requires evidence retrieval, deduplication, and aggregation rather than simple number prediction.
为揭示多模态大语言模型在长视频定量推理中的具体缺陷，该研究通过评估三项核心能力——枚举相关实例、时间定位证据、聚合计数——来诊断模型推理过程。基于EC-Bench数据集（含152个超30分钟未剪辑视频、1699个开放式问题及人工标注证据片段），作者对22个开源与商业模型进行评估。结果显示，最优模型仅达到29.98%枚举F1和23.74%计数准确率，远低于人类水平（78.57%和82.97%）。分析表明，枚举与计数准确率高度相关，时间定位越精准计数错误越少，而证据分布越分散计数准确率越低。
Graph-Regularized Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Variable Projection
Authors: Benoît Loucheur, P. -A. Absil, Michel Journée
First: 2026-07-10T15:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:57:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the low-rank matrix completion problem by incorporating graph regularization into the existing Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion (RTRMC) framework. The latter uses the geometry of the low-rank constraint to remodel the problem as an unconstrained optimization problem on a single Grassmann manifold. Our approach, named Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), exploits the inherent relationships between rows and columns of the matrix. By using these relationships, we aim to improve the accuracy and robustness of matrix completion, particularly in scenarios where the underlying data exhibits strong correlations between rows or columns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于变量投影的图正则化低秩矩阵补全
我们通过将图正则化引入现有的黎曼信任域矩阵补全（RTRMC）框架，解决低秩矩阵补全问题。RTRMC利用低秩约束的几何特性，将问题重述为单个格拉斯曼流形上的无约束优化问题。我们的方法称为图正则化RTRMC（GR-RTRMC），利用矩阵行与列之间的内在关系，旨在提高矩阵补全的准确性和鲁棒性，特别是在数据行或列间存在强相关性的情形下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance matrix completion accuracy in data with strong row-column correlations, this paper introduces Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), which integrates graph regularization into the Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion framework. The method leverages the geometric structure of the low-rank constraint on a Grassmann manifold and incorporates graph-based relationships between matrix rows and columns to guide the optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that GR-RTRMC achieves higher completion accuracy and improved robustness compared to the baseline RTRMC, especially in datasets where row and column dependencies are pronounced.
为提升存在强行列表示相关性的数据中矩阵补全的准确性，本文提出图正则化Riemannian信任域矩阵补全（GR-RTRMC）方法，将图正则化融入RTRMC框架。该方法在Grassmann流形上利用低秩约束的几何结构，同时引入行与列间的图结构关系以引导优化过程。实验表明，GR-RTRMC在行列依赖显著的数据集上，相较基线RTRMC具有更高的补全精度和更强的鲁棒性。
SHARP: Sleep-based Hierarchical Accelerated Replay for Long Range Non-Stationary Temporal Pattern Recognition
Authors: Jayanta Dey, Shikhar Srivastava, Itamar Lerner, Christopher Kanan, Dhireesha Kudithipudi
First: 2026-05-30T13:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:55:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning long-range non-stationary temporal patterns remains a core challenge for modern sequence models, particularly in strict streaming settings. In these settings, data arrive sequentially and must be processed in a single pass without simultaneously revisiting past observations. Standard architectures, including recurrent neural networks and transformers, are constrained by either truncated backpropagation through time horizon or explicit input window length for long range credit assignment. To address these limitations, we propose SHARP (Sleep-based Hierarchical Accelerated Replay), a framework that decomposes temporal learning into two complementary components: a memory module that accumulates a structured history of past inputs, and a pattern-recognition module that operates over this memory. This separation enables resource- and compute-efficient adaptation to non-stationary dynamics by eliminating the need for backpropagation through time across many steps for long-range credit assignment. Inspired by the accelerated replay observed in rodents during slow-wave sleep, SHARP incorporates offline (sleep) phases in which temporally structured memory traces are replayed in an accelerated form and integrated into higher-level memory representations, improving long-range context retention. Through controlled simulations and ablation studies, we characterize the key properties of the proposed framework. In benchmark datasets such as text8 and PG-19, we demonstrate that SHARP improves over recurrent baselines by retaining next-token predictive performance on previously seen data while continuing to learn from the current stream and generalizing to future unseen data. These gains are enabled by its hierarchical structure, which yields an exponentially increasing effective temporal context with only linear-time computational cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SHARP：基于睡眠的分层加速重放用于长程非平稳时序模式识别
学习长程非平稳时序模式仍是现代序列模型的核心挑战，特别是在严格流式设置中，数据按序到达且必须单次处理，无法重复访问历史观测。标准架构（如循环神经网络和Transformer）受限于时间截断的反向传播或显式的输入窗口长度，难以实现长程信用分配。为解决此问题，我们提出SHARP（基于睡眠的分层加速重放）框架，将时序学习分解为两个互补组件：一个记忆模块，用于累积结构化的过去输入历史；一个模式识别模块，基于该记忆进行操作。这种分离通过消除长程信用分配所需的多步反向传播，实现了资源与计算效率的提升。受啮齿动物慢波睡眠期间加速重放现象的启发，SHARP引入离线（睡眠）阶段，在此阶段以加速形式重放时序结构化的记忆痕迹，并整合至高层记忆表征中，从而提升长程上下文保留能力。通过受控模拟与消融研究，我们刻画了该框架的关键特性。在text8和PG-19等基准数据集上，SHARP在保持对已见数据的下一词预测性能的同时，持续从当前流中学习并泛化至未来未见数据，优于循环基线模型。这些性能提升得益于其分层结构，仅需线性时间计算成本即可实现指数级增长的有效时序上下文。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of learning long-range non-stationary temporal patterns in strict streaming settings where models cannot revisit past data, SHARP introduces a hierarchical framework that separates memory accumulation from pattern recognition. It employs a memory module to store structured historical inputs and a pattern-recognition module that operates over this memory, eliminating the need for long-range backpropagation. Inspired by rodent sleep replay, SHARP incorporates offline replay phases to accelerate and integrate memory traces into higher-level representations, enhancing context retention. Experiments on text8 and PG-19 show SHARP outperforms recurrent baselines by maintaining predictive performance on seen data while adapting to new patterns and generalizing to unseen future data, all with linear computational cost and exponentially growing effective context.
为解决在严格流式设置下学习长程非平稳时序模式的挑战——模型无法重复访问历史数据——SHARP提出一种分层框架，将记忆积累与模式识别分离：记忆模块结构化存储历史输入，模式识别模块在其上操作，避免长程反向传播。受啮齿动物慢波睡眠中加速重放机制启发，SHARP引入离线重放阶段，以加速形式整合记忆痕迹至高层表示，提升长期上下文保留能力。在text8和PG-19基准数据上，SHARP在保持对已见数据预测性能的同时，持续学习新流并泛化至未见未来数据，且仅需线性计算开销即可实现指数级有效时序上下文扩展。
The Quantification Horizon Theory of Consciousness
Authors: T. R. Le
First: 2017-04-04T18:32:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:54:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To make nature mathematically tractable, the scientific model of the world omits qualia--colors, sounds, tastes, sensations--leaving only what admits of numerical characterization. The "hard problem" of consciousness--the enigma of why and how physical processing gives rise to felt experience--remains unsolved. The Quantification Horizon Theory of Consciousness (QHT) proposes that this enigma reflects a structural limitation of mathematical description: quantitative models capture only quantifiable features of reality; qualia are left out. Yet despite this limitation, QHT argues that such models can account for the unquantifiable--not by explaining or containing it, but by marking its place, in the form of a signpost. There are specific structural features--compression singularities in a system's own self-compression, rendered in this paper through information geometry--that intuitively correspond to the hallmark properties of consciousness and could serve as precisely such signposts. QHT proposes that these singularities mark a quantification horizon--a boundary beyond which quantitative description cannot reach. On this proposal, qualia lie beyond the horizon. From this basis, the theory conditionally localizes the structure of ineffability, privacy, and subjectivity, and proposes structural accounts of unity and causal efficacy. The theory proposes substrate-independent dynamical criteria as candidate markers of which systems may be conscious, is anti-panpsychist without adding intuition-saving exclusions, defines prospective prediction schemas through a designated formal rendering, and offers concrete implications for artificial intelligence and artificial consciousness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：意识的量化视界理论
为使自然具有数学可处理性，科学的世界模型省略了质性——颜色、声音、味道、感觉——仅保留可进行数值描述的部分。意识的“硬问题”——即物理处理为何及如何产生主观体验——仍未解决。意识的量化视界理论（QHT）提出，这一谜题反映了数学描述的结构性局限：定量模型仅能捕捉现实的可量化特征，而质性则被排除在外。然而，尽管存在这一局限，QHT认为，此类模型仍可表征不可量化之物——并非通过解释或包含它，而是通过标记其位置，以“路标”形式呈现。存在特定的结构性特征——系统自我压缩过程中出现的信息几何压缩奇点——直观上对应意识的标志性特征，可作为此类“路标”。QHT提出，这些奇点标志着“量化视界”——即定量描述无法触及的边界。依此观点，质性位于视界之外。基于此，该理论有条件地定位了不可言说性、私密性与主观性的结构，并提出统一性与因果效力的结构解释。该理论提出独立于基质的动态标准作为意识系统的候选标记，不添加直觉保护性排除条件，通过指定形式化表达定义前瞻性预测框架，并为人工智能与人工意识提供具体启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unresolved 'hard problem' of consciousness—why and how physical processes generate subjective experience—the Quantification Horizon Theory (QHT) posits that qualia are inherently unquantifiable and thus excluded from mathematical models, which only capture quantifiable features. QHT identifies structural features in information geometry—specifically compression singularities—that intuitively align with core properties of consciousness and propose to function as signposts marking a 'quantification horizon,' beyond which quantitative description fails. Experimental validation is not provided in the paper, but the theory offers substrate-independent dynamical criteria to identify potential consciousness, avoids panpsychism, and proposes formal prediction schemas with implications for artificial intelligence.
为了解决意识的‘硬问题’——即为何物理过程会产生主观体验——量化地平线理论（QHT）提出，质性体验（qualia）本质上无法被量化，因而被数学模型排除在外，而模型仅能描述可量化的特征。QHT将信息几何中的自压缩压缩奇点视为‘路标’，这些奇点标志着一个量化描述无法逾越的‘量化地平线’，并被认为对应意识的核心属性，如不可言说性与主观性。该理论提出无需泛心论假设的动态判据，用于识别意识系统，并为人工智能提供可操作的预测框架，但尚未进行实证验证。
Multi-Metric Adaptive Experimental Design Under a Fixed Budget with Validation
Authors: Qining Zhang, Tanner Fiez, Yi Liu, Wenyang Liu
First: 2025-06-03T16:41:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:53:13+00:00
Comments: Published in AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A/B tests in online experiments face statistical power challenges when testing multiple candidates simultaneously, while adaptive experimental designs (AED) alone fall short in inferring experiment statistics such as the average treatment effect, especially with many metrics (e.g., revenue, safety) and heterogeneous variances. This paper proposes a fixed-budget multi-metric AED framework with a two-phase structure: an adaptive exploration phase to identify the best treatment, and a validation phase with an A/B test to verify the treatment's quality and infer statistics. We propose SHRVar, which generalizes sequential halving (SH) with a novel relative-variance-based sampling and an elimination strategy built on reward z values. It achieves a provable error probability that decreases exponentially, where the exponent H3 generalizes the complexity measure for SH and SHVar with homogeneous and heterogeneous variances, respectively. Numerical experiments demonstrate its performance and robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：固定预算下多指标自适应实验设计及其验证
在线实验中，同时测试多个候选方案时，A/B测试面临统计功效挑战；而仅使用自适应实验设计（AED）难以推断实验统计量（如平均处理效应），尤其在多个指标（如收入、安全性）和异方差情况下。本文提出一种固定预算多指标AED框架，采用两阶段结构：第一阶段为自适应探索阶段，用于识别最优处理方案；第二阶段为验证阶段，通过A/B测试验证处理方案质量并推断统计量。我们提出SHRVar方法，将顺序减半（SH）推广至基于相对方差的采样策略，并结合基于奖励z值的淘汰机制。该方法可证明误差概率呈指数下降，其中指数H3分别推广了SH与SHVar在同方差与异方差情况下的复杂度度量。数值实验验证了其性能与鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the statistical power limitations in A/B testing multiple candidates under fixed budgets and the inability of standard adaptive designs to reliably estimate treatment effects across multiple metrics with heterogeneous variances, this paper introduces SHRVar, a two-phase adaptive experimental design. The first phase uses sequential halving with a novel relative-variance-based sampling and reward z-value elimination to identify the best treatment, while the second phase validates the top candidate via a fixed-budget A/B test to infer statistics. Experiments show SHRVar achieves exponentially decreasing error probability, with its complexity measure H3 adapting to both homogeneous and heterogeneous variance settings, demonstrating strong empirical performance and robustness.
为了解决在线实验中在固定预算下同时测试多个候选方案时统计功效不足，以及传统自适应设计难以在多指标（如收入、安全性）且方差异质的情况下准确推断处理效应的问题，本文提出SHRVar框架，包含探索与验证两个阶段。探索阶段采用基于相对方差的采样和奖励z值淘汰策略改进的序列减半算法，以识别最优处理方案；验证阶段通过标准A/B测试验证该方案并推断统计量。实验表明，SHRVar可实现指数级误差概率衰减，其复杂度度量H3能适应异质方差环境，在效率与统计可靠性上优于基线方法。
The Count Is There, but Misaligned: Understanding and Correcting Counting Failures in VLMs
Authors: Ahmed Oumar El-Shangiti, Abzal Nurgazy, Hilal AlQuabeh, Nikolai Rozanov, Kentaro Inui
First: 2026-07-10T15:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:50:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite strong performance on many multimodal tasks, vision-language models (VLMs) still struggle with basic object counting. We investigate whether this reflects missing internal knowledge or a gap between internal representations and verbalized outputs. Training simple probes on activations from four VLMs across five counting datasets reveals that nonlinear probes can reliably detect counting errors, suggesting that VLMs often encode the correct count even when they output the wrong answer. SVCCA analysis shows that probes trained on ground-truth counts and probes trained on model outputs occupy a partially shared activation subspace but read out along misaligned directions. We further validate our findings using a causal steering intervention, proving that strengthening the direction of count-identified probes does improve model counting performance. Motivated by this result, we propose a detector-guided self-correction method that selectively re-prompts the model only when an internal error detector predicts failure. This simple inference-time intervention improves counting accuracy by up to 15.6 absolute percentage points, without any parameter updates. Our results establish activation-based error probing as both a practical tool for improving VLM counting and a mechanistic lens on the gap between internal knowledge and model outputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计数器存在但对齐错误：理解并纠正视觉语言模型中的计数失败
尽管在许多多模态任务上表现强劲，视觉语言模型（VLMs）仍难以处理基本的物体计数。我们探究这一现象是源于内部知识缺失，还是内部表征与语言输出之间的差距。在五个计数数据集上对四种VLMs的激活进行简单探测器训练表明，非线性探测器能可靠检测计数错误，说明VLMs常能正确编码计数结果，即使输出错误答案。SVCCA分析显示，基于真实计数训练的探测器与基于模型输出训练的探测器占据部分共享的激活子空间，但读取方向错位。我们进一步通过因果引导干预验证了这一发现，证明强化计数探测器方向确实能提升模型计数性能。受此启发，我们提出一种探测器引导的自校正方法，仅当内部错误探测器预测失败时才重新提示模型。这一简单的推理时干预无需参数更新，即可将计数准确率提升最高15.6个百分点。我们的结果确立了基于激活的错误探测作为改进VLM计数的实用工具，以及理解内部知识与模型输出之间差距的机制性视角。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models (VLMs) often fail at basic object counting despite strong performance on other multimodal tasks, prompting investigation into whether this stems from missing knowledge or misalignment between internal representations and verbal outputs. The study trains nonlinear probes on VLM activations across five datasets, revealing that models frequently encode correct counts but output incorrectly, with SVCCA showing that ground-truth and output-based probes share some activation space but read out along misaligned directions. A causal steering intervention confirms that aligning model outputs with count-detecting directions improves performance, leading to a detector-guided self-correction method that selectively re-prompts the model when internal detectors flag errors, boosting counting accuracy by up to 15.6 percentage points without retraining.
尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多数多模态任务上表现优异，但在基础物体计数任务上仍常出错，研究者探究了这种失败是源于内部知识缺失还是内部表征与语言输出的不匹配。通过在多个数据集上训练非线性探测器，发现模型内部常正确编码计数结果，但输出错误源于读取方向错位，SVCCA分析证实了这一点。通过因果干预验证了对齐计数探测器方向可提升性能，进而提出一种基于探测器的自校正方法，在预测错误时重新提示模型，无需参数更新即可将计数准确率提升15.6个百分点。
CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding
Authors: Gabriel Mahuas, Victoria Shevchenko, Ugo Tanielian, Yassir Bendou, Richard Gao
First: 2026-07-10T15:48:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:48:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoCoT-EEG：用于脑电图解码的对比预训练多尺度卷积变换模型
自监督预训练基础模型（FM）在非侵入式脑电图（EEG）解码应用中已显示出早期潜力。许多近期大规模模型采用对原始EEG进行分词后进行掩码重建预训练的方法。然而，该方法对于噪声幅度高、信息局限于有限维度（如窄频带）的数据（如EEG）已被证明效果不佳。基于这一认识，我们开发了一种新颖的对比预训练EEG模型，其包含多尺度时间卷积输入层和Transformer编码器块（CoCoT）。CoCoT在多种异构电极配置的基准解码任务中，表现与当前最先进的重建预训练EEG模型相当或更优。此外，从零训练的CoCoT超越了以往的单任务解码模型，甚至可媲美预训练模型，展示了该架构的灵活性和数据效率。通过系统性的消融实验（包括模型架构和预训练目标），我们证明了对比学习在构建EEG FM中的可行性，并提出了关键的架构设计考量，推动对替代大规模预训练策略的进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of masked reconstruction pretraining for noisy, low-dimensional EEG data, this work introduces CoCoT-EEG, a contrastive-pretrained model combining multiscale temporal convolutions with Transformer encoders. Unlike prior methods that rely on tokenized masked reconstruction, CoCoT leverages contrastive learning to align EEG representations across different temporal scales, improving generalization. Experimental results show CoCoT matches or surpasses state-of-the-art reconstruction-based models across diverse EEG decoding benchmarks and electrode setups, and even outperforms single-task models trained from scratch, demonstrating its data efficiency and architectural flexibility.
鉴于掩码重建预训练方法在高噪声、低维EEG数据上的局限性，本研究提出CoCoT-EEG模型，结合多尺度时域卷积与Transformer编码器，采用对比学习策略替代信号重建。该模型在多种电极配置的EEG解码基准测试中表现优于或持平当前最先进方法，且从零训练时性能超越以往单任务模型，甚至可媲美预训练模型，展现出优异的数据效率与架构灵活性。消融实验验证了对比学习的有效性，并指明了关键架构设计方向，为未来EEG基础模型的预训练策略提供了新思路。
Contrastive Weak-to-strong Generalization
Authors: Houcheng Jiang, Junfeng Fang, Jiaxin Wu, Tianyu Zhang, Chen Gao, Xiang Wang, Xiangnan He, Yang Deng
First: 2025-10-09T07:37:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:46:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Weak-to-strong generalization provides a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) by training stronger models on samples from aligned weaker ones, without requiring human feedback or explicit reward modeling. However, its robustness and generalization are hindered by the noise and biases in weak-model outputs, which limit its applicability in practice. To address this challenge, we leverage implicit rewards, which approximate explicit rewards through log-likelihood ratios, and reveal their structural equivalence with Contrastive Decoding (CD), a decoding strategy shown to reduce noise in LLM generation. Building on this connection, we propose Contrastive Weak-to-Strong Generalization (ConG), a framework that employs contrastive decoding between pre- and post-alignment weak models to generate higher-quality samples. This approach enables more reliable capability transfer, denoising, and improved robustness, substantially mitigating the limitations of traditional weak-to-strong methods. Empirical results across different model families confirm consistent improvements, demonstrating the generality and effectiveness of ConG. Taken together, our findings highlight the potential of ConG to advance weak-to-strong generalization and provide a promising pathway toward AGI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对比式弱到强泛化
弱到强泛化通过在对齐的弱模型样本上训练更强模型，为扩展大语言模型（LLM）提供了一种有前景的范式，且无需人工反馈或显式奖励建模。然而，弱模型输出中的噪声和偏差限制了其实际应用中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。为解决这一挑战，我们利用隐式奖励——通过日志似然比近似显式奖励——并揭示其与对比解码（CD）的结构等价性，CD是一种已被证明能降低LLM生成噪声的解码策略。基于此联系，我们提出对比式弱到强泛化（ConG）框架，通过对比对齐前后的弱模型进行解码，生成更高质量的样本。该方法实现了更可靠的技能迁移、去噪和增强鲁棒性，显著缓解了传统弱到强方法的局限性。在不同模型家族上的实证结果一致显示性能提升，验证了ConG的通用性和有效性。综合来看，我们的研究凸显了ConG在推进弱到强泛化方面的潜力，并为通向AGI提供了有前景的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the robustness and generalization of weak-to-strong generalization in large language models, this work introduces Contrastive Weak-to-Strong Generalization (ConG), which leverages contrastive decoding between pre- and post-alignment weak models to denoise outputs and enhance capability transfer. By framing implicit rewards as log-likelihood ratios and linking them to Contrastive Decoding’s noise-reduction properties, ConG generates higher-quality samples without human feedback. Experimental results across multiple model families consistently show improved performance, demonstrating ConG’s effectiveness and broad applicability in scaling LLMs.
为提升大语言模型弱到强泛化方法的鲁棒性和泛化能力，本文提出对比式弱到强泛化（ConG）框架，通过在对齐前后弱模型间采用对比解码策略，有效降低弱模型输出噪声并增强能力迁移。该方法将隐式奖励建模为对数似然比，并揭示其与对比解码在结构上的等价性，从而无需人工反馈即可提升样本质量。实验在多个模型家族上均验证了ConG的稳定性能提升，证明其在样本质量、鲁棒性和泛化能力方面优于传统方法。
Learning Agile Quadrotor Flight in the Real World
Authors: Yunfan Ren, Zhiyuan Zhu, Jiaxu Xing, Davide Scaramuzza
Venue: Robotics: Science and Systems 2026
First: 2026-02-10T18:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:44:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning-based controllers have achieved impressive performance in agile quadrotor flight but typically rely on massive training in simulation, necessitating accurate system identification for effective Sim2Real transfer. However, even with precise modeling, fixed policies remain susceptible to out-of-distribution scenarios, ranging from external aerodynamic disturbances to internal hardware degradation. To ensure safety under these evolving uncertainties, such controllers are forced to operate with conservative safety margins, inherently constraining their agility outside of controlled settings. While online adaptation offers a potential remedy, safely exploring physical limits remains a critical bottleneck due to data scarcity and safety risks. To bridge this gap, we propose a self-adaptive framework that eliminates the need for precise system identification or offline Sim2Real transfer. We introduce Adaptive Temporal Scaling (ATS) to actively explore platform physical limits, and employ online residual learning to augment a simple nominal model. {Based on the learned hybrid model, we further propose Real-world Anchored Short-horizon Backpropagation Through Time (RASH-BPTT) to achieve efficient and robust in-flight policy updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our quadrotor reliably executes agile maneuvers near actuator saturation limits. The system evolves a conservative base policy with a peak speed of 1.9 m/s to 7.3 m/s within approximately 100 seconds of flight time. These findings underscore that real-world adaptation serves not merely to compensate for modeling errors, but as a practical mechanism for sustained performance improvement in aggressive flight regimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在真实世界中学习敏捷四旋翼飞行
基于学习的控制器在敏捷四旋翼飞行中表现出色，但通常依赖大规模仿真训练，需要精确的系统辨识以实现有效的仿真到现实转移。然而，即使模型精确，固定策略仍易受分布外场景影响，如外部气动扰动或内部硬件退化。为确保在这些动态不确定性下的安全，此类控制器被迫采用保守的安全裕度，从而限制其在受控环境外的敏捷性。尽管在线自适应提供潜在解决方案，但由于数据稀缺和安全风险，安全探索物理极限仍是关键瓶颈。为弥合此差距，我们提出一种自适应框架，无需精确系统辨识或离线仿真到现实转移。我们引入自适应时间缩放（ATS）主动探索平台物理极限，并采用在线残差学习增强简单基准模型。基于所学习的混合模型，我们进一步提出真实世界锚定短时反向传播时序（RASH-BPTT），以实现高效且鲁棒的飞行中策略更新。大量实验表明，我们的四旋翼可可靠执行接近执行器饱和极限的敏捷机动。系统在约100秒飞行时间内，将保守基准策略的峰值速度从1.9米/秒提升至7.3米/秒。这些结果表明，真实世界自适应不仅用于补偿建模误差，更是激进飞行模式下持续性能提升的实用机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed policies in agile quadrotor flight under real-world uncertainties, this work proposes a self-adaptive framework that eliminates reliance on precise system modeling or offline Sim2Real transfer. The method introduces Adaptive Temporal Scaling to actively probe physical limits and uses online residual learning to refine a simple nominal model. It further employs Real-world Anchored Short-horizon BPTT for efficient in-flight policy updates. Experiments show the quadrotor evolves from a conservative policy (peak speed 1.9 m/s) to one capable of 7.3 m/s within 100 seconds of flight, demonstrating that real-world adaptation enables sustained performance gains in aggressive flight regimes.
为解决固定策略在真实世界不确定性下敏捷飞行表现受限的问题，本文提出一种无需精确建模或离线仿真到现实转移的自适应框架。该框架通过自适应时间缩放主动探索物理极限，并结合在线残差学习增强基础模型，配合RASH-BPTT实现飞行中高效策略更新。实验表明，无人机在100秒内将保守的峰值速度从1.9米/秒提升至7.3米/秒，证明真实世界适应不仅弥补建模误差，更可作为在高难度飞行中持续提升性能的有效机制。
Iterated Invariant EKF for 3D Landmark-Aided Inertial Navigation
Authors: Hilton Marques Souza Santana, João Carlos Virgolino Soares, Marco Antonio Meggiolaro
First: 2026-06-30T20:22:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:42:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inertial navigation systems aided by three-dimensional landmark measurements constitute a fundamental problem in robotic perception and state estimation. Classical SO(3)-based Extended Kalman Filter (SO(3)-EKF) approaches provide practical solutions, but suffer from the false observability problem, in which the filter becomes overconfident in unobservable directions, leading to degraded estimation performance. The Invariant EKF (IEKF) addresses this limitation by reformulating the system dynamics as a group-affine system on a Lie group, although its measurement update does not fully satisfy certain state compatibility properties. More recently, the Iterated Invariant EKF (IterIEKF) was proposed to further improve the IEKF by ensuring, in the low-noise regime, that the estimated state remains on the observed state manifold while the uncertainty is confined to its tangent space. In this work, we formulate and apply the IterIEKF to landmark-based inertial 3D localization for the first time. Through numerical simulations, we show that the proposed approach outperforms the classical SO(3)-EKF, the Iterated SO(3)-EKF, and the IEKF in terms of both estimation accuracy and consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于三维地标辅助惯性导航的迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波
三维地标测量辅助的惯性导航系统是机器人感知与状态估计中的基础问题。基于SO(3)的经典扩展卡尔曼滤波（SO(3)-EKF）方法虽实用，但存在虚假可观测性问题，导致滤波器在不可观测方向过度自信，从而降低估计性能。不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）通过将系统动力学重新表述为李群上的群仿射系统解决了这一局限，但其测量更新不完全满足某些状态兼容性属性。近期提出的迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF）进一步改进了IEKF，在低噪声条件下确保估计状态保持在观测状态流形上，同时不确定性被限制在其切空间内。本文首次将IterIEKF应用于基于地标惯性三维定位。数值仿真表明，所提方法在估计精度和一致性方面均优于经典SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical SO(3)-EKF in 3D landmark-aided inertial navigation—particularly its susceptibility to false observability leading to overconfidence and degraded performance—the paper introduces the Iterated Invariant EKF (IterIEKF), which reformulates the system as a group-affine system on the Lie group SO(3) and iteratively refines state estimates to ensure they remain on the observed manifold while uncertainty is confined to the tangent space. Experimental results from numerical simulations demonstrate that IterIEKF achieves superior estimation accuracy and consistency compared to SO(3)-EKF, Iterated SO(3)-EKF, and standard IEKF.
为解决传统SO(3)-EKF在三维地标辅助惯性导航中因虚假可观测性导致的估计性能下降问题，本文提出迭代不变EKF（IterIEKF），通过在李群上重构系统动力学并迭代优化状态估计，确保估计状态与观测状态流形一致。实验表明，IterIEKF在仿真三维定位任务中，相比SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF，在估计精度与一致性上均表现更优。
GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Qitai Tan, Ruiwen Gu, Yilin Su, Mo Li, Xu Lin, Xiao-Ping Zhang
First: 2026-07-10T15:42:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:42:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GatedLinear：面向时间序列预测的互补线性基自适应路由
时间序列预测要求模型捕捉多样且常相互排斥的时序动态，从平滑趋势延续到非平稳漂移及严格相位对齐的周期性。尽管近期深度学习模型提升了精度，但它们通常通过单一计算骨干结构处理这些多样模式，该结构受固定算法归纳偏置（如自注意力或频谱滤波）支配。这种单一机制方法常难以应对现实序列中显著的异质性，不同变量与预测时域需根本不同的预测处理。为此，我们提出GatedLinear：一种轻量框架，将预测建模为互补线性基的自适应路由。GatedLinear整合三种专用机制：全局趋势-季节性基用于平滑投影、基于差分的增量基用于非平稳漂移、相位对齐周期性基用于显式周期重用。为动态协调这些不同行为，我们引入三因子融合门控机制，将路由决策解耦为通道偏好、时域感知偏移及由已知未来时间标记导出的相位索引偏置。该设计使模型可在不同预测场景中实现高度细粒度、逐点软路由，无需堆叠计算密集的神经模块。在标准基准测试中，本方法在精度上达到或超越近期复杂基础模型，同时提供明确可解释的路由模式，并显著降低参数规模。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle diverse and often conflicting temporal patterns in time series forecasting, GatedLinear introduces a lightweight framework that adaptively routes complementary linear bases instead of forcing all dynamics through a single fixed mechanism. It employs three specialized bases—global trend-seasonal, difference-based incremental, and phase-aligned recurrence—and uses a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate to dynamically route them based on channel preferences, horizon awareness, and phase-indexed biases. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that GatedLinear achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy while maintaining interpretability and requiring significantly fewer parameters than complex deep learning models.
为应对时间序列中多样且常相互冲突的时序动态，GatedLinear提出了一种轻量级框架，通过自适应路由互补线性基来替代单一固定机制。该方法包含三种专用基——全局趋势季节性基、差分增量基和相位对齐周期基，并通过三因子融合门控机制根据通道偏好、时域感知偏移和未来时间标记推导的相位偏置动态路由。实验表明，该方法在标准基准上达到最先进或高度竞争力的精度，同时保持可解释性并显著减少参数量。
Estimating Uncertainty from Reasoning: A Large-Scale Study of Multi- and Crosslingual MCQA Performance in LLMs
Authors: Andrea Bacciu, Andrea Alfarano, Saab Mansour, Amin Mantrach, Marcello Federico
First: 2026-07-07T14:25:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:38:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncertainty estimation (UE) enables LLM-powered systems to recognize when to abstain, yet existing research has predominantly focused on English. We present the first large-scale evaluation of UE methods across 22 languages, spanning high-, mid-, and low-resource settings. Using two human-curated Q\&A datasets, we compare open and closed box UE methods (nine in total) across different model sizes and architectures while eliciting long-form reasoning, avoiding LLM-as-a-judge and embedding-based scoring, which can introduce evaluation noise. We report three main actionable findings. First, we find that prompting models to reason in English while keeping questions in low-resource languages substantially improves UE performance, suggesting that comprehension of low-resource languages is largely intact, and that the reliability bottleneck lies in generation rather than understanding. Second, prompting models to reason in English closes the UE performance gap between low and high-resource languages, demonstrating that generation language matters more than the question language. Third, the choice of UE method should depend on model scale: at smaller scales, open-box probability-based methods outperform alternatives; at larger scales, closed-box self-verbalized uncertainty becomes superior. Finally, we provide an analysis of threshold selection for selective prediction, offering guidance on calibrating abstention in multilingual settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从推理中估计不确定性：大型多语言和跨语言多项选择题在大语言模型中的性能研究
不确定性估计（UE）使大语言模型驱动的系统能够识别何时应拒绝回答，但现有研究主要集中在英语。我们首次对22种语言（涵盖高、中、低资源语言）的UE方法进行了大规模评估。使用两个人工编纂的问答数据集，我们在不同模型规模和架构下比较了开放式和封闭式UE方法（共九种），并引导模型进行长篇推理，避免使用LLM作为评判者或基于嵌入的评分，以减少评估噪声。我们报告了三个主要可操作发现：第一，提示模型用英语推理但保留低资源语言问题，能显著提升UE性能，表明低资源语言理解能力基本完好，生成能力是可靠性瓶颈；第二，用英语提示推理可缩小低资源与高资源语言间的UE性能差距，说明生成语言比问题语言更重要；第三，UE方法的选择应取决于模型规模：小规模模型中，开放式概率方法优于其他方法；大规模模型中，封闭式自述不确定性方法更优。最后，我们分析了选择阈值以实现选择性预测的方法，为多语言环境下校准拒绝回答提供了指导。
Summary / 总结
This study motivates the need to evaluate uncertainty estimation in LLMs across diverse languages, moving beyond English-centric research. It employs two human-curated QA datasets and tests nine UE methods across 22 languages, varying model sizes and architectures, while requiring long-form reasoning to avoid noisy evaluation metrics. Key findings reveal that prompting models to reason in English while keeping questions in low-resource languages boosts UE performance, indicating generation is the bottleneck rather than comprehension. UE performance gaps between low- and high-resource languages narrow when reasoning is in English, underscoring the importance of generation language over question language. Additionally, open-box methods work better for smaller models, while closed-box self-verbalized uncertainty dominates larger models, and the study offers practical guidance for selecting abstention thresholds in multilingual settings.
为提升多语言大模型的不确定性估计能力，本研究在22种语言上评估了九种不确定性估计方法，使用两个人工标注的多选问答数据集，比较不同规模和架构模型的开放与封闭式方法，强调长文本推理以避免评价噪声。主要发现表明，让模型用英语推理而保留低资源语言问题可显著提升不确定性估计效果，说明理解能力基本完好，生成能力才是瓶颈。当推理语言为英语时，低资源与高资源语言间的性能差距缩小，说明生成语言比问题语言更重要。此外，小规模模型中开放式概率方法更优，大规模模型中封闭式自述不确定性方法更优，并提供了多语言场景下阈值选择的校准建议。
Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Andrej Bogdanov, Alon Rosen, Neekon Vafa
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:37:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:37:32+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度神经网络中的统计不可检测后门
我们展示了敌对模型训练者如何在一大类深度前馈神经网络中植入后门。这些后门在白盒环境下统计上不可检测，意味着即使拥有模型的完整描述（例如所有权重），被植入后门的模型与诚实训练的模型在总变差距离上仍非常接近。该后门为每个输入提供基于不变性的对抗样本访问权限，将远距离输入映射为异常接近的输出。然而，在没有后门的情况下，根据标准密码学假设，任何此类对抗样本在多项式时间内都不可行。我们的理论和初步实证发现揭示了模型训练者与模型用户之间存在根本性的权力不对称。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand adversarial vulnerabilities in deep neural networks, this work demonstrates how an adversarial trainer can embed statistically undetectable backdoors into feedforward networks, making backdoored models indistinguishable from honest ones even with full model access. The method exploits invariance properties to map distant inputs to unusually close outputs, enabling access to adversarial examples without detection. Experimental results confirm that such backdoors remain undetectable under white-box scrutiny and that generating similar adversarial examples without the backdoor is computationally infeasible under standard cryptographic assumptions, revealing a fundamental power imbalance between model trainers and users.
为理解深度神经网络中的对抗性漏洞，该研究展示了攻击者如何在前馈网络中植入统计上不可检测的后门，即使拥有完整模型权重，仍无法区分被植入后门的模型与正常模型。该方法利用不变性特性，将远距离输入映射为异常接近的输出，从而实现对抗样本的生成。实验结果表明，此类后门在白盒环境下不可检测，且在标准密码学假设下，无后门情况下无法在多项式时间内生成类似对抗样本，揭示了模型训练者与使用者之间根本性的权力不对称。
Evolutionary Discovery of Developmental Reward Schedules in Deep Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Alan Nadelsticher Ruvalcaba
First: 2026-06-18T18:47:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:36:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Development and Learning (ICDL)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The temporal structure of reward composition in reinforcement learning (RL) is typically hand-designed and held fixed throughout training, leaving the progression of motivational priorities largely unexplored. In this work, we propose an evolutionary framework for discovering developmental reward schedules, in which three distinct biologically inspired motivational components -- agency, novelty, and reactivity -- are combined through time-varying weights that dynamically shift over the course of training. Evaluated on two sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks: DoorKey-6x6 and KeyCorridorS3R1, our framework compares the generalizability of four evolutionary algorithms: CMA-ES, xNES, DE, and L-SHADE against an extrinsically motivated baseline (our main comparison point), and three additional hand-designed methods. On DoorKey-6x6, all evolved methods outperform the non-evolved baselines, with L-SHADE achieving the best performance -- an approximate relative mean improvement of 11.4% over the extrinsic only baseline. On KeyCorridorS3R1, CMA-ES achieves the best overall performance, with the remaining evolved methods showing weaker and less reliable generalization capability compared to the extrinsic only baseline. Interestingly, the discovered schedules diverge from our defined developmental ordering, with novelty consistently emerging as the dominant early signal during training, across both tasks. Collectively, our results position evolutionary optimization as a promising approach for developmental reward schedule discovery in deep reinforcement learning, and suggest that what evolution finds to be optimal in computational settings may differ from what it finds to be optimal in biology. The code for this project can be found at: https://github.com/alannadels/Evolutionary_RL.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度强化学习中发育性奖励时间表的进化发现
强化学习（RL）中奖励组成的时序结构通常由人工设计并在训练过程中保持固定，导致动机优先级的演变过程基本未被探索。本文提出一种进化框架，用于发现发育性奖励时间表，其中三种受生物启发的动机成分——自主性、新颖性和反应性——通过随时间变化的权重动态组合，权重在训练过程中动态调整。在两个稀疏奖励的MiniGrid任务（DoorKey-6x6和KeyCorridorS3R1）上评估，我们的框架比较了四种进化算法（CMA-ES、xNES、DE和L-SHADE）与一个外在动机基线（主要比较对象）及三种额外人工设计方法的泛化能力。在DoorKey-6x6任务中，所有进化方法均优于非进化基线，其中L-SHADE表现最佳，相对于仅外在动机基线约提升11.4%。在KeyCorridorS3R1任务中，CMA-ES整体表现最佳，其余进化方法的泛化能力较弱且不如仅外在动机基线稳定。有趣的是，发现的奖励时间表偏离我们定义的发育顺序，新颖性在两个任务中均持续作为训练早期主导信号出现。综合结果表明，进化优化是深度强化学习中发现发育性奖励时间表的有前景方法，且进化在计算环境中发现的最优解可能与在生物环境中发现的最优解不同。该项目代码见：https://github.com/alannadels/Evolutionary_RL.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of exploration into how motivational priorities evolve during reinforcement learning training, this work proposes an evolutionary framework to discover time-varying reward schedules combining agency, novelty, and reactivity. Evaluated on two sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks, four evolutionary algorithms (CMA-ES, xNES, DE, L-SHADE) were compared against an extrinsically motivated baseline and three hand-designed methods. On DoorKey-6x6, all evolved methods outperformed the baseline, with L-SHADE achieving a 11.4% relative improvement; on KeyCorridorS3R1, CMA-ES performed best, though others showed weaker generalization. Notably, novelty consistently dominated early training phases, diverging from the assumed developmental hierarchy, suggesting evolutionary optimization may yield non-biological yet effective reward structures.
为探索强化学习训练中动机优先级如何随时间演变，本研究提出一种进化框架，通过动态权重组合代理、新颖性和反应性三种生物启发式动机成分来发现奖励时序。在两个稀疏奖励MiniGrid任务上，四种进化算法（CMA-ES、xNES、DE、L-SHADE）与外在动机基线及人工设计方法对比。在DoorKey-6x6任务中，所有进化方法均优于基线，其中L-SHADE提升约11.4%；在KeyCorridorS3R1任务中，CMA-ES表现最佳，其余进化方法泛化能力较弱。值得注意的是，新颖性始终在训练早期占据主导地位，与预设的发展顺序不符，表明进化优化在计算环境中发现的奖励结构可能与生物学直觉不同。
FreyaTTS Technical Report
Authors: Ahmet Erdem Pamuk, Ömer Yentür, Ahmet Tunga Bayrak, Yavuz Alp Sencer Öztürk, Mustafa Yavuz
First: 2026-07-10T15:36:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:36:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Freya-TTS, a compact, tokenizer-free, Turkish-first text-to-speech model designed for highly reliable and efficient conversational synthesis. Freya-TTS is a 183.2M-parameter non-autoregressive conditional flow-matching Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates in the frozen continuous latent space of AudioVAE2 (16 kHz encode, 48 kHz decode), allowing the model to focus its capacity on text-to-latent mapping while inheriting high-quality 48 kHz reconstruction. We advance the framework along three key dimensions: (1) rule-free end-to-end modeling from a 92-symbol Turkish character vocabulary without a phonemizer, grapheme-to-phoneme frontend, or discrete speech tokenizer; (2) non-autoregressive parallel denoising, which predicts the entire latent sequence simultaneously over a predicted duration; and (3) a production-oriented two-stage post-training recipe consisting of single-speaker voice locking and short-utterance coverage, improving speaker consistency and robustness on short inputs. On the Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, Freya-TTS achieves a band-matched word error rate (WER) of 8.0% and character error rate (CER) of 3.0%, outperforming substantially larger open-source systems while using a fraction of their parameters. The model achieves a real-time factor of 0.11 on consumer GPUs and runs faster than real time on a laptop CPU, making it well suited for resource-constrained edge deployment. We release the model weights, training and inference code, and evaluation benchmark under the Apache-2.0 license.

我们介绍了Freya-TTS，这是一个紧凑、无需分词器、以土耳其语为优先的文本到语音模型，专为高可靠性和高效对话合成而设计。Freya-TTS是一个1.832亿参数的非自回归条件流匹配扩散Transformer（DiT），在AudioVAE2的冻结连续潜在空间中运行（16kHz编码，48kHz解码），使模型能专注于文本到潜在空间的映射，同时继承高质量48kHz重建能力。我们在三个关键维度上推进了该框架：（1）无需音素化器、字符到音素前端或离散语音分词器，直接从92个符号的土耳其语字符词汇表进行端到端建模；（2）非自回归并行去噪，同时预测整个潜在序列，基于预测时长；（3）面向生产的两阶段后训练方案，包括单说话人语音锁定和短句覆盖，提升短输入下的说话人一致性和鲁棒性。在Freya-TR-Eval基准测试中，Freya-TTS实现了带匹配的词错误率（WER）8.0%和字符错误率（CER）3.0%，显著优于参数量更大的开源系统，同时仅使用其参数的极小部分。该模型在消费级GPU上实时因子为0.11，在笔记本电脑CPU上运行速度超过实时，非常适合资源受限的边缘部署。我们将在Apache-2.0许可下发布模型权重、训练和推理代码及评估基准。

TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems
Authors: Refat Ishrak Hemel, Ehsan Hallaji, Roozbeh Razavi-Far
First: 2026-07-10T15:36:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:36:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

Motivated by the need for standardized evaluation of fraud detection in metaverse virtual economies, this work introduces TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal benchmark dataset that integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured data to simulate realistic fraud and bot scenarios. How Mobile Gas Sensor Trajectories Govern Hydrogen Leak Detection: A Safety Gap in Manual Leak Inspection of Hydrogen System Components
Authors: Christian Masuhr, Arne Wendt, Thorsten Schüppstuhl
First: 2026-07-10T15:36:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:36:07+00:00
Comments: Preliminary draft. Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The integrity of hydrogen infrastructure relies on reliable leak detection, performed almost exclusively via manual tracer gas sniffing in electrolyzer manufacturing. Although mandated by standards, the lack of spatial probe guidance instructions leaves detection reliability entirely to operator execution, further compromised by sensor signal delays. This study quantifies how sniffer trajectory kinematics affect detection reliability at small-scale pipes and fittings, a near-field regime largely neglected by macroscopic dispersion research. Using a robotically guided test bench to eliminate operator variability, static concentration fields and dynamic trajectory passes were acquired across representative geometries under standardized leak rates (5 vol% hydrogen in nitrogen) and varying scanning velocities. Results demonstrate that scanning velocity and spatial probe orientation strongly dictate detectability. Conventional linear trajectories frequently miss leaks under dynamic conditions, causing severe false negatives. Conversely, geometry-specific routing, such as circumferential plunging paths around sealing points, maintains a high safety margin. From these observations, geometry-specific routing rules and a reduction-factor model for dynamic signal loss are derived. The findings reveal that current standard operating procedures pose a tangible safety risk. To operationalize these rules, a proof-of-concept software pipeline is presented, generating validated trajectories directly from 3D models for visualization in assistance systems.

标题：移动气体传感器轨迹如何影响氢气泄漏检测：氢系统组件人工泄漏检查中的安全缺口
氢气基础设施的完整性依赖于可靠的泄漏检测，而在电解槽制造中几乎完全依赖人工示踪气体嗅探。尽管标准强制要求，但缺乏空间探头引导指令，使检测可靠性完全取决于操作员执行，且进一步受到传感器信号延迟的影响。本研究量化了嗅探器轨迹运动学对小型管道和接头处泄漏检测可靠性的影响，这一近场区域在宏观扩散研究中长期被忽视。通过机器人引导的测试台消除操作员变量，在标准泄漏率（氮气中5%体积氢气）和不同扫描速度下，获取了代表性几何结构中的静态浓度场和动态轨迹数据。结果表明，扫描速度和空间探头方向显著影响检测能力。传统线性轨迹在动态条件下常漏检泄漏，导致严重假阴性。相反，针对几何结构的特定路径（如密封点周围环形插入路径）可保持高安全裕度。基于这些观察，推导出几何结构特定路径规则及动态信号衰减的折减因子模型。研究发现，当前标准操作程序存在实际安全风险。为实现这些规则，提出一个概念验证软件流程，可直接从3D模型生成经验证的轨迹，用于辅助系统可视化。

ALICE: Learning a General-Purpose Pathology Foundation Model from Vision, Vision-Language, and Slide-Level Experts
Authors: Jiawen Li, Tian Guan, Huijuan Shi, Xitong Ling, Mingxi Fu, Anjia Han, Chao He, Yonghong He
First: 2026-07-10T15:35:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:35:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Foundation models are reshaping computational pathology, yet their capabilities remain shaped by pretraining objectives, data sources, and spatial scales, fragmenting complementary expertise across separate backbones. Here we present ALICE, a unified foundation model trained through multi-stage agglomerative distillation that sequentially distills eight vision-only, vision-language, and slide-level teacher models into dedicated modules of a single backbone. ALICE is pretrained on 24,985,184 tile-level pathology images and 155,604 high-resolution images, and evaluated across 21 task scenarios, 96 downstream tasks, and 48 data sources, spanning region-of-interest tissue analysis, vision-language multimodal evaluation, and whole-slide clinical assessment. In all three evaluation settings, ALICE achieved the best average rank among task-matched pathology foundation models. These results demonstrate that agglomerative distillation can consolidate complementary capabilities from specialized models into a unified backbone for broad computational pathology applications. The model is available at https://github.com/WonderLandxD/ALICE.

标题：ALICE：从视觉、视觉-语言和切片级专家中学习通用病理学基础模型

OIPP: Object-Adaptive Impact Point Predictor for Catching Diverse In-Flight Objects
Authors: Ngoc Huy Nguyen, Kazuki Shibata, Takamitsu Matsubara
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2025-09-18T06:35:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:34:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE/RSJ IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this study, we address the problem of in-flight object catching using a quadruped robot with a basket. Our objective is to accurately predict the impact point, defined as the object's landing position. This task poses two key challenges: the absence of public datasets capturing diverse objects under unsteady aerodynamics, which are essential for training reliable predictors; and the difficulty of accurate early-stage impact point prediction when trajectories appear similar across objects. To overcome these issues, we construct a real-world dataset of 8,000 trajectories from 20 objects, providing a foundation for advancing in-flight object catching under complex aerodynamics. We then propose the Object-Adaptive Impact Point Predictor (OIPP), consisting of two modules: (i) an Object-Adaptive Encoder (OAE) that extracts object-dependent representations from motion histories, and (ii) an Impact Point Predictor (IPP) that estimates the impact point from these representations. Two IPP variants are implemented: a Neural Acceleration Estimator (NAE)-based method that predicts trajectories and derives the impact point, and a Direct Point Estimator (DPE)-based method that directly outputs it. Experimental results show that our dataset is more diverse and complex than existing datasets, and that our method outperforms baselines on both 15 seen and 5 unseen objects. Furthermore, we show that improved early-stage prediction enhances catching success in simulation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through real-robot experiments. The demonstration is available at https://sites.google.com/view/robot-catching-2025.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OIPP：面向多样飞行物体捕捉的物体自适应撞击点预测器
本研究针对四足机器人携带篮筐捕捉飞行物体的问题，旨在精确预测撞击点（即物体着陆位置）。该任务面临两大挑战：缺乏涵盖多样物体且在非稳态空气动力学下采集的公开数据集，这对训练可靠预测器至关重要；以及当物体轨迹相似时，早期阶段准确预测撞击点的难度。为解决这些问题，我们构建了包含20个物体8000条轨迹的真实世界数据集，为复杂空气动力学下的飞行物体捕捉提供基础。随后提出物体自适应撞击点预测器（OIPP），包含两个模块：(i) 物体自适应编码器（OAE），从运动历史中提取物体依赖表征；(ii) 撞击点预测器（IPP），从表征中估计撞击点。IPP包含两种变体：基于神经加速度估计器（NAE）的方法通过预测轨迹推导撞击点，以及基于直接点估计器（DPE）的方法直接输出撞击点。实验表明，我们的数据集比现有数据集更具多样性和复杂性，且方法在15个已见和5个未见物体上均优于基线。此外，我们证明了早期阶段预测精度提升可增强仿真中的捕捉成功率，并通过真实机器人实验验证了方法的有效性。演示见 https://sites.google.com/view/robot-catching-2025。
Summary / 总结
This study aims to improve quadruped robots' ability to catch diverse in-flight objects by accurately predicting the impact point—the landing position—despite challenges posed by unsteady aerodynamics and similar early trajectories. To address the lack of diverse training data, the authors collect 8,000 real-world trajectories from 20 different objects. They propose OIPP, a two-module system: an Object-Adaptive Encoder (OAE) that learns object-specific motion representations, and an Impact Point Predictor (IPP) with two variants—a Neural Acceleration Estimator (NAE) that infers trajectories and a Direct Point Estimator (DPE) that directly predicts impact points. Experiments show OIPP outperforms baselines on both seen and unseen objects, and early-stage prediction improvements lead to higher catching success in simulation and real-world tests.
本研究旨在提升四足机器人对多种飞行物体的抓取能力，通过准确预测物体着陆点（即冲击点）来实现。为解决缺乏多样且具有复杂空气动力学特性的数据集以及早期轨迹预测困难的问题，作者构建了包含20个物体8000条轨迹的真实世界数据集。提出OIPP系统，包含两个模块：对象自适应编码器用于提取物体特异性运动表征，以及冲击点预测器，其有两种实现方式——一种通过神经加速度估计预测轨迹，另一种直接输出冲击点。实验表明，OIPP在已见和未见物体上均优于基线方法，且早期预测显著提升仿真与真实机器人抓取成功率。
SAGEAgent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Cost-Aware Modality Acquisition in Multimodal Survival Prediction
Authors: Chongyu Qu, Can Cui, Zhengyi Lu, Junchao Zhu, Tianyuan Yao, Junlin Guo, Juming Xiong, Yanfan Zhu, Yuechen Yang, Bennett A. Landman, Yuankai Huo
First: 2026-07-10T15:32:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:32:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Does every cancer patient truly need a complete diagnostic workup for accurate survival prediction? In multimodal clinical oncology, diagnostic modalities follow a clinically mandated order of escalating burden -- from demographics collected at intake to genomic profiling requiring specialized tissue analysis. Current multimodal survival methods either assume all modalities are available or passively handle missing data, but none actively reason about whether acquiring the next modality is justified for a given patient along this ordered workflow. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem and propose SAGEAgent (Sequential Acquisition Guided by Experience), a self-evolving LLM-based clinical agent that decides which diagnostic modalities to acquire for each patient, balancing predictive accuracy against clinical invasiveness. SAGEAgent reasons about each patient's evolving diagnostic state through clinical tools that translate numerical predictions into text, an episodic memory that retrieves similar past cases, and a semantic memory that accumulates reusable decision patterns from experience. Experiments on a glioma cohort combining TCGA-LGG, TCGA-GBM, and BraTS with four diagnostic modalities demonstrate that SAGEAgent achieves competitive survival prediction accuracy while reducing average acquisition burden by 55%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAGEAgent：一种面向多模态生存预测的成本感知模态获取自进化代理
每个癌症患者真的都需要完整的诊断流程来准确预测生存期吗？在多模态临床肿瘤学中，诊断模态遵循临床规定的递增负担顺序——从入院时收集的人口统计信息到需要特殊组织分析的基因组测序。当前的多模态生存预测方法要么假设所有模态均可用，要么被动处理缺失数据，但均未主动判断在该有序工作流中为特定患者获取下一模态是否合理。我们将此问题建模为序列决策问题，并提出SAGEAgent（基于经验引导的序列获取），这是一种自进化的大语言模型临床代理，用于决定为每位患者获取哪些诊断模态，在预测准确性与临床侵入性之间取得平衡。SAGEAgent通过临床工具将数值预测转化为文本、通过情景记忆检索相似历史病例、通过语义记忆积累可复用的决策模式，来推理每位患者的诊断状态演变。在结合TCGA-LGG、TCGA-GBM和BraTS数据集的胶质瘤队列实验中，SAGEAgent在保持竞争力的生存预测精度的同时，将平均获取负担降低了55%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of acquiring all diagnostic modalities for every cancer patient, the authors propose SAGEAgent, a self-evolving LLM-based agent that dynamically decides which modalities to acquire during a sequential clinical workflow, balancing predictive accuracy against patient burden. The agent uses clinical tools to interpret predictions, episodic memory to recall similar past cases, and semantic memory to generalize reusable decision patterns. Experiments on glioma data with four modalities show SAGEAgent matches or exceeds baseline survival prediction accuracy while reducing average acquisition burden by 55%.
为减少对每位癌症患者进行全部诊断模态的冗余操作，作者提出SAGEAgent，这是一种基于自进化LLM的临床代理，能在临床有序工作流中动态决定为每位患者获取哪些诊断模态，平衡预测准确性与临床负担。该代理通过临床工具将数值预测转化为文本，利用情节记忆检索相似历史病例，以及语义记忆积累可复用的决策模式。在整合TCGA-LGG、TCGA-GBM和BraTS数据的胶质瘤队列实验中，SAGEAgent在保持竞争力生存预测精度的同时，平均获取负担降低了55%。
Seeing is Free, Speaking is Not: Uncovering the True Energy Bottleneck in Edge VLM Inference
Authors: Junfei Zhan, Haoxun Shen, Mingang Guo, Zixuan Huang, Tengjiao He
Venue: ACM MM 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:31:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:31:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACM MM 2026. 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are the perceptual backbone of embodied AI, but their energy footprint on edge hardware remains poorly understood. Existing efficiency efforts focus predominantly on reducing visual tokens, implicitly treating visual processing as the dominant energy cost. We overturn this implicit assumption through the first systematic energy profiling of on-device VLM inference, spanning five models across three architecture families, four input resolutions, and two hardware platforms (NVIDIA RTX 3070 and Jetson Orin NX). Our analysis yields three findings. First, average inference power is a model-intrinsic constant, invariant to input resolution, image complexity, and prompt type, with less than 5% variation across all conditions. This means that all energy variation across inputs must arise from variation in inference time, not from variation in power draw. Second, each output token costs 11 to 39x more wall-clock time than each input token due to the compute-bound and memory-bound asymmetry between prefill and decode, making output token count the dominant driver of both latency and energy. Third, image complexity, measured by the number of objects in an image, induces up to 4.1x energy differences at identical resolution. This variation arises not from increased visual processing cost, but from differences in output length. These findings expose a fundamental limitation of visual token pruning: even removing all visual tokens saves at most 10% of total energy for fixed-token models. Across models spanning 1 billion to 8 billion parameters, controlling output length saves up to 97% of total energy, with the energy dominance of decoding growing stronger at larger model scale. In short, the true energy bottleneck in edge VLM inference is not what the model sees, but how much it says.
中文标题/摘要
标题：看见是免费的，说话却不是：揭示边缘视觉语言模型推理中的真实能耗瓶颈
视觉语言模型（VLMs）是具身AI的感知核心，但其在边缘硬件上的能耗仍不甚明了。现有效率优化工作主要聚焦于减少视觉token，隐含地将视觉处理视为主要能耗来源。我们通过首次对设备端VLM推理的系统性能耗分析，颠覆这一隐含假设。分析覆盖五种模型、三种架构、四种输入分辨率和两种硬件平台（NVIDIA RTX 3070与Jetson Orin NX）。结果发现三点：第一，平均推理功耗为模型固有常数，与输入分辨率、图像复杂度和提示类型无关，所有条件下波动小于5%。这意味着输入间能耗差异源于推理时长变化，而非功耗变化。第二，由于预填充与解码阶段的计算与内存瓶颈不对称，每个输出token耗时是输入token的11至39倍，使输出token数量成为延迟与能耗的主要驱动因素。第三，图像复杂度（以图像中物体数量衡量）在相同分辨率下可导致高达4.1倍的能耗差异，但其根源并非视觉处理成本增加，而是输出长度差异。这些发现揭示了视觉token剪枝的根本局限：即使移除所有视觉token，固定token模型最多仅节省10%总能耗。在10亿至80亿参数规模的模型中，控制输出长度可节省高达97%总能耗，且随着模型规模增大，解码阶段能耗主导性愈发显著。简言之，边缘VLM推理的真实能耗瓶颈并非模型所见，而是其所说。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the energy efficiency of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) running on edge devices, challenging the prevailing assumption that visual processing dominates energy consumption. Through systematic energy profiling across five models, three architectures, four resolutions, and two hardware platforms, the authors find that average inference power is nearly constant regardless of input or prompt, meaning energy variation stems from inference time, not power draw.
