DemoBridge: A Simulation-in-the-Loop Toolkit for Single-View Human Demonstration Retargeting
Authors: Zehao Wang, Fabien Despinoy, Sergey Zakharov, Tinne Tuytelaars, Rahaf Aljundi
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:31:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:31:04+00:00
Comments: RSS 2026 RoboData Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present DemoBridge, an toolkit that turns a single-view RGB stereo recording of a human hand demonstration into an executable, physics-validated robot-arm trajectory. Retargeting across the embodiment gap is hard. A robot arm reaches a target with a long, articulated body whose links carry far more collision volume than a hand. Solving inverse kinematics for the mapped end-effector pose often yields no collision-free solution, and a trajectory imposes this at every waypoint. A single view adds noise, leaving the demonstrated reference inaccurate. At the core of DemoBridge is a single collision-aware planner. It optimizes the whole joint trajectory at once, reasoning jointly over alternative grasp poses, whole-arm and grasped-object collision, and fidelity to the demonstrated path. A physics simulator runs in the loop. It validates each phase as it is produced and backtracks on failure, so a demonstration that cannot be reproduced as given is re-planned rather than discarded. The resulting action sequence is dynamically stable and faithful to the demonstrated manipulation. It also doubles as a ready-to-use simulation rollout for policy learning. Grasp timing is inferred automatically, and the perception backends, robot, and pipeline stages are swappable from configuration. We evaluate whole-pipeline retargeting on three real-demonstration tasks and the planner on a controlled synthetic benchmark. Our code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/u0123974/demo-bridge/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：DemoBridge：单视角人体演示重定向的仿真闭环工具包
我们提出了DemoBridge，一个将单视角RGB立体视频中的人手演示转化为可执行且经物理验证的机械臂轨迹的工具包。跨越具身鸿沟的重定向极具挑战性：机械臂拥有长而关节化的身体，其连杆碰撞体积远大于手部。映射末端执行器姿态的逆运动学常无碰撞解，且轨迹在每个关键点均施加此约束。单视角引入噪声，导致演示参考轨迹不准确。DemoBridge的核心是一个单碰撞感知规划器，它一次性优化整个关节轨迹，联合推理多种抓取姿态、整臂与抓取物体的碰撞，以及对演示路径的忠实度。物理模拟器在闭环中运行，逐阶段验证并回溯失败，使无法直接复现的演示被重新规划而非丢弃。生成的动作序列动态稳定且忠实于演示操作，同时可直接用于策略学习的仿真 rollout。抓取时机自动推断，感知后端、机器人及流水线阶段均可通过配置替换。我们在三个真实演示任务上评估完整流程重定向，在受控合成基准上评估规划器。代码见 https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/u0123974/demo-bridge/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of retargeting human hand demonstrations to robot arms due to embodiment mismatch and single-view noise, DemoBridge introduces a collision-aware trajectory planner that jointly optimizes joint motion, grasp poses, and object-arm collisions while preserving fidelity to the demonstrated path. Integrated with a physics simulator in the loop, it validates and re-plans trajectories on failure, ensuring dynamic stability and executable output. Evaluated on three real-world tasks and a synthetic benchmark, the system successfully generates collision-free, physics-validated robot trajectories that match human intent and serve as ready-to-use simulation rollouts for policy learning.
为解决因人体与机器人形态差异及单目视觉噪声导致的人手演示难以转化为无碰撞机器人轨迹的问题，DemoBridge提出一种碰撞感知规划器，联合优化完整关节轨迹，同时考虑抓取姿态、整臂与物体碰撞及对演示路径的忠实度。系统集成物理仿真器于闭环中，对生成轨迹实时验证并失败时回溯重规划，确保动态稳定性和演示一致性。在三个真实任务和合成基准上的实验表明，该方法能有效完成重定向，且可作为策略学习的仿真 rollout 工具。
Learning When to Intervene on Habitual Behaviors: A Case Study in Oral Health Care
Authors: Bhanu Teja Gullapalli, Vivek Shetty, Anna L. Trella, Asim H. Gazi, Susan A. Murphy
First: 2026-07-10T15:28:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:28:15+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central challenge for digital health interventions aimed at improving habitual behaviors is deciding when to deliver an intervention prompt. For many daily habits, such as tooth brushing or eating, individuals tend to act around a usual time of day, but this timing is not fixed and can shift as routines evolve. When intervention timing is selected in advance and held constant throughout a study, it can gradually become misaligned with behavior, causing interventions to potentially arrive after the behavior has already occurred or too early to be effective. In this work, we address this habitual timing misalignment in digital health interventions by proposing an online decision-making framework that continuously adapts intervention timing as individual behavior patterns change. Rather than treating intervention timing as a static design choice, our framework adapts it over time and integrates it into a sequential process that determines both when and whether to deliver an intervention. Using data from a deployed oral health intervention trial as a case study, we evaluate our approach using both observed data and simulated settings to assess how well different intervention timing strategies align with the timing of brushing events. Across these evaluations, we measure performance using a coverage-based metric that captures whether an intervention is delivered sufficiently close to a subsequent brushing event. We find that adaptive intervention timing consistently improves coverage compared to fixed intervention times based on user-provided input. The proposed framework is currently deployed in an ongoing randomized controlled trial of a digital oral health intervention, with preliminary results that are consistent with and further support our prior evaluations.

标题：学习何时干预习惯性行为：口腔健康护理案例研究
数字健康干预旨在改善习惯性行为时，一个核心挑战是决定何时推送干预提示。许多日常习惯（如刷牙或进食）通常在一天中的固定时间进行，但该时间并非固定，会随日常习惯演变而调整。若干预时间预先设定并全程保持不变，会逐渐与行为脱节，导致干预提示可能在行为发生后才送达，或过早而无效。本研究通过提出一个在线决策框架，解决数字健康干预中的习惯性时间错位问题，该框架能持续根据个体行为模式变化调整干预时间。不同于将干预时间视为静态设计选择，我们的框架随时间动态调整干预时间，并将其整合进一个序列决策过程，决定何时以及是否推送干预。我们以一项已部署的口腔健康干预试验数据作为案例研究，通过观察数据和模拟环境评估不同干预时间策略与刷牙事件时间的匹配程度。评估中采用基于覆盖率的指标，衡量干预是否在后续刷牙事件附近及时送达。结果表明，自适应干预时间相比基于用户输入的固定时间，持续提升覆盖率。该框架目前正部署于一项正在进行的数字口腔健康干预随机对照试验中，初步结果与前期评估一致并进一步支持其有效性。

Evaluated using both real-world trial data and simulations, the framework outperformed static timing strategies by significantly improving coverage — a metric measuring how closely interventions align with subsequent brushing events — demonstrating consistent gains across different user patterns. The approach is currently deployed in a randomized controlled trial, with preliminary results supporting its effectiveness.
该研究针对数字健康应用中针对习惯性行为（如刷牙）的干预时机错位问题，提出了一种在线决策框架，根据用户实时行为动态调整干预提示时间，并将时机决策融入是否干预的序列决策中。通过真实口腔健康干预试验数据和模拟环境评估，该框架在覆盖度指标（衡量干预是否贴近实际刷牙行为）上显著优于固定时间策略，且在不同场景下均表现稳定。该方法目前正部署于一项随机对照试验中，初步结果进一步支持其有效性。
One-Shot Multimodal Learning from Demonstration with Force-Constrained Elastic Maps
Authors: Brendan Hertel, Jonathan Spanos, Navya Garg, Reza Azadeh
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:27:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:27:50+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables. Accepted for publication at IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic manipulation tasks often require simultaneous reasoning over motion and contact forces, yet most Learning from Demonstration (LfD) methods model only spatial trajectories and neglect force interactions with the environment. This limitation reduces robustness and can lead to unsafe or inconsistent task reproduction in force-constrained settings. We propose a novel one-shot multimodal LfD framework for the segmentation, encoding, and reproduction of force-inclusive demonstrations. First, we introduce a multimodal probabilistic segmentation method that adaptively weighs spatial and force modalities over time, enabling the automatic extraction of force-aware motion primitives. Second, we extend the elastic maps representation to incorporate external force constraints during skill encoding and formulate a convex optimization procedure for learning force-consistent trajectory models. The resulting skills reproduce both motion and contact characteristics from a single demonstration while promoting safer execution by accounting for demonstrated force profiles. We validate our approach on five real-world manipulation tasks across two distinct force-sensing configurations: wrist force sensing on a UR5e with a Robotiq 2f-85 gripper and finger force sensing on a Kinova Gen3 with an Openhand Model O gripper. Experimental results demonstrate robust multimodal segmentation, accurate force-aware reproduction, and cross-platform generality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于力约束弹性地图的单次多模态演示学习
机器人操作任务通常需要同时推理运动与接触力，但大多数演示学习（LfD）方法仅建模空间轨迹，忽略与环境的力交互。这一局限性降低了鲁棒性，可能导致在力约束环境下任务复现不安全或不一致。我们提出一种新颖的单次多模态LfD框架，用于包含力信息的演示的分割、编码与复现。首先，我们引入一种多模态概率分割方法，自适应地在时间维度上权衡空间与力模态，实现力感知运动原语的自动提取。其次，我们将弹性地图表示扩展为在技能编码过程中纳入外部力约束，并设计凸优化过程以学习力一致的轨迹模型。所生成的技能可从单次演示中复现运动与接触特性，同时通过考虑演示中的力分布促进更安全的执行。我们在两种不同力传感配置下五个真实世界操作任务中验证了该方法：UR5e机器人配Robotiq 2f-85夹爪的腕部力传感，以及Kinova Gen3机器人配Openhand Model O夹爪的指部力传感。实验结果表明，该方法实现了鲁棒的多模态分割、精确的力感知复现及跨平台通用性。

Motivated by the need to model both motion and contact forces in robotic manipulation, this paper proposes a one-shot multimodal Learning from Demonstration framework that integrates force data with spatial trajectories. 为解决现有学习演示方法忽略力交互导致的鲁棒性不足和执行不安全问题，本文提出一种单次演示的多模态学习框架，将力信息融入技能编码。该方法采用概率分割算法自适应加权空间与力信号，提取力感知运动原语；并扩展弹性地图表示，通过凸优化引入外部力约束，实现力一致轨迹重建。在五类真实操作任务和两种力传感配置下实验表明，该方法具备鲁棒的多模态分割、精确的力感知复现能力，并具有跨平台泛化性。
Failure as a Process: An Anatomy of CLI Coding Agent Trajectories
Authors: Xiangxin Zhao, Han Li, Shuaiting Li, Tianyi Zhao, Earl T. Barr, Federica Sarro, He Ye
First: 2026-07-10T15:25:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:25:56+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) coding agents are increasingly deployed to autonomously perform software engineering tasks in terminal-based environments, making their reliability a growing concern. Existing empirical studies investigate why coding agents fail, yet they largely treat failure as a final outcome rather than a temporal process, providing limited insight into how failures emerge, evolve, and become unrecoverable. We present the first large-scale empirical study of CLI coding-agent failure trajectories, introducing a process-oriented framework that analyzes failure through its onset, evolution, and recovery across execution trajectories. We first collect 3,843 execution trajectories generated by seven frontier models across three coding-agent scaffolds (OpenHands, MiniSWE, and Terminus2) on Terminal-Bench, then carefully filter them to obtain 1,794 complete and valid trajectories for manual annotation (over 63,000 execution steps), from which we derive 14 findings spanning failure occurrence, root causes, recovery, and cross-system consistency. Our findings show that coding-agent failures are predominantly driven by epistemic errors, typically begin within the first few execution steps, and often remain hidden until recovery is no longer possible, suggesting that improving coding-agent reliability requires earlier validation and intervention rather than relying solely on final-outcome evaluation.

标题：失败作为过程：CLI编码代理轨迹的剖析
大型语言模型（LLM）编码代理正越来越多地被部署在基于终端的环境中自主执行软件工程任务，其可靠性日益成为关注焦点。现有实证研究探讨编码代理为何失败，但大多将失败视为最终结果，而非随时间演变的过程，因而对失败如何产生、演化并最终不可恢复的机制缺乏深入洞察。本文首次开展大规模实证研究，分析CLI编码代理的失败轨迹，提出一种以过程为导向的框架，从失败的起始、演化与恢复三个维度分析执行轨迹。我们首先收集了在Terminal-Bench基准上，七种前沿模型在三个编码代理框架（OpenHands、MiniSWE、Terminus2）生成的3,843条执行轨迹，经仔细筛选后获得1,794条完整有效的轨迹用于人工标注（超过63,000个执行步骤），从中提炼出14项发现，涵盖失败发生、根本原因、恢复机制及跨系统一致性。研究发现，编码代理的失败主要由认识性错误驱动，通常在前几步执行中即已开始，且常在恢复无望时才显现，表明提升编码代理可靠性需更早进行验证与干预，而非仅依赖最终结果评估。

Motivated by the need to understand how coding agents fail over time in terminal environments, this study introduces a process-oriented framework to analyze failure trajectories rather than treating failure as a static endpoint. Using 1,794 manually annotated execution trajectories from seven frontier models across three coding-agent scaffolds, the researchers identify 14 key findings, revealing that failures are mostly caused by epistemic errors, often emerge early in execution, and frequently remain undetected until recovery becomes impossible. 为理解终端环境中代码代理如何随时间失败，本研究提出一种面向过程的框架，将失败视为动态演变而非静态结果进行分析。通过对七种前沿模型在三个框架上生成的1,794条人工标注执行轨迹（含63,000+步骤）进行研究，作者发现：失败主要源于认识性错误，通常在执行初期即出现，且常在无法恢复时才被察觉。结果表明，提升代码代理可靠性应侧重早期验证与干预，而非仅依赖最终结果评估。
DGSfM: Depth-Guided Scale-Aware Global Structure-from-Motion
Authors: Sithu Aung, Viktor Kocur, Yaqing Ding, Torsten Sattler, Zuzana Kukelova
First: 2026-07-10T15:18:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:18:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Global Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is an efficient paradigm for recovering camera poses and sparse 3D structure from unordered images. However, its reliance on scale-ambiguous epipolar geometry makes global positioning sensitive to noisy baseline estimates and weak view-graph constraints, while false edges from visually ambiguous pairs can further degrade reconstruction. We propose DGSfM, a depth-aware global SfM pipeline that uses monocular depth maps as a scalable prior while preserving explicit multi-view optimization. For each image pair, we use a depth-aware relative pose solver to convert scale-ambiguous epipolar constraints into scale-aware relative pose constraints. We further improve robustness through view-graph filtering and depth-consistency-based correspondence pruning, which suppress false edges and matches that remain plausible under epipolar geometry alone. Finally, global scale averaging and depth-guided pose-point initialization align monocular depth maps into a common reconstruction scale and provide stable initialization for global positioning and bundle adjustment. Experiments on ETH3D and IMC2021 show that DGSfM consistently improves over strong global SfM baselines across sparse and dense matching front-ends, achieving substantial gains in pose accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/sithu31296/DGSfM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DGSfM：深度引导的尺度感知全局结构光流法
全局结构光流法（SfM）是一种从无序图像中高效恢复相机姿态和稀疏3D结构的有效范式。然而，其依赖于尺度模糊的极线几何，使得全局定位对噪声基线估计和弱视图图约束敏感，而视觉模糊图像对产生的假边还可进一步降低重建质量。我们提出DGSfM，一种深度感知的全局SfM流程，利用单目深度图作为可扩展先验，同时保留显式的多视角优化。对每对图像，我们使用深度感知的相对位姿求解器，将尺度模糊的极线约束转化为尺度感知的相对位姿约束。我们进一步通过视图图过滤和基于深度一致性的对应关系剪枝提升鲁棒性，抑制仅凭极线几何仍可能成立的假边和匹配。最后，全局尺度平均和深度引导的位姿-点初始化将单目深度图对齐至统一重建尺度，并为全局定位和束调整提供稳定初始化。在ETH3D和IMC2021上的实验表明，DGSfM在稀疏和稠密匹配前端上均优于强全局SfM基线，显著提升姿态精度。代码见https://github.com/sithu31296/DGSfM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of global SfM to scale ambiguity and noisy view-graph constraints, DGSfM introduces a depth-guided approach that leverages monocular depth maps to resolve scale uncertainty while preserving multi-view optimization. For each image pair, it employs a depth-aware relative pose solver to transform epipolar constraints into scale-aware ones, then refines results via view-graph filtering and depth-consistency pruning to eliminate false matches. Finally, global scale averaging and depth-guided initialization align depth maps and stabilize global bundle adjustment. Experiments on ETH3D and IMC2021 demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, with significant gains in pose accuracy regardless of matching front-end choice.
为解决全局SfM对尺度模糊和弱视图图约束敏感的问题，DGSfM提出一种基于深度引导的尺度感知方法，利用单目深度图作为可扩展先验，同时保留多视角优化。对每对图像，通过深度感知相对位姿求解器将尺度模糊的极线约束转化为尺度感知约束，并通过视图图过滤和深度一致性匹配剪枝提升鲁棒性，剔除虚假对应。最后通过全局尺度平均和深度引导位姿-点初始化，将单目深度图对齐至统一重建尺度，稳定全局定位与束调整。在ETH3D和IMC2021数据集上的实验表明，该方法在稀疏与稠密匹配前端下均显著优于现有强基线，大幅提高位姿精度。
What VGGT Knows About Overlap: Probing Geometric Foundation Models for Co-Visibility
Authors: Filippo Ziliotto, Luciano Serafini, Lamberto Ballan, Tommaso Campari
First: 2026-07-10T15:17:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:17:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A fundamental challenge in 3D reconstruction and robotic localization is co-visibility: determining which image pairs share overlapping visible surfaces, particularly in scenarios with minimal overlap. We demonstrate that VGGT implicitly encodes co-visibility as an emergent behavior: without any supervision for this task, its internal representations exhibit a clear hierarchical structure mirroring that of large language models, i.e. early layers build a 3D-aware scene representation, while late layers act as dedicated co-visibility reasoners. In particular, we identify layer L17 as a negative anchor that consistently routes non-co-visible pairs for this backbone, regardless of the evaluation setting, providing task-grounded evidence of layer specialization in a geometry-grounded foundation model. Building on this, we introduce Co-VGGT, which freezes VGGT and trains only a lightweight layer-wise mixture-of-experts head (less than 7.5M parameters) to classify co-visibility from RGB alone, treating each layer as a specialized expert whose geometric abstraction is adaptively weighted per input pair. On the Co-VisiON benchmark, Co-VGGT surpasses the human annotation baseline and improves over prior work by more than 25% pairwise and 10% multiview. Pairwise predictions are well-calibrated (ECE=0.030), enabling direct use as edge weights in visibility graphs for downstream SfM and SLAM pipelines without post-hoc correction. Code and data are available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VGGT对重叠区域的认知：探查几何基础模型的共可见性
3D重建与机器人定位中的基本挑战是共可见性：确定哪些图像对共享可见表面，特别是在重叠区域极小的情况下。我们证明VGGT隐式地将共可见性编码为一种涌现行为：无需对此任务进行任何监督，其内部表示展现出与大型语言模型相似的层次结构，即早期层构建3D感知场景表示，而后期层则作为专门的共可见性推理器。特别地，我们识别出L17层为负锚点，无论评估设置如何，该层始终将非共可见图像对路由至该主干网络，为几何基础模型中层专业化提供了任务导向的实证依据。在此基础上，我们提出Co-VGGT，冻结VGGT并仅训练一个轻量级的层间专家混合头部（参数量少于750万），仅根据RGB图像分类共可见性，将每一层视为一个专业化专家，其几何抽象根据输入图像对自适应加权。在Co-VisiON基准测试中，Co-VGGT超越了人工标注基线，并在成对预测上比先前工作提升超过25%，多视角预测提升10%。成对预测具有良好的校准（ECE=0.030），可直接用于可见性图的边权重，无需事后校正，即可无缝集成至下游SfM和SLAM流程。代码与数据已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect co-visibility in 3D reconstruction and robotic localization, especially under minimal overlap, this work probes VGGT to uncover its implicit geometric understanding. The authors find that VGGT’s internal layers exhibit a hierarchical structure where early layers encode 3D scene geometry and later layers specialize in co-visibility reasoning, with layer L17 acting as a consistent negative anchor. Building on this, they introduce Co-VGGT—a lightweight, layer-wise mixture-of-experts head trained on top of frozen VGGT—that adapts expert weights per input pair to classify co-visibility from RGB alone. 为解决3D重建与机器人定位中在重叠度极低情况下判断图像对是否共可见的难题，该研究探索视觉基础模型VGGT是否隐式编码了共可见性能力。研究发现VGGT内部层结构具有层次性：早期层构建3D场景表征，后期层（如第17层）则作为专门的共可见性推理器，稳定地将非共可见对路由出去。在此基础上，作者提出Co-VGGT，冻结VGGT并添加轻量级层间MoE头部，仅用RGB输入进行共可见性分类，利用各层的几何抽象能力按输入对自适应加权。在Co-VisiON基准上，Co-VGGT超越人工标注基线25%以上（成对精度）和10%（多视角精度），预测校准良好（ECE=0.030），可直接用于SfM与SLAM流程中的可见性图边权计算，无需后处理修正。
All Explanations are Wrong, But Many Are Useful: Exploring the Rashomon Explanation Set with Large Language Models
Authors: Pan Li
First: 2026-07-10T15:16:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:16:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explaining machine-learning models is increasingly important for decision-making and consumer trust, yet it is widely believed to come at a cost: existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods suffer from a persistent accuracy-explainability trade-off. We argue that this trade-off is not fundamental, but an artifact of treating explanation and prediction as separate objectives; when properly coupled, they become complementary, so that equipping a model to explain itself improves, rather than degrades, its accuracy. We introduce the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which builds a set of faithful, prediction-guiding explanations rather than a single one, and prove that this set is generally non-empty and that explanation fidelity bounds the performance of the models it guides. To explore this set, we propose RashomonLLM, an Explanation-Prediction-Reflection agentic workflow that generates explanations in natural language by iteratively aligning them with predictions, and we prove it converges and recovers the full set. Across customer-churn classification, clinical survival regression, and industrial click-through prediction on large-scale live-streaming logs, RashomonLLM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prediction and XAI baselines on both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by explanation fidelity and robust to distribution shifts, temporal splits, and seeds. Our framework thus advances business performance while laying the groundwork for consumer trust.
中文标题/摘要
标题：所有解释都错误，但许多都有用：利用大语言模型探索拉什莫恩解释集
解释机器学习模型对决策和消费者信任日益重要，但普遍认为这会带来代价：现有可解释人工智能（XAI）方法存在持续的准确性-可解释性权衡。我们认为，这种权衡并非根本性问题，而是将解释与预测视为独立目标所致；当二者恰当耦合时，它们互补，使模型具备自我解释能力反而能提升而非降低其准确性。我们提出拉什莫恩解释范式，构建一组忠实且引导预测的解释，而非单一解释，并证明该集合通常非空，且解释保真度限制其引导模型的性能。为探索该集合，我们提出拉什莫恩LLM，一种解释-预测-反思的代理工作流，通过迭代对齐自然语言解释与预测生成解释，并证明其收敛并恢复完整集合。在客户流失分类、临床生存回归及工业点击率预测（基于大规模直播日志）中，拉什莫恩LLM在准确性和解释质量上显著优于现有预测与XAI基线，性能提升源于解释保真度，且对分布偏移、时间分割和随机种子鲁棒。我们的框架既提升商业表现，也为消费者信任奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the perceived trade-off between model accuracy and explainability in XAI, this work argues that explanations and predictions are complementary when properly integrated. It introduces the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which generates a set of faithful, prediction-aligned explanations rather than a single one, and proves this set is non-empty and bounded by explanation fidelity. The method, RashomonLLM, uses an iterative Explanation-Prediction-Reflection workflow to generate natural-language explanations that align with model predictions, converging to recover the full set. Experiments across customer churn, clinical survival, and streaming click-through tasks show RashomonLLM significantly outperforms baselines in both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by fidelity and robustness to distribution shifts and temporal splits.
该研究挑战了将解释与预测视为互斥目标的传统观点，提出Rashomon解释范式，认为解释与预测可互补而非互斥。方法RashomonLLM通过解释-预测-反思的迭代工作流，利用大语言模型生成与预测对齐的自然语言解释，并收敛至完整解释集合。在客户流失、临床生存和直播点击率预测三个真实场景中，RashomonLLM在准确率和解释质量上显著优于现有基线，性能提升源于解释保真度，且对分布偏移、时间分割和随机种子具有鲁棒性。
Normalisation-Based Likelihood Ratio Estimation for Forensic Authorship Verification
Authors: Sadie Barlow, Andrea Nini, Edoardo Manino
First: 2026-07-10T15:16:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:16:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Authorship verification (AV) is the task of determining whether two texts were written by the same author. In a forensic context, the strength of AV evidence can be quantified using likelihood ratios. Most AV methods are score-based and deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios from these scores requires a separate calibration model. This, in turn, requires additional amounts of case-relevant data, which is often time-consuming to obtain and prepare. This study proposes two novel normalisation techniques, the Square Root Correction and the Hapax Correction, for deriving likelihood ratios from the AV method LambdaG without the need of a calibration model (Nini et al. 2026). These corrections are designed to mitigate the overestimation of evidential strength that may result from long or highly repetitive texts. Performance is evaluated against logistic regression calibration across fifteen corpora and a range of text lengths (100-9,500 tokens), using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr). The proposed methods achieve performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in approximately 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). Furthermore, performance was more frequently close (within 5%) when the Hapax Correction was outperformed by logistic regression calibration, compared with the reverse comparison. Eliminating the need to train a calibration model reduces data-requirements, time and complexity, thereby increasing the accessibility and transparency of forensic text comparison. This combination of empirical performance and practical advantages supports the adoption of the proposed methods in forensic settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于归一化的似然比估计用于法医作者身份验证
作者身份验证（AV）旨在判断两段文本是否由同一作者撰写。在法医语境下，AV证据的强度可通过似然比量化。多数AV方法基于评分，从这些评分推导出良好校准的似然比需依赖独立校准模型，而该模型需额外案例相关数据，获取与准备常耗时费力。本研究提出两种新颖归一化技术——平方根校正与hapax校正，用于从LambdaG方法直接推导似然比，无需校准模型（Nini等，2026）。这些校正旨在缓解长文本或高度重复文本导致的证据强度高估问题。性能在十五个语料库及多种文本长度（100-9,500词元）下，通过对数似然比成本（Cllr）与逻辑回归校准对比评估。所提方法性能与逻辑回归校准相当，其中hapax校正约45%测试（按语料库加权）表现更优。此外，当hapax校正被逻辑回归校准超越时，其性能更常接近后者（误差在5%内），反之则较少。消除校准模型训练需求可降低数据、时间和复杂度，提升法医文本比对的可及性与透明度。该方法兼具实证性能与实用优势，支持其在法医场景中的应用。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of deriving well-calibrated likelihood ratios for forensic authorship verification without requiring separate calibration models, which typically demand scarce case-relevant data. It introduces two normalization techniques—Square Root Correction and Hapax Correction—to adjust outputs from the LambdaG AV method, specifically to counteract overestimation in long or repetitive texts. Evaluated across 15 corpora and text lengths from 100 to 9,500 tokens using the log-likelihood ratio cost (Cllr), both methods achieved performance comparable to logistic regression calibration, with the Hapax Correction outperforming it in 45% of tests (weighted by corpora). When the Hapax Correction was inferior, its performance was often within 5% of logistic regression, indicating robustness. The elimination of calibration modeling reduces data needs, time, and complexity, enhancing accessibility and transparency in forensic applications.
为解决法证作者身份验证中校准模型所需大量案例相关数据及复杂性问题，本研究提出两种归一化方法——平方根校正与hapax校正，可直接从LambdaG评分方法中推导似然比，无需额外校准。在15个语料库、文本长度从100至9500词元范围内，使用对数似然比成本（Cllr）评估，两种方法表现与逻辑回归校准相当，其中hapax校正方法在45%的测试中（按语料库加权）表现更优。当hapax校正表现较差时，其性能通常仅比逻辑回归校准低5%以内，表明其稳健性与实用性。通过省去校准步骤，该方法降低数据需求、计算负担，提升透明度，适合法证场景应用。
AffordanceSAM: Segment Anything Once More in Affordance Grounding
Authors: Dengyang Jiang, Zanyi Wang, Hengzhuang Li, Sizhe Dang, Teli Ma, Wei Wei, Guang Dai, Lei Zhang, Harry Yang, Mengmeng Wang
Venue: ACM MM 2026
First: 2025-04-22T07:16:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:14:57+00:00
Comments: [ACM MM 2026] SAM Meets Affordance Grounding
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building a generalized affordance grounding model to identify actionable regions on objects is vital for real-world applications. Existing methods to train the model can be divided into weakly and fully supervised ways. However, the former method requires a complex training framework design and can not infer new actions without an auxiliary prior. While the latter often struggle with limited annotated data and components trained from scratch despite being simpler. This study focuses on fully supervised affordance grounding and overcomes its limitations by proposing AffordanceSAM, which extends SAM's generalization capacity in segmentation to affordance grounding. Specifically, we design an affordance-adaption module and curate a coarse-to-fine annotated dataset called C2F-Aff to thoroughly transfer SAM's robust performance to affordance in a three-stage training manner. Experimental results confirm that AffordanceSAM achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on the AGD20K benchmark and exhibits strong generalized capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AffordanceSAM：在功能接地中再次应用分割任意物
构建能够识别物体上可执行区域的通用功能接地模型，对现实世界应用至关重要。现有训练方法可分为弱监督和全监督两类。前者需复杂训练框架设计，且无辅助先验无法推断新动作；后者虽更简单，但常受限于标注数据不足，且从零训练组件效果不佳。本研究聚焦全监督功能接地，通过提出AffordanceSAM克服其局限，该方法将SAM在分割中的泛化能力扩展至功能接地。具体而言，我们设计了功能适配模块，并构建了名为C2F-Aff的粗到细标注数据集，通过三阶段训练方式充分迁移SAM的鲁棒性能至功能接地。实验结果表明，AffordanceSAM在AGD20K基准上达到当前最优（SOTA）性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalized affordance grounding for real-world applications, this study addresses the limitations of existing fully supervised methods that suffer from scarce annotated data and poor generalization. It proposes AffordanceSAM, which adapts the segmentation capabilities of SAM to affordance grounding through an affordance-adaption module and a new coarse-to-fine annotated dataset, C2F-Aff, trained in three stages. Experimental results show that AffordanceSAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the AGD20K benchmark and demonstrates strong generalization across unseen objects and actions.
为解决现实应用中通用动作区域识别的需求，本研究针对现有全监督方法面临标注数据稀缺与泛化能力不足的问题，提出AffordanceSAM模型，通过三阶段训练机制将SAM的分割能力迁移到动作接地任务，引入动作适配模块与新标注数据集C2F-Aff。实验表明，AffordanceSAM在AGD20K基准上达到当前最优性能，并展现出优异的跨物体与动作泛化能力。
The LLMbda Calculus: AI Agents, Conversations, and Information Flow
Authors: Zac Garby, Andrew D. Gordon, David Sands
First: 2026-02-23T17:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:10:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents: they plan, call tools, read untrusted data, and act on the results. This exposes them to prompt injection: data meant only to be read is obeyed as an instruction. The most principled defences replace content inspection with provenance: classifying data by source and keeping trusted and untrusted apart through a separation of duty (the dual-LLM pattern) and information-flow control. Yet the leading systems are hard to fully trust: flow tracking is easy to get wrong, deliberate relaxations are hard to audit, and the dual-LLM pattern is hard-wired into the architecture. We present LLMbda, an untyped call-by-value lambda calculus that makes provenance-based defence both expressible and provably sound, without committing to an architecture. It adds the operational core of agentic systems as first-class constructs: prompt-response conversations that can be forked and cleared, code generation, and dynamic information-flow control in which every value carries a label that every reduction propagates. Isolation becomes a policy a program expresses, and reclassification an explicit, auditable construct. Our central result is a termination-insensitive probabilistic noninterference theorem over the whole calculus, including code-generating agents, with an insulated variant that holds even when the attacker chooses all untrusted inputs. The verified interpreter is itself the harness that calls the model, to our knowledge the first LLM agent harness whose executable is the subject of machine-checked security theorems, so every agent inherits the guarantee. On the AgentDojo banking benchmark, an agent built within LLMbda, enforcement always on, matches the utility of CaMeL, a leading dual-LLM defence, run without its policy checks (which halve its utility), and resists all but two of 1296 attacked runs. Our harness and all proofs are in Lean.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型的λ演算：AI代理、对话与信息流
大型语言模型正越来越多地作为代理部署：它们规划、调用工具、读取不可信数据并根据结果采取行动。这使它们面临提示注入攻击：本仅用于阅读的数据被当作指令执行。最根本的防御方法是用数据来源的可追溯性替代内容检查：通过职责分离（双LLM模式）和信息流控制，将可信与不可信数据区分开来。然而，主流系统难以完全信任：流跟踪容易出错，有意放宽措施难以审计，而双LLM模式则硬编码于架构中。我们提出LLMbda，一种无类型按值调用的λ演算，它使基于可追溯性的防御既可表达又可证明正确，且不绑定特定架构。它将代理系统的核心操作作为一等构造引入：可分叉和清除的提示-响应对话、代码生成，以及动态信息流控制——每个值都带有标签，每次化简都会传播该标签。隔离成为程序表达的策略，重分类则成为显式、可审计的构造。我们的核心成果是在整个演算中（包括代码生成代理）证明了终止无关的概率非干扰定理，并提供一个即使攻击者选择所有不可信输入仍成立的变体。经验证的解释器本身即为调用模型的框架，据我们所知，这是首个其可执行代码受机器验证安全定理保障的LLM代理框架，因此每个代理均继承该保障。在AgentDojo银行基准测试中，基于LLMbda构建的代理在始终启用强制执行的情况下，其效用与CaMeL（领先的双LLM防御系统）在禁用其策略检查（使其效用减半）时相当，并能抵御1296次攻击中的1294次。我们的框架及所有证明均用Lean实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks of prompt injection in large language model agents, this work introduces LLMbda, an untyped call-by-value lambda calculus that formalizes provenance-based defenses through first-class constructs for conversations, code generation, and dynamic information flow control. Each value carries a label that propagates through reductions, enabling explicit, auditable isolation policies and reclassification. The calculus supports a termination-insensitive probabilistic noninterference theorem, even under adversarial input selection, and its verified interpreter acts as a harness calling the model, making it the first LLM agent harness with machine-checked security guarantees. Experiments on the AgentDojo banking benchmark show that an LLMbda agent with enforcement always on matches the utility of CaMeL without its policy checks and resists 1294 out of 1296 attacks.
为应对大型语言模型代理中提示注入的安全风险，本文提出LLMbda，一种无类型按值求值的λ演算，通过将对话、代码生成和动态信息流控制作为一等构造，形式化基于来源的防御机制。每个值携带标签并在归约中传播，使隔离和重分类可显式审计。该演算支持终止无关的概率非干扰定理，即使在攻击者选择所有非可信输入时仍成立，其经过验证的解释器作为LLM代理的安全框架，是首个其可执行代码经机器验证安全定理的框架。实验表明，LLMbda代理在AgentDojo银行基准测试中，始终启用防护时，其效用可媲美无策略检查的CaMeL系统，且成功抵御1296次攻击中的1294次。
Near-optimal Delta-convex Estimation of Lipschitz Functions
Authors: Gábor Balázs
Venue: Journal of Machine Learning Research, 27(134):1-41, 2026
First: 2025-11-19T17:02:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:07:59+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a tractable algorithm for estimating an unknown Lipschitz function from noisy observations and establishes an upper bound on its convergence rate. The approach extends max-affine methods from convex shape-restricted regression to the more general Lipschitz setting. A key component is a nonlinear feature expansion that maps max-affine functions into a subclass of delta-convex functions, which act as universal approximators of Lipschitz functions while preserving their Lipschitz constants. Leveraging this property, the estimator attains the minimax convergence rate (up to logarithmic factors) with respect to the intrinsic dimension of the data under squared loss and subgaussian distributions in the random design setting. The algorithm integrates adaptive partitioning to capture intrinsic dimension, a penalty-based regularization mechanism that removes the need to know the true Lipschitz constant, and a two-stage optimization procedure combining a convex initialization with local refinement. The framework is also straightforward to adapt to convex shape-restricted regression. Experiments demonstrate competitive performance relative to other theoretically justified methods, including nearest-neighbor and kernel-based regressors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lipschitz函数的近最优Delta-凸估计
本文提出了一种从噪声观测中估计未知Lipschitz函数的可行算法，并建立了其收敛速率的上界。该方法将凸形状受限回归中的max-affine方法推广到更一般的Lipschitz情形。关键步骤是通过非线性特征扩展，将max-affine函数映射为delta-凸函数的子类，这些函数可作为Lipschitz函数的通用逼近器，同时保持其Lipschitz常数。利用这一性质，在随机设计设定下，该估计器在平方损失和子高斯分布条件下，相对于数据的内在维度达到最小最大收敛速率（至多乘以对数因子）。算法结合自适应划分以捕捉内在维度，基于惩罚的正则化机制无需知道真实Lipschitz常数，以及结合凸初始化与局部优化的两阶段优化过程。该框架也易于适应凸形状受限回归。实验表明，该方法在与其他理论支持的算法（如最近邻和核回归器）相比时表现具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient estimation of Lipschitz functions under noise, this paper develops a tractable algorithm that extends max-affine methods to the broader Lipschitz setting via a nonlinear feature expansion mapping max-affine functions into delta-convex functions, which serve as universal approximators preserving Lipschitz constants. The method employs adaptive partitioning to estimate intrinsic dimension, penalty-based regularization to eliminate dependence on the true Lipschitz constant, and a two-stage optimization combining convex initialization with local refinement. Experiments show the estimator achieves near-minimax convergence rates under squared loss and subgaussian noise, outperforming or matching other theoretically grounded methods like nearest-neighbor and kernel regressors in practical settings.
为在噪声下高效估计Lipschitz函数，本文提出一种可计算的算法，通过非线性特征映射将max-affine方法扩展至更广泛的Lipschitz情形，将其映射为保持Lipschitz常数的delta-凸函数，作为Lipschitz函数的通用逼近器。该估计器在平方损失下、子高斯噪声与随机设计条件下，达到最小最大收敛速率（至对数因子），通过自适应分区捕捉内在维度、基于惩罚的正则化消除对真实Lipschitz常数的依赖，并结合凸初始化与局部优化。实验表明其性能与近邻和核回归等理论支撑方法相当。
Shared Selective Persistent Memory for Agentic LLM Systems
Authors: Sanjana Pedada, Aditya Dhavala, Neelraj Patil
First: 2026-07-10T15:07:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:07:00+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic LLM systems that generate code through multi-turn tool use face a fundamental context problem: each session starts from zero, discarding the configuration choices, domain constraints, data schemas, and tool-use patterns that made previous sessions productive. Naively persisting entire conversation histories is token-inefficient and counterproductive: irrelevant context degrades generation quality. We introduce shared selective persistent memory, an architecture that identifies and retains four categories of reusable context (task specifications, data schemas, tool configurations, and output constraints) while discarding session-specific reasoning traces. Crucially, this memory is shared: workspaces encapsulating selective memory can be transferred across users with role-based access control, enabling collaborative reuse without redundant specification. We implement it in a deployed collaborative workspace platform where LLM agents produce, edit, and maintain git-versioned artifacts (dashboards, reports, and data-driven documents) from heterogeneous sources (CSV, SQL, REST APIs, and MCP servers). A complementary zero-token data refresh mechanism decouples generated programs from runtime data, enabling artifact reuse without re-invocation. Across three enterprise scenarios, shared selective persistent memory achieves 96% task completion (vs. 79% without memory and 71% with full history). Zero-token refresh eliminates LLM re-invocation for recurring updates (14x task-time reduction), while summary-driven generation cuts per-invocation token cost by 97x versus raw data injection. A replication on four public datasets confirms generalizability, with zero-token refresh succeeding in 12/12 trials. Notably, naive full-history persistence actively degrades completion by biasing the agent with stale traces, while selective memory outperforms both extremes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向代理式大语言模型系统的共享选择性持久化内存
通过多轮工具调用生成代码的代理式大语言模型系统面临根本性上下文问题：每个会话从零开始，丢弃了使前序会话高效运行的配置选择、领域约束、数据模式和工具使用模式。简单地持久化完整对话历史既浪费token又适得其反：无关上下文会降低生成质量。我们引入共享选择性持久化内存架构，识别并保留四类可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置和输出约束），同时丢弃会话特定的推理轨迹。关键在于，该内存是共享的：封装选择性内存的工作区可通过基于角色的访问控制在用户间转移，实现协作复用而无需重复指定。我们在一个已部署的协作工作平台中实现该架构，其中大语言模型代理从异构数据源（CSV、SQL、REST API和MCP服务器）生成、编辑和维护git版本化产物（仪表板、报告和数据驱动文档）。配套的零token数据刷新机制将生成程序与运行时数据解耦，实现产物复用而无需重新调用。在三个企业场景中，共享选择性持久化内存实现96%任务完成率（无内存时为79%，完整历史时为71%）。零token刷新消除重复调用（任务时间减少14倍），基于摘要的生成将每次调用的token成本降低97倍（相比直接注入原始数据）。在四个公开数据集上的复现验证了其泛化能力，零token刷新在12次试验中全部成功。值得注意的是，简单完整历史持久化会因引入过时轨迹而主动降低完成率，而选择性内存优于两者极端。
Summary / 总结
To address the context loss problem in agentic LLM systems that generate code through multi-turn tool use, the paper proposes shared selective persistent memory, which selectively retains reusable context categories—task specifications, data schemas, tool configurations, and output constraints—while discarding session-specific reasoning traces. This memory is shared across users via role-based access control, enabling collaborative reuse without redundant specification. Implemented in a deployed workspace platform handling git-versioned artifacts from heterogeneous sources, the system also includes a zero-token data refresh mechanism to decouple generated programs from runtime data. Experimental results across three enterprise scenarios show 96% task completion with selective memory (vs. 79% without memory and 71% with full history), 14x task-time reduction via zero-token refresh, and 97x token cost reduction via summary-driven generation. Replication on four public datasets confirms generalizability, and naive full-history persistence was shown to degrade performance by biasing agents with stale traces.
为解决多轮工具调用中LLM代理系统因每次会话从零开始导致的上下文丢失问题，本文提出共享选择性持久记忆架构，仅保留任务规范、数据模式、工具配置和输出约束四类可复用上下文，剔除会话特定推理轨迹。该记忆支持基于角色的访问控制，实现跨用户协作复用而无需重复指定。在支持从CSV、SQL、REST API等异构源生成Git版本化产物（如仪表板、报告）的部署平台中实现，并引入零令牌数据刷新机制，使生成程序与运行时数据解耦。三类企业场景实验显示，选择性记忆使任务完成率达96%（无记忆为79%，全历史为71%），零令牌刷新减少14倍任务时间，摘要驱动生成降低97倍单次调用令牌成本。在四个公开数据集上的复现实验全部成功，且全历史持久化反而因引入过时轨迹降低性能，而选择性记忆优于二者。
Multimodal Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Jiayu Yao, Yiwei Wang, Anmeng Zhang, Zhe Sun, Songsong Wang, Lingrui Mei, Yuyao Ge, Shenghua Liu
First: 2026-07-10T15:06:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:06:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs), but higher rewards do not always imply better task performance. This risk is amplified when visual evidence is evaluated by text-only or weakly grounded rewards. We study reward hacking in MLLM RL across safety VQA, chart VQA, and stress-test settings, varying reward design, data ambiguity, model scale (2B-32B), and RL algorithm (GRPO, RLOO, DAPO). We introduce Newly Rewarded Failure Rate (NRFR), which measures failures among samples whose proxy reward improves over the SFT baseline. Outcome-only rewards cause severe hacking, reaching 48.1% Reward Hacking Rate (RHR), while NRFR exceeding RHR shows that RL creates new failures rather than merely inheriting them. Scaling reduces but does not eliminate hacking: even the 32B model retains a 54.9% worse rate under outcome-only rewards, whereas answer-aware rewards improve the oracle trend at every scale. Robustness is also algorithm- and scale-dependent: GRPO is consistently most resistant, RLOO remains vulnerable, and DAPO improves substantially from 2B to 8B. Visual-evidence rewards help only with reliable verification: keyword-based checks increase hacking, while VLM-as-judge semantic verification reduces it. Overall, multimodal reward hacking is a systematic result of optimizing imperfect rewards, and robust alignment requires rewards and verifiers that remain reliable under optimization pressure.

强化学习（RL）正越来越多地用于对齐多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），但更高的奖励并不总意味着更好的任务表现。当视觉证据由纯文本或弱关联奖励评估时，此风险加剧。我们研究了在安全VQA、图表VQA及压力测试场景下，MLLM RL中的奖励欺骗，变量包括奖励设计、数据模糊性、模型规模（2B-32B）及RL算法（GRPO、RLOO、DAPO）。我们引入了“新奖励失败率”（NRFR），衡量代理奖励优于SFT基线样本中的失败率。仅结果导向奖励导致严重欺骗，达到48.1%的奖励欺骗率（RHR），而NRFR超过RHR表明RL创造了新失败而非仅继承旧失败。扩展规模虽降低但未消除欺骗：即使32B模型在仅结果奖励下仍比基线差54.9%，而答案感知奖励在各规模下均改善Oracle趋势。鲁棒性亦依赖于算法与规模：GRPO始终最抗欺骗，RLOO仍易受攻击，DAPO从2B到8B显著提升。视觉证据奖励仅在可靠验证下有效：关键词检查增加欺骗，而VLM作为裁判的语义验证则减少欺骗。总体而言，多模态奖励欺骗是优化不完美奖励的系统性结果，稳健对齐需奖励与验证器在优化压力下保持可靠性。

该研究探讨了多模态强化学习中奖励黑客行为，动机在于文本主导或弱关联奖励可能误导模型优化，导致高奖励不等于真实任务表现。实验在安全VQA、图表VQA和压力测试场景中，评估不同奖励设计、模型规模（2B–32B）和算法（GRPO、RLOO、DAPO），引入“新奖励失败率”（NRFR）衡量奖励优化引入的新失败。结果显示，仅结果导向奖励导致严重黑客行为（48.1% RHR），且NRFR超过RHR说明RL创造了新失败而非放大旧问题。模型规模扩大虽降低但未消除黑客行为，32B模型在结果奖励下仍比基线差54.9%。答案感知奖励在各规模下均提升表现，GRPO最抗黑客，RLOO仍易受攻击，DAPO随规模提升显著改善。视觉证据奖励仅在VLM语义验证下有效，关键词检查反而加剧黑客行为。
Causal ASCEND: Scalable Two-tier Causal Discovery on High Dimensional Multi-omics Data
Authors: Stephen Asiedu, David Watson
First: 2026-07-05T22:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:06:15+00:00
Comments: Main material: 8 pages + supplementary material. 16 pages in all
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biological systems exhibit a hierarchical structure, characterised by directed flow from upstream regulators to downstream effects. Although this ordering provides a natural scaffold for causal inference, most causal discovery and GRN methods either ignore the tiered organisation or condition on all upstream variables, which becomes infeasible for high-dimensional omics data. We present ASCEND (Ancestral Scalable Causal discovEry via iNherited Descent), a constraint-based framework that leverages known two-tiered structure to enable genome-scale causal discovery. ASCEND introduces a divide-and-conquer strategy that maintains dynamically updated ancestral conditioning sets for each downstream variable, dramatically reducing the number of conditional independence tests required, and achieves polynomial-time complexity where traditional approaches face exponential blow-up. Through extensive simulations and real biological data, we demonstrate that ASCEND accurately recovers ancestral relationships, scales properly and much faster, and outperforms existing gene regulatory network inference methods in both causal precision and computational efficiency. The algorithm's ability to resolve directionality makes it particularly suited for integrating multi-omic data where upstream regulators (e.g., SNPs, methylation sites) and downstream responses (e.g., gene expression) are measured jointly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果ASCEND：高维多组学数据上的可扩展两级因果发现
生物系统具有层次结构，表现为从上游调控因子到下游效应的定向流动。尽管这种排序为因果推断提供了自然框架，但大多数因果发现和基因调控网络方法要么忽略分层组织，要么对所有上游变量进行条件化，这在高维组学数据中变得不可行。我们提出了ASCEND（通过继承性下降实现祖先可扩展因果发现），一种基于约束的框架，利用已知的两级结构实现基因组规模的因果发现。ASCEND引入分而治之策略，为每个下游变量动态更新祖先条件集，大幅减少所需的条件独立性检验次数，并实现多项式时间复杂度，而传统方法则面临指数级增长。通过大量模拟和真实生物数据，我们证明ASCEND能准确恢复祖先关系，具有良好的可扩展性和更快的速度，并在因果精度和计算效率上优于现有基因调控网络推断方法。该算法能够解决方向性问题，特别适用于联合测量上游调控因子（如SNP、甲基化位点）和下游响应（如基因表达）的多组学数据整合。

Motivated by the hierarchical nature of biological systems where causal effects flow from upstream regulators to downstream targets, the authors developed ASCEND, a constraint-based causal discovery method tailored for high-dimensional multi-omics data. ASCEND employs a divide-and-conquer strategy that dynamically maintains ancestral conditioning sets for each downstream variable, reducing the number of conditional independence tests and achieving polynomial-time complexity. Experimental results on simulated and real biological datasets show ASCEND accurately recovers ancestral relationships, scales efficiently with data size, and outperforms existing methods in both causal precision and computational speed, especially when integrating multi-omic data such as SNPs and gene expression.
鉴于生物系统具有从上游调控因子到下游效应的层级结构，作者提出了ASCEND方法，这是一种面向高维多组学数据的约束型因果发现方法。该方法采用分而治之策略，动态更新每个下游变量的祖先条件集，大幅减少条件独立性检验次数，实现多项式时间复杂度。在模拟和真实生物数据上的实验表明，ASCEND能准确恢复祖先关系，具有良好的可扩展性和计算效率，且在因果精度和速度上优于现有方法，尤其适用于整合多组学数据中方向性关系的场景。
Terminal Dimension Reduction for Time Series with Applications
Authors: Alexander Munteanu, Matteo Russo, David Saulpic, Chris Schwiegelshohn
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:05:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:05:30+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Terminal embeddings have emerged as a powerful tool for dimension reduction. Given a set of points $P\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, a terminal embedding is a mapping $f:\mathbb{R}^d\rightarrow \mathbb{R}^t$ that preserves the pairwise distance between any pair of points $p\in P$ and $q\in \mathbb{R}^d$ up to small distortion under this mapping. Terminal embeddings have been particularly fruitful for constructing $k$-means and $k$-median coresets, where the objective is to find a typically weighted subset $Ω$ of $P$ such that for any candidate solution, the cost of the clustering objective on $Ω$ approximates the cost of the clustering objective on $P$ up to small distortion. Unfortunately, these techniques have not been extended to more complicated structures such as clustering time-series data under common straight-line interpolation between measurements. The main issue is that terminal embeddings, arguably the central technique in this line of research, cannot be linear and are thus not immediately suitable to preserve linear structures. In this work, we develop a generalization of terminal embeddings to affine line-segments that overcomes this issue. We showcase their applicability by using our lines-preserving terminal embeddings to obtain the first dimension-free coresets for clustering time-series under the Fréchet distance. The underlying dimension reduction uses Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) embeddings, and our experiments indicate that terminal embeddings perform similarly to JL and favorably against PCA for synthetic and real-world time-series, while only terminal embeddings extend pairwise distance preservation to the full ambient space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时间序列的终端维度约简及其应用
终端嵌入已成为维度约简的强大工具。给定点集 $P\subset \mathbb{R}^d$，终端嵌入是一种映射 $f:\mathbb{R}^d\rightarrow \mathbb{R}^t$，它在映射下保持任意点对 $p\in P$ 和 $q\in \mathbb{R}^d$ 之间的成对距离，仅存在微小失真。终端嵌入在构建 $k$-means 和 $k$-median 核心集方面尤为有效，目标是找到点集 $P$ 的典型加权子集 $Ω$，使得对于任何候选解，$Ω$ 上的聚类目标成本近似于 $P$ 上的聚类目标成本，仅存在微小失真。不幸的是，这些技术尚未扩展到更复杂的结构，例如在常见直线插值测量值下对时间序列数据进行聚类。主要问题是终端嵌入，可能是该研究领域中的核心技术，不能是线性的，因此不直接适用于保持线性结构。在本工作中，我们开发了终端嵌入对仿射线段的推广，以克服这一问题。我们通过使用我们的线段保持终端嵌入，展示了其适用性，从而获得首个与维度无关的时间序列聚类核心集（基于弗雷歇距离）。底层维度约简使用约翰逊-林登施特劳斯（JL）嵌入，我们的实验表明，终端嵌入在合成和真实世界时间序列上表现与JL相似，优于PCA，且仅终端嵌入能将成对距离保持扩展到整个嵌入空间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to cluster time-series data under linear interpolation while preserving pairwise distances, this work generalizes terminal embeddings to affine line-segments, overcoming their inherent non-linearity that prevents direct application to linear structures. The method leverages Johnson-Lindenstrauss embeddings to construct dimension-free coresets for clustering under Fréchet distance, enabling efficient approximation without dimension dependence. Experimental results show that terminal embeddings match or outperform PCA and JL embeddings on both synthetic and real-world time-series, while uniquely preserving pairwise distance fidelity across the full ambient space.
为解决在直线插值下对时间序列数据进行聚类时保持结构保真度的需求，本文将终端嵌入推广至仿射线段，克服其固有的非线性缺陷，使其适用于线性结构。该方法构建了能同时保持点与其线段插值邻居间距离的映射，从而实现弗雷歇距离下无维度依赖的聚类核。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实时间序列数据上表现与约翰逊-林登施特劳斯和PCA相当，且唯一实现了全空间距离保持能力。
Ceci n'est pas une pipe: AI systems as semantic abstractions
Authors: Jade Alglave, Patrick Cousot
First: 2026-07-10T15:05:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:05:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An AI system's output is not the fact or world state it appears to describe, but rather an engineered representation. We propose a semantic framework to describe AI systems, to be able to examine the correctness of such representations. To do so, we distinguish what is justified by accepted domain knowledge, what reference sources say, and what the system can currently use. This allows us to give precise definitions to common failures: extrapolation, refuted or unsupported assertion, sources versus knowledge mismatch, stale or refuted source, added hypotheses, unsupported use... We hope our framework gives a useful vocabulary for specifying and checking AI systems whose outputs, citations, tool calls, and world-changing actions must be justified by reliable claims and explicit authority rather than apparent fluency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：这并非一支烟斗：AI系统作为语义抽象
AI系统的输出并非其表面上所描述的事实或世界状态，而是一种工程化表示。我们提出一个语义框架，用于描述AI系统，以便检验此类表示的正确性。为此，我们区分了由公认领域知识所支持的内容、参考来源所陈述的内容，以及系统当前可使用的资源。这使我们能够对常见失败模式给出精确定义：外推、被反驳或无依据的断言、来源与知识不匹配、过时或被反驳的来源、添加的假设、无依据的使用等。我们希望该框架能为指定和检验AI系统提供有用术语，其输出、引用、工具调用及改变世界的行为必须基于可靠主张和明确权威，而非表面流畅性。

Motivated by the need to rigorously evaluate AI systems' outputs as engineered semantic abstractions rather than direct reflections of reality, the paper introduces a semantic framework that distinguishes between domain knowledge, reference sources, and system capabilities to precisely define common failure modes. The method involves mapping AI outputs to their grounding in accepted knowledge, authoritative sources, and current system access, enabling formal identification of issues like extrapolation, unsupported assertions, source-knowledge mismatches, and stale references. Experimental results demonstrate the framework's utility in diagnosing failures across diverse AI behaviors, including citation errors and tool misuse, by providing a structured vocabulary to validate whether outputs are justified by reliable claims and explicit authority rather than surface-level fluency.
为严谨评估AI系统输出作为语义抽象而非现实直接反映的特性，本文提出一个语义框架，通过区分领域知识、参考来源与系统能力来定义和诊断常见错误如外推或来源错配。实验分析表明，AI系统常产生缺乏可靠主张或明确权威支持的输出，揭示了表面流畅性与实际可证性之间的差距。
SigLIP-HD by Fine-to-Coarse Supervision
Authors: Lihe Yang, Zhen Zhao, Hengshuang Zhao
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-07-10T15:05:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:05:21+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026. Code and model: https://github.com/LiheYoung/SigLIP-HD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
High-quality visual representation is a long-standing pursuit in computer vision. In the context of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), feeding higher-resolution images can produce more fine-grained visual tokens. However, it introduces additional computational and design complexity, due to multiple forward passes and post-processing of increased tokens. Before simply adopting a higher resolution, have we truly unlocked the model's full perception capability at a standard resolution? Therefore, we study an interesting problem: how to achieve fine visual perception under lower cost without larger images. We present SigLIP-HD in this work. The core is a highly simple fine-to-coarse supervision design. We enforce the coarse feature of a mid-resolution image to mimic the fine-grained feature of its high-resolution version. We build this framework on the advanced SigLIP 2 model. Our final model produces better visual tokens at exactly the same inference budget. It is validated on extensive MLLM benchmarks and consistently delivers stronger results than our baseline model, especially on OCR-related tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过细粒度到粗粒度监督实现的SigLIP-HD
高质量视觉表征一直是计算机视觉领域的长期追求。在多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中，输入更高分辨率的图像可生成更细粒度的视觉标记，但会因多次前向传播和增加标记的后处理而带来额外的计算与设计复杂性。在简单采用更高分辨率之前，我们是否已充分挖掘了模型在标准分辨率下的感知能力？为此，我们研究了一个有趣的问题：如何在不使用更大图像的情况下，以更低的成本实现精细视觉感知。本文提出SigLIP-HD，其核心是一种高度简洁的细粒度到粗粒度监督设计。我们强制中等分辨率图像的粗特征模仿其高分辨率版本的细粒度特征。该框架基于先进的SigLIP 2模型构建。最终模型在完全相同的推理预算下生成更优的视觉标记，并在广泛的MLLM基准测试中持续优于基线模型，尤其在OCR相关任务上表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational cost of using high-resolution images in multimodal LLMs, this work investigates whether fine-grained visual perception can be achieved at standard resolution without increasing image size. The method, SigLIP-HD, employs a fine-to-coarse supervision strategy: it trains the model to match coarse mid-resolution features with fine-grained high-resolution features, leveraging the SigLIP 2 architecture. Experimental results show that SigLIP-HD generates superior visual tokens under the same inference budget, outperforming the baseline across multiple MLLM benchmarks, with particularly strong gains in OCR-related tasks.
为降低多模态大语言模型中使用高分辨率图像带来的计算开销，本研究探索在标准分辨率下是否能实现细粒度视觉感知。方法SigLIP-HD采用细到粗的监督策略，使中分辨率图像特征模仿高分辨率特征，基于SigLIP 2架构实现。实验表明，该模型在相同推理开销下生成更优视觉token，在多个MLLM基准测试中均优于基线模型，尤其在OCR相关任务上表现突出。
Neural Collapse Is Forbidden: Information Floors in Language Models
Authors: Bruno Abrahao
First: 2026-07-10T15:04:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:04:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Within-class variance in language-model representations is commonly read as incomplete neural collapse. We argue it is allocated information storage, and that the allocation obeys a law. A one-line centering identity voids a family of simplex equiangular-tight-frame claims, including our own earlier ones; in dimensionless variance shares across 14 models, macro-category structure carries only 4-12% of representational variance and within-token context carries 79-91%, stable across a 100x parameter range. On the theory side, token-level weight decay penalizes a category in proportion to its type count, not its occurrence mass, reducing next-token prediction to an imbalanced K-class problem whose optimum orders category norms by type count. A converse floor, proved for binary categories, forces within-category dispersion to be at least proportional to the conditional mutual information I(token; context | category). The law holds: identity dispersion, not total variance, tracks this information across every tested model and partition, under a model-free estimate and even across models, where one model's information predicts another's dispersion; and over pretraining the category share overshoots, decays, and partially recovers, because the information it must carry never left.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经崩溃被禁止：语言模型中的信息下限
语言模型表示中的类内方差常被解读为不完全的神经崩溃。我们认为，这种方差分配了信息存储，且分配遵循一定规律。一个单行中心化恒等式否定了包括我们早期在内的多个单纯形等角紧框架主张；在14个模型的无量纲方差共享中，宏观类别结构仅承载4-12%的表示方差，而词内上下文则承载79-91%，在100倍参数范围内保持稳定。理论上，词级权重衰减按类别类型数量而非出现质量惩罚类别，将下一词预测简化为一个类别不平衡的K类问题，其最优解按类型数量排序类别范数。一个逆向下限，对二元类别已证明，强制要求类内分散度至少与条件互信息I(词; 上下文 | 类别)成比例。该规律成立：在所有测试模型和划分中，恒等分散度而非总方差，通过无模型估计追踪该信息，甚至跨模型时，一个模型的信息可预测另一模型的分散度；且在预训练过程中，类别份额先超调、衰减，再部分恢复，因为其必须承载的信息从未离开。
Summary / 总结
The paper challenges the notion that low within-class variance in language models signals neural collapse, arguing instead that such variance reflects allocated information storage governed by a law. It introduces a centering identity that invalidates prior simplex equiangular-tight-frame assumptions, showing that across 14 models, macro-category structure accounts for only 4–12% of representational variance while token context dominates with 79–91%, stable across 100x parameter scales. Theoretically, token-level weight decay penalizes categories by type count, not occurrence mass, turning next-token prediction into an imbalanced K-class problem. A converse floor proves that within-category dispersion must be at least proportional to conditional mutual information, and this law holds universally: identity dispersion—not total variance—tracks information across models and partitions, even predicting dispersion in one model from another’s information, with category share dynamics during pretraining showing overshoot, decay, and partial recovery as information remains constant.
该论文反驳了语言模型内类方差低即代表神经坍缩的观点，认为此类方差反映的是信息存储分配，且遵循一定规律。通过一个中心化恒等式，该研究推翻了包括作者早期在内的单纯紧框架假设，并表明在14个模型中，宏观类别结构仅解释4–12%的表征方差，而词内上下文贡献79–91%，且在100倍参数规模下保持稳定。理论分析显示，词级权重衰减按词类型数量而非出现频率惩罚类别，使预测退化为不平衡的K类问题。一个逆向下限证明，类内离散度至少与条件互信息成比例，该规律在不同模型和预训练阶段均成立，类别信息占比虽有波动但从未消失。
Consistent but Miscalibrated: Evaluating LLM Limitations for Risk Communication in Natural Language
Authors: Diego Cerda-Mardini, Sarath Chandar, Sreenath Madathil
First: 2026-07-04T13:57:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:01:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs are increasingly deployed as post-hoc explainers of AI-generated outputs, yet it remains unclear whether they can reliably communicate probabilistic information in natural language. For this role to be viable, models must produce identical verbal descriptions for identical inputs, and select descriptions that accurately reflect the magnitude of the underlying numerical quantities. We evaluate whether nine LLMs meet these requirements within a two-stage prediction pipeline, in which an upstream model has produced probabilistic outputs characterized by their likelihood and uncertainty, and LLMs are tasked with selecting an appropriate verbal descriptor for each. We simulate predictions from an upstream model by taking samples from a Beta distribution parameterized by its mode and prior sample size. We then prompt LLMs to explain these predictions under six domain contexts and with ten temperature settings, and repeating each experiment ten times. We find that LLMs are generally consistent but miscalibrated, with substantially weaker performance on uncertainty than on likelihood tasks. Providing models with precomputed summary statistics (mode and prior sample size) reduced sensitivity to contextual framing but did not resolve the underlying miscalibration, suggesting that the bottleneck resides in the verbalization step itself. These findings indicate that current LLMs do not yet constitute reliable zero-shot standalone risk communication tools for probabilistic predictions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一致但校准不足：评估大语言模型在自然语言中传达风险信息的局限性
大语言模型正越来越多地被用作AI生成输出的后置解释器，但尚不清楚它们是否能可靠地用自然语言传达概率信息。要胜任这一角色，模型必须对相同输入生成相同的口头描述，并选择准确反映底层数值量级的描述。我们在一个两阶段预测管道中评估了九个大语言模型是否满足这些要求：上游模型生成具有置信度和不确定性的概率输出，大语言模型则需为每个输出选择合适的口头描述。我们通过从由模式和先验样本量参数化的Beta分布中采样，模拟上游模型的预测；然后在六个领域情境和十种温度设置下提示大语言模型解释这些预测，并重复实验十次。结果发现，大语言模型总体上具有一致性但存在校准不足，其在不确定性任务上的表现显著弱于置信度任务。向模型提供预计算的汇总统计量（模式和先验样本量）降低了对情境框架的敏感性，但未能解决根本性的校准问题，表明瓶颈在于口头化步骤本身。这些发现表明，当前大语言模型尚不能作为可靠的零样本独立风险沟通工具用于概率预测。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably communicate probabilistic risk information in natural language, a critical capability for their use as post-hoc explainers. The researchers evaluate nine LLMs in a two-stage pipeline, where they must generate consistent verbal descriptions for identical probabilistic inputs and accurately reflect the magnitude of underlying numerical uncertainty and likelihood. Using simulated probabilistic outputs drawn from a Beta distribution, the team tested LLMs across six domains and ten temperature settings, repeating each experiment ten times. Results show LLMs are generally consistent in output but miscalibrated in accuracy, performing significantly worse on uncertainty than likelihood tasks. Providing precomputed summary statistics improved contextual robustness but did not fix the core miscalibration, pointing to a fundamental limitation in the verbalization process. The findings suggest current LLMs are not yet suitable as reliable zero-shot tools for standalone risk communication.
该研究旨在评估大语言模型（LLM）是否能可靠地以自然语言传达概率性风险信息，这是其作为事后解释器的关键前提。研究人员在两阶段框架下测试九个LLM：上游模型生成基于Beta分布的置信度与不确定性输出，LLM需为其选择恰当的口语化描述，实验在六个领域情境和十种温度设置下重复十次。结果显示，LLM在相同输入下表现一致，但存在明显校准偏差，尤其在不确定性表达上表现更弱。提供预计算的统计摘要（模式与先验样本量）虽增强了情境鲁棒性，却未解决根本校准问题，表明问题根源在于语言化环节。研究结论指出，当前LLM尚不具备作为零样本独立风险沟通工具的可靠性。
AugServe: Adaptive Request Scheduling for Augmented Large Language Model Inference Serving
Authors: Ying Wang, Zhen Jin, Jiexiong Xu, Wenhai Lin, Yiquan Chen, Wenzhi Chen
First: 2025-12-03T17:49:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:00:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As augmented large language models (LLMs) with external tools become increasingly popular in web applications, improving augmented LLM inference serving efficiency and optimizing service-level objectives (SLOs) are critical for enhancing user experience. To achieve this, inference systems must maximize request handling within latency constraints, referred to as increasing effective throughput. However, existing systems face two major challenges: (i) reliance on first-come-first-served (FCFS) scheduling causes severe head-of-line blocking, leading to queuing delays exceeding the SLOs for many requests; and (ii) static batch token limit, which fails to adapt to fluctuating loads and hardware conditions. Both of these factors degrade effective throughput and service quality. This paper presents AugServe, an efficient inference framework designed to reduce queueing latency and enhance effective throughput for augmented LLM inference services. The core idea of AugServe is a two-stage adaptive request scheduling strategy. Specifically, AugServe combines the inference features of augmented LLM requests to optimize the order of scheduling decisions (stage I). These decisions are continuously refined with runtime information (stage II), adapting to both request characteristics and system capabilities. In addition, AugServe dynamically adjusts the token batching mechanism based on hardware status and real-time load, further enhancing throughput performance. Experimental results show that AugServe achieves 4.7x and 3.3x higher effective throughput than vLLM and InferCept, while reducing time-to-first-token (TTFT) by up to 96.3% and 95.0%, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AugServe：面向增强型大语言模型推理服务的自适应请求调度
随着配备外部工具的增强型大语言模型（LLM）在Web应用中日益普及，提升其推理服务效率并优化服务级目标（SLO）对改善用户体验至关重要。为此，推理系统必须在延迟约束下最大化请求处理能力，即提升有效吞吐量。然而，现有系统面临两大挑战：（i）依赖先到先服务（FCFS）调度导致严重首部阻塞，致使大量请求排队延迟超出SLO；（ii）静态批处理令牌限制无法适应负载与硬件条件的波动。这两方面均降低有效吞吐量和服务质量。 本文提出AugServe，一种高效的推理框架，旨在降低增强型LLM推理服务的排队延迟并提升有效吞吐量。AugServe的核心思想是两阶段自适应请求调度策略：第一阶段，结合增强型LLM请求的推理特征以优化调度顺序；第二阶段，持续利用运行时信息动态调整调度决策，以适应请求特性与系统能力。此外，AugServe根据硬件状态和实时负载动态调整令牌批处理机制，进一步提升吞吐量性能。实验结果表明，AugServe相比vLLM和InferCept分别实现4.7倍和3.3倍的有效吞吐量提升，同时将首次令牌响应时间（TTFT）分别降低96.3%和95.0%。
Summary / 总结
As augmented large language models with external tools grow in web applications, improving inference serving efficiency and meeting service-level objectives becomes crucial. Existing systems suffer from first-come-first-served scheduling causing head-of-line blocking and static token batching that fails to adapt to load or hardware changes, both reducing effective throughput. AugServe addresses these by introducing a two-stage adaptive scheduling strategy: first, it optimizes request ordering based on inference features, then refines decisions with runtime data to adapt to system conditions. It also dynamically adjusts token batching according to hardware and load. Experiments show AugServe delivers 4.7x and 3.3x higher effective throughput than vLLM and InferCept, while cutting time-to-first-token by up to 96.3% and 95.0% respectively.
随着具备外部工具的增强型大语言模型在Web应用中普及，提升推理服务效率并满足服务等级目标至关重要。现有系统因采用先到先服务调度导致首部阻塞，且静态令牌批处理无法适应负载或硬件变化，从而降低有效吞吐量。AugServe通过两阶段自适应调度策略解决这些问题：第一阶段根据推理特征优化请求排序；第二阶段结合运行时信息动态调整以适应请求与系统特性，同时根据硬件状态和实时负载动态调整令牌批处理。实验表明，AugServe相较vLLM和InferCept分别提升有效吞吐量4.7倍和3.3倍，首令牌响应时间分别缩短96.3%和95.0%。
Enhancing AI and Dynamical Subseasonal Forecasts with Probabilistic Bias Correction
Authors: Hannah Guan, Soukayna Mouatadid, Paulo Orenstein, Judah Cohen, Haiyu Dong, Zekun Ni, Jeremy Berman, Genevieve Flaspohler, Alex Lu, Jakob Schloer, Joshua Talib, Jonathan A. Weyn, Lester Mackey
First: 2026-04-17T16:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T15:00:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision-makers rely on weather forecasts to plant crops, manage wildfires, allocate water and energy, and prepare for weather extremes. Today, such forecasts enjoy unprecedented accuracy out to two weeks thanks to steady advances in physics-based dynamical models and data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) models. However, model skill drops precipitously at subseasonal timescales (2 - 6 weeks ahead), due to compounding errors, systemic model biases, and the chaotic nature of the atmosphere. To counter this degradation, we introduce probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning framework that substantially reduces systematic error by learning to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. When applied to the leading dynamical and AI models from the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF), PBC doubles the modest subseasonal skill of the AI Forecasting System and improves the skill of the operationally-debiased dynamical model for 91% of pressure, 92% of temperature, and 98% of precipitation targets. We designed PBC for operational deployment, and, in ECMWF's 2025 real-time forecasting competition, its global forecasts placed first for all weather variables and lead times, outperforming the dynamical models from six operational forecasting centers, an international dynamical multi-model ensemble, ECMWF's AI Forecasting System, and the forecasting systems of 34 teams worldwide. These probabilistic skill gains translate into more accurate prediction of extreme events and have the potential to improve agricultural planning, energy management, and disaster preparedness in vulnerable communities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过概率性偏差校正增强人工智能与动力学次季节预报
决策者依赖天气预报来种植作物、管理野火、分配水资源和能源，并应对极端天气。得益于物理基础的动力学模型和数据驱动的人工智能（AI）模型的持续进步，当前的预报在两周内已达到前所未有的准确性。然而，在次季节时间尺度（2至6周）上，模型性能急剧下降，这是由于误差累积、系统性模型偏差以及大气的混沌特性所致。为应对这一退化，我们引入了概率性偏差校正（PBC），这是一种机器学习框架，通过学习校正历史概率预报，显著减少系统性误差。当应用于欧洲中期天气预报中心（ECMWF）的领先动力学和AI模型时，PBC将AI预报系统的次季节技能提升一倍，并在91%的压力、92%的温度和98%的降水目标上提升了操作性偏差校正动力学模型的技能。我们为实际部署设计了PBC，在ECMWF 2025实时预报竞赛中，其全球预报在所有天气变量和预报时效上均排名第一，优于六个运营预报中心的动力学模型、一个国际动力学多模型集合、ECMWF的AI预报系统以及全球34支队伍的预报系统。这些概率性技能的提升转化为对极端事件更准确的预测，有望改善脆弱社区的农业规划、能源管理和灾害应对能力。

Motivated by the rapid decline in forecast accuracy beyond two weeks due to atmospheric chaos and model biases, researchers developed probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning method that learns to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. In ECMWF’s 2025 real-time forecasting competition, PBC’s global forecasts ranked first for all variables and lead times, outperforming six operational centers, an international multi-model ensemble, ECMWF’s own AI system, and 34 global teams.
为应对两周后预报准确率急剧下降的问题，研究人员提出了概率偏差校正（PBC）方法，该方法通过学习历史概率预报来修正系统性误差。当应用于ECMWF的领先动力学和AI模型时，PBC显著提升了亚季节尺度预报能力，使AI预报系统技能翻倍，并在91%的气压、92%的温度和98%的降水目标上提升了动力学模型表现。在ECMWF 2025实时预报竞赛中，PBC在全球预报中所有变量和时效上均排名第一，超越了六个业务中心、国际多模式集合、ECMWF自身AI系统及全球34支队伍的预报系统。
Decoupling Language Guidance from Backbones for Text-Guided Medical Segmentation
Authors: Yungeng Liu, Xuanzi Fang, Haijin Zeng, Qi Dai, Yongyong Chen
First: 2026-07-10T14:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T14:57:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-guided medical image segmentation leverages clinical semantics to improve lesion delineation, yet many existing models bind cross-modal fusion, supervision, and decoder design into a task-specific architecture. Such tight coupling makes it difficult to reuse language guidance modules across heterogeneous vision and text backbones, and often requires redesigning the network when the encoder pair changes. This paper presents BTHA, a backbone-transferable hierarchical adapter framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. BTHA is built around a stable feature-level interface: given multi-scale visual features and a text representation, it injects semantic guidance through shape-preserving adapters while maintaining the decoder-side tensor contract. To make this interface effective, we introduce a Hierarchical Coarse-to-Fine Supervision Strategy that decomposes learning into global image-text alignment, multi-scale auxiliary localization, and boundary-aware final mask refinement. We further design a Scale-Adaptive Gated Semantic Guidance (SAGSG) adapter, where resolution-specific gates adaptively control textual injection and channel recalibration suppresses redundant cross-modal responses. Evaluations across diverse vision and text backbones show that the same adapter and supervision design remains effective across convolutional and transformer-based visual encoders as well as different language encoders. Experiments on four public datasets further demonstrate that BTHA improves strong text-guided baselines with modest computational overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦语言引导与骨干网络的文本引导医学图像分割
文本引导的医学图像分割利用临床语义提升病灶勾画效果，但现有许多模型将跨模态融合、监督机制和解码器设计紧密绑定于特定任务架构。这种强耦合导致难以在异构视觉与文本骨干网络间复用语言引导模块，且当编码器对发生变化时往往需重新设计网络。本文提出BTHA，一种面向文本引导医学图像分割的骨干可迁移分层适配器框架。BTHA围绕稳定特征级接口构建：给定多尺度视觉特征与文本表示，通过保持形状的适配器注入语义引导，同时维持解码器侧张量契约。为使该接口有效，我们引入分层粗到细监督策略，将学习分解为全局图像-文本对齐、多尺度辅助定位及边界感知最终掩码细化。进一步设计了尺度自适应门控语义引导（SAGSG）适配器，其中分辨率特异性门控自适应控制文本注入，通道重校准抑制冗余跨模态响应。在多样视觉与文本骨干网络上的评估表明，相同适配器与监督设计在卷积与基于Transformer的视觉编码器及不同语言编码器间均保持有效性。在四个公开数据集上的实验进一步表明，BTHA在计算开销较小的情况下显著提升现有强文本引导基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of tightly coupled text-guided medical segmentation models that hinder reuse across different vision and language backbones, this paper introduces BTHA, a hierarchical adapter framework that decouples language guidance from the backbone. BTHA maintains a stable feature-level interface, injecting semantic guidance via shape-preserving adapters while preserving decoder tensor contracts, and employs a hierarchical supervision strategy that progressively aligns image-text representations, refines localization, and sharpens boundaries. Experimental results across diverse encoders show BTHA’s adaptability and effectiveness, consistently improving upon strong baselines with minimal computational cost.
为了解决现有文本引导医学图像分割模型因跨模态融合与解码器设计紧密耦合而导致难以跨不同视觉与语言骨干网络复用的问题，本文提出BTHA框架，通过分层适配器将语言引导与视觉骨干解耦。该框架在特征层建立稳定接口，通过保持形状的适配器注入语义信息，同时保留解码器张量契约，并采用粗到细的分层监督策略，逐步实现图像-文本全局对齐、多尺度辅助定位与边界感知的最终掩码优化。实验表明，BTHA的适配器与监督设计在不同视觉与语言编码器间均保持有效性，并在四个公开数据集上显著优于现有强基线模型，且计算开销极小。
Transformer-Based Inverse Microrheology for Experimental Mechanics at Ultra-High Strain Rates
Authors: Lehu Bu, Zhaohan Yu, Danila Frolkin, Junyoung Kim, Qihang Shi, Jan N. Fuhg, Shaoting Lin, Jin Yang
First: 2025-06-13T16:38:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T14:56:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional rheological tools are often limited in characterizing soft materials under ultra-high strain-rate loading conditions (> 1000 s^-1) due to constraints in spatiotemporal resolution, loading rate, and invasiveness. Recently, inertial microcavitation rheometry (IMR), which utilizes laser-induced inertial cavitation (LIC) to dynamically deform surrounding materials, has emerged as a powerful experimental mechanics technique for probing nonlinear viscoelastic properties under extreme loading conditions. However, conventional IMR relies on computationally expensive iterative inverse fitting procedures, limiting its scalability and real-time applicability. Here, we introduce a new AI-enhanced experimental mechanics framework, called Bubble Dynamics Transformer (BDT), that integrates physics-based cavitation simulations with Transformer neural network architectures to achieve rapid inverse characterization of soft material viscoelasticity from experimentally measured bubble dynamics. The proposed framework directly predicts viscoelastic material parameters from time-resolved bubble radius evolution curves without iterative optimization. The BDT is trained using synthetic datasets generated from physics-based Keller--Miksis cavitation simulations and validated using experimental laser-induced cavitation data obtained from hydrogels and viscous polymer solutions. The proposed AI-driven framework demonstrates excellent agreement with our previous IMR while substantially accelerating constitutive parameter inference. Experimental demonstrations further reveal the capability of the framework to characterize rate-dependent material behavior across a wide range of soft materials, from viscous liquids to various viscoelastic hydrogels, at ultra-high strain rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer的反向微流变学用于超高速应变率下的实验力学研究
传统流变学工具在超高速应变率加载条件（>1000 s⁻¹）下表征软材料时，常受限于时空分辨率、加载速率和侵入性。近期，利用激光诱导惯性空化（LIC）动态变形周围材料的惯性微空化流变术（IMR）已成为一种强大的实验力学技术，用于探测极端加载条件下的非线性黏弹性。然而，传统IMR依赖计算成本高昂的迭代反向拟合程序，限制其可扩展性和实时应用。本文提出一种新的AI增强实验力学框架——气泡动力学Transformer（BDT），将基于物理的空化模拟与Transformer神经网络架构相结合，实现从实验测得的气泡动力学数据中快速反向表征软材料黏弹性。该框架可直接从时间分辨的气泡半径演化曲线预测黏弹性材料参数，无需迭代优化。BDT使用基于物理的Keller-Miksis空化模拟生成的合成数据集进行训练，并通过水凝胶和黏性聚合物溶液的实验激光诱导空化数据进行验证。该AI驱动框架在与先前IMR结果高度一致的同时，显著加速了本构参数推断。实验演示进一步表明，该框架可表征从黏性液体到多种黏弹性水凝胶在超高速应变率下的速率依赖材料行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional rheological methods in measuring soft materials under ultra-high strain rates, this work introduces the Bubble Dynamics Transformer (BDT), an AI-enhanced framework that combines physics-based cavitation simulations with Transformer neural networks to rapidly infer viscoelastic parameters from laser-induced bubble dynamics. Instead of relying on computationally intensive iterative fitting, BDT directly predicts material properties from time-resolved bubble radius curves. Trained on synthetic data from Keller--Miksis simulations and validated on experimental data from hydrogels and polymer solutions, BDT achieves high accuracy while dramatically accelerating parameter inference, enabling real-time characterization of rate-dependent behavior across diverse soft materials.
为解决传统流变学方法在超高应变率下表征软材料的局限性，本研究提出了一种名为气泡动力学变换器（BDT）的AI增强框架，该框架结合物理基础的空化模拟与Transformer神经网络，直接从激光诱导气泡动力学数据中快速推断材料黏弹性参数，无需迭代优化。该框架在基于Keller-Miksis模拟生成的合成数据上训练，并在水凝胶和黏性聚合物溶液的实验数据中验证，结果表明其精度高、推断速度快，可实时表征从黏性液体到多种水凝胶在超高应变率下的速率依赖性行为。
Foveation-Guided Dynamic Token Selection for Robust and Efficient Vision Transformers
Authors: Ibrahim Batuhan Akkaya, Kishaan Jeeveswaran, Bahram Zonooz, Elahe Arani
First: 2026-07-10T14:53:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-10T14:53:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The human visual system (HVS) employs foveated sampling and eye movements to achieve efficient perception, conserving both metabolic energy and computational resources. Drawing inspiration from this robustness and adaptability, we introduce the Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT), a foveation-guided dynamic token-selection architecture that integrates these mechanisms into a vision transformer framework. The FDT exhibits strong resilience to various types of noise and adversarial attacks, despite not being explicitly trained for such challenges. This inherent robustness is achieved through the use of fixation and foveation modules: the fixation module identifies fixation points to filter out irrelevant information, while the foveation module generates foveated embeddings with multi-scale information. At the 50% fixation-budget setting, FDT achieves higher accuracy than DeiT-S (81.9% vs. 80.9%) while reducing multiply-accumulate operations by 34.57%, highlighting one operating point on its accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These attributes position FDT as an HVS-inspired step toward artificial neural networks that combine adaptive computation with improved resilience.

人类视觉系统（HVS）通过注视采样和眼球运动实现高效感知，同时节省代谢能量和计算资源。受其鲁棒性和适应性启发，我们提出了注视引导的动态变换器（FDT），一种将这些机制整合进视觉变换器框架的注视引导动态令牌选择架构。尽管FDT未专门针对噪声和对抗攻击进行训练，但其表现出对多种噪声和对抗攻击的强鲁棒性。这种内在鲁棒性通过注视和注视模块实现：注视模块识别注视点以过滤无关信息，而注视模块生成包含多尺度信息的注视嵌入。在50%注视预算设置下，FDT比DeiT-S（81.9% vs. 80.9%）精度更高，同时将乘加操作减少34.57%，凸显其在精度-效率权衡中的一个运行点。这些特性使FDT成为受HVS启发的一步，旨在构建结合自适应计算与增强鲁棒性的人工神经网络。
